# RH proof-route ledger

**State:** RH open. 30 live routes; 18 standalone candidates plus PM's PW-B candidate sub-branch. BVI supports PM/MC/CTF; not counted as a route or candidate. No route has a source-derived RH-closing sign or bound.

**Lean checkpoint:** 55862c0a52ee14bf2bc0acdbdcdc1c1e8b1ca707; uncommitted/non-umbrella source marked [S]. [L] = Lean declaration reachable through committed source/umbrella imports (no fresh build); [S] = uncommitted or non-umbrella Lean source (no fresh compiler audit); [P] = paper claim. R:X = formal/GaloisForLFunctions/RHTower/X.lean; H:X = formal/GaloisForLFunctions/HTGeometry/X.lean; B:X = formal/GaloisForLFunctions/BiScaleUncertainty/X.lean; V:X = formal/GaloisForLFunctions/BezoutValuationIncidence/X.lean; T:X = formal/GaloisForLFunctions/TransHorizonIncidenceMechanics/X.lean; A:X = formal/GaloisForLFunctions/arithmetic-positivity-theory/X.lean; C:X = formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticContinuationAndCriticalEnergy/X.lean; I:X = formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticIncidencePositivity/X.lean; Q:X = formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ScaleSignedIncidenceCriticality/X.lean; S:X = formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/X.lean; E = source modules, not extra premises.

**Classification:** Live = fixed source-defined observable, proved RH-sufficient receiver, explicit open arithmetic estimate. Candidate = distinct proposed proof mechanism with incomplete source-to-RH formulation or transfer. Supporting framework = tools, coordinates and estimates feeding existing routes; not a route candidate. An unproved closing estimate alone does not demote a live route. Killed = scoped mechanism refuted/circular/nonselective; not necessarily a retired route.

**Frameworks:** ArithmeticCompensationTheory (ACCC) → AIP/MC/RW/ACT/BVI; SignedArithmeticEnergyIncidence (SAEI) → AIP/MC/PM/CTF; SignChangingScrewGauge (SCG) → AIP/ACT. ReverseTwistedArcPoleCompression repackages RW/OWU pole receivers; ReflectiveDirichletPolynomialS2 combines reflection identities, existing S₂ bounds and unbalanced dispersion. None is a separate proof route.

## Live routes

<a id="wl"></a>
### WL — Weil lag function

- **Path:** Exact prime data → one-sided bound on Φ(log n), prime lags, or integrated Chebyshev deficit → RH.
- **Established [S], compensated prime residual:** For compact `C¹` F supported in `[-r,r]`, r>0, the full translated prime-shift sum minus its `4cosh(s/2)` polar integral is bounded by `K exp(S/2−c√S)(2‖F′‖₁+‖F‖₁)`, `S=2r+|x|` sufficiently large, universal `c>0,K≥0`. Exact cancellation precedes estimation; exponential rate `1/2` remains, not every-positive-slack control. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCompensationTheory/ThePrimePolarResidualHasTheDeLaValleePoussinSaving.lean (`exists_abs_primeShift_sub_polar_le`).
- **Established [L], lag calculus:** Lag Abel summation gives `ΣΛ(n)h(log n)−∫(e^s+1)h(s)ds=∫(e^s+s−1−ψ(e^s))h′(s)ds` for compact `C¹` weights; second-order/band versions pair the integrated deficit with test curvature. Source rigidity and finite-aperture truncation are exact. PNT gives only `e^s+s−1−ψ(e^s)=o(e^s)`, not the critical one-sided bound. E: I:LagChebyshevAbelSummation.
- **Established [L], arithmetic transport:** Pole shifts change the defect by an explicit source-independent exponential term; two endpoint defect bounds control window mass. Dirichlet convolution transports lag prefixes exactly; Möbius inversion gives `ψ(N)=Σ_{d≤N}μ(d)log(⌊N/d⌋!)` and `|Σ_{d≤N}μ(d)/d|≤1`. Finite Euler filtering preserves autocorrelation and bounds sequential prime-lag payment by initial mass. Remaining mass equals the finite Euler comb's squared norm, strictly positive on every nonzero compact continuous test when all lags are nonzero. Exact raw charge = initial mass − comb energy + predecessor-filtered sum/difference-lag cross-charge; hence raw prime charge ≤ mass + cross-charge. Cross-charge has no supplied closing sign; unfiltered prime−Gamma−polar positivity remains open. E: I:LagChebyshevAbelSummation.
- **Established [L], local source control:** `criticalScalePrefix(s)>0` for `0<s<log 3`; propagation to `log(N+1)` follows if `ψ(N)/N<1+criticalScalePrefix(log N)`, still unpaid for all N. Prime-power-free intervals force endpoint Chebyshev-defect size ≥ half their length. Prime-only/higher-power decomposition is exact; higher powers disappear when `2r<log 4`. Unconditional defect envelope remains `O((1+s)e^s)`. Arbitrary-pole test bounds retain the calibrated defect; for `ρ>1`, its explicit lower envelope grows at rate `e^{ρs}`. E: I:LagChebyshevAbelSummation.
- **Established [L], packet scope:** Every nonzero real compact `C²` source has stationary prime-minus-polar packet values `>c` and `<−c` arbitrarily late for some `c>0`; packets cannot converge. Narrow probes with nonzero integral have arbitrarily late absolute amplitude `>√(c t log(1/t))`. Under RH, tuned packet-energy/Weil-energy ratios read critical-zero multiplicity. None is an arithmetic upper bound. E: B:PacketPersistenceLaw; B:ProbeAmplitudeLaw; B:MultiplicitySpectroscopyLaw.
- **Established [L], critical-zero amplitudes:** For each critical zero `ρ`, a compact real `C²` packet has arbitrarily late values above and below `±m_ρ|f̂(Imρ)|` up to any positive error; tuned cubic splines give explicit `15m_ρR/32` amplitudes under `R|Imρ|≥1`. Zero-side oscillation, not a source upper bound. E: B:PacketZeroAmplitudeLaw.
- **Established [L], alternate packet receiver:** Eventual *one-sided* upper or lower bound on every compact stationary prime-minus-polar packet is RH-equivalent; for a fixed packet, either bound at rate `e^(στ)` excludes every zero of depth `>σ` it sees. A visible deeper zero forces arbitrarily late positive and negative excursions beyond each multiple of `e^(στ)`. If one packet is eventually bounded above, its Weil energy is nonnegative and at most its asymptotic packet mean square; equality detects simplicity of visible critical zeros under RH. Open: arithmetic one-sided packet bound, not a zero-side oscillation law. E: B:PacketOneSidedGrowthLaw; B:PacketWeilEnergyLaw.
- **Established [L], universal probe:** Every nonzero real compact `C²` source has an exponential boost seeing every ζ zero (all but countably many boost parameters work). Explicit `U(u)=e^u(U_1^{*4})(u)` is compact `C²`, supported on `[-4,4]`, and sees every zero: RH iff its packet is eventually bounded above, equivalently a single spline-weighted prime sum obeys an eventual polar-mass-plus-`O(e^τ)` upper bound. Its packet exponent gives zero width; under RH, packet mean-square equals Weil energy iff all zeros are simple. Source upper bound/equality open. E: B:UniversalProbeLaw; B:CubicSplineUniversalProbe; B:SimpleZeroPacketCriterion.
- **Established [L], packet bands/sign:** Eventual upper packet bound forces recurrent positive and negative packets in every sufficiently late fixed-length interval. For compact real `C²` `f` and `σ≥0`, `∫_T^L P_f²=O((1+L)e^{2σL})` iff `f̂` vanishes at every zero deeper than `σ`; one universal probe's linear band is RH-equivalent. For every `K`, the universal critical-energy ceiling `E_crit(f)≤K W_crit(f)` iff every critical zero has multiplicity `≤K`; tuned spline ratios attain the ceiling. These are zero-side receivers, not arithmetic bounds. E: B:PacketSignGapLaw; B:PacketBandLaw; B:MultiplicityCeilingLaw.
- **Established [L], pairing recurrence:** Conditional on eventual upper packet boundedness, negated translated prime−polar pairing has recurrent limsup equal to the source's Weil energy; for each source under RH. This reconstructs energy, not the source bound. E: B:PairingRecurrenceLaw.
- **Established [L], translated-pairing envelope:** One-sided eventual bound on the stationary packet of an autocorrelation forces nonnegative Weil energy, eventual absolute packet bound by that energy `+ε`, and arbitrarily late negative-packet values `≥Weil energy−ε`. The latter frequent lower envelope holds unconditionally, even if packets are unbounded. This constrains a putative source bound; it does not supply one. E: B:TranslatedPairingEnvelopeLaw.
- **Established [L], Chebyshev-error transfer:** Compact `C²` packet square energy is bounded by `(1+η)‖f'−f/2‖₁²` times normalized Chebyshev-error square energy through `L+w+3`, plus `(1+1/η)10‖f‖₂²`. Under RH, `cramer_liminf_law` gives each finite-zero lower mean-square contribution; `summable_zeroEnergy_of_riemannHypothesis` and `cramer_liminf_law_zeroEnergy` give the full zero-energy lower liminf. These are RH-conditional lower bounds, not a source-derived Chebyshev upper bound or a one-sided WL sign. E: B:CramerLiminfLaw.
- **Established [L], alternate receiver:** Under RH, stationary prime-minus-polar packets obey a Parseval mean-square law. For each compact `C²` packet `f`, growth `O(e^(στ))` iff `f̂` vanishes at every zero deeper than `σ`; RH iff every such packet is eventually bounded. Open: source-derived eventual boundedness for every packet. E: B:ProbeParsevalLaw; B:PacketBoundednessCriterion.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** On `Re s>1/2`, the actual continued prime–archimedean relative trace equals `i[(1/4−s²)·lagPoleSeries(s)+s·Σ lagWeight]`, via the Xi relative resolvent. This identifies WL/PA coordinates, not a one-sided lag bound. E: R:PrimeArchWeilLagReceiverIdentity.
- **Established [L]:** Either one-sided bounded lag criterion is RH-equivalent; power-gap, prime-mesh, progression, and thin-exception sampling transfer source bounds. By Landau's theorem, a one-sided square-root bound `±∫₀ᵀ(eᵗ−ψ(eᵗ))dt ≤ B e^(T/2)` on the logarithmic Chebyshev-deficit primitive forces RH. Explicit prime-lag/zero-free-strip exponent laws, curvature and Cayley reconstructions retain affine charge. Sampling and operator encodings do not produce a bound.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Signed prime−polar packet equals a compact-source Chebyshev-error gauge plus boundary/residual terms; its energy is bounded by weighted Chebyshev-error mean-square plus source/endpoint costs and has exact Plancherel form `∫|𝓕f(ξ)|²|ν̂_N(ξ)|²dξ = ∫|𝓕f(ξ)|²(¼+4π²ξ²)S_N(ξ)dξ`. Finite-cutoff causal-response energy equals its critical-line Fourier norm for the pole-subtracted truncated `ΣΛ(n)n^(−s)`; its high-frequency tail is explicitly bounded. For band `|ξ|≤√(N+2)`, band energy ≤ full Laplace energy ≤ band energy `+O(log(N+2))`; Chebyshev-error mean-square is bounded above by twice band energy plus `O(log(N+2))`, while the reverse bound retains an endpoint square. No one-sided square-root deficit follows. E: B:HyperbolicGaugeChebyshevEnergyRepresentation; B:SignedPrimePolarHalfLineTransport; B:PacketEnergyPlancherel; B:CausalExponentialResponseAndLaplaceEnergy; B:SpectralLaplaceEnergyCriticalLine; B:HighFrequencyLaplaceEnergyMeanValue; B:BandLimitedLaplaceEnergy.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** The finite prime−polar frequency transform equals a windowed normalized Chebyshev-error transform plus an endpoint transform bounded by `(|error at cutoff|+5)/|1/2+2πiξ|`. The causal-response energy has a dyadic coarse–fine comparison: block discrepancies are weighted increments of `ψ(2^k)−2^k`, but both directions retain fine-block energies. These identities give no one-sided critical sign. E: B:DiscrepancyTransformLaw; B:DyadicRenewalChain.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Stirling/alternating-producer bounds control dyadic inputs and finite causal-response windows. Divisor-averaged Chebyshev error equals `log K!−K H_K`, with negative linear-order remainder after the `K log K` cancellation; gauge pairing equals response against `h′−h/2`, bounded by Laplace energy times derivative energy. Fine-block/source budgets remain; no exponent-zero one-sided Chebyshev bound follows. E: B:DyadicRenewalStirlingAndLogMobiusTotalEnergyProducer; B:DivisorAverageChebyshevAndDyadicBlockVarianceProducer; B:GaugeDualNormAndDivisorSampledResponse.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Window causal-response energy is bounded by weighted dyadic `ψ(2^k)−2^k` jumps plus within-block discrete Chebyshev deviations; exact block variance splits those fine deviations into within-block variance and block-mean drift. Fine terms are not paid by coarse data alone. E: B:DyadicBlockEnergyDiscreteDeviation; B:IntegerBlockVarianceBridge.
- **Established [L], conditional on RH:** Cramér's `∫_X^{2X}(ψ(x)−x)² dx ≤ C X²` for every `X≥1`, via dyadic zero sums and multiplicative-window transfer. This is RH-to-mean-square, not a source-derived prime-error estimate. E: R:CramerZeroSumMeanSquare; R:CramerChebyshevMeanSquare.
- **Established [L], receiver:** One-sided power bounds on the actual normalized `chebyshevSqrtIncrementAction` determine the zero abscissa `Θ≤1/2+max(0,c)`; the monotone convex Chebyshev potential has the analogous excess exponent law. These are source-defined Chebyshev rewrites, not unconditional critical bounds. E: R:QuasiRHDilationTailHyperbolaAndOutgoingPotentialAbscissaLaw.
- **Established [L], receiver:** Normalized Chebyshev Cesàro, square-root von Mangoldt Riesz, integer-lag linear Cesàro/Mertens actions and sparse power-mesh/exceptional-lag observations have exact zero-abscissa or strip laws; cumulative Cesàro mean-square has optimal exponent `2Θ`. Exact triadic lag–Chebyshev–Mertens recombination retains `O(√M)`/linear bridge costs. No unconditional critical one-sided or mean-square bound follows. E: R:QuasiRHNormalizedChebyshevCesaroAndMertensActionAbscissaLaw; R:QuasiRHPowerMeshSquareSampleAndObserverJetAbscissaLaw.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Window Laplace energy has exact finite cell quadrature in `ψ(n)`, plus endpoint square. At frequency zero, signed prime−polar packet mass is `Σ_{n≤N+2}Λ(n)/√n−4sinh(L/2)=½∫₀ᴸ normalizedLogError+normalizedLogError(L)−1+2e^(−L/2)`; spectral density there is four times its square. Neither identity supplies one-sided square-root Chebyshev sign. E: B:WindowEnergyArithmeticQuadrature; B:PoleRegionNetSignedMass.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Atomic prime Dirichlet-polynomial large-value sets obey a critical-band mean-square bound and a Halász-type `L¹` bound; Chebyshev and dyadic sawtooth errors inherit unconditional Vinogradov–Korobov rates, uniform in common cutoff offset. These subpower bounds do not reach the exponent-zero one-sided square-root receiver. E: B:CriticalBandLargeValuesAndHalaszSpectralBounds.
- **Established [L], receiver:** Xi zero-width `α<1/2` is equivalent to mean-square, Laplace-energy and spectral-band rates below every exponent `β>α`; subexponential band growth is RH-equivalent and then improves to a linear mean-square band. A linear band's slope bounds `Σ_ρ m_ρ²/|ρ|²`. No unconditional critical band bound follows. E: B:BandSpectrumZeroWidthLaw; B:BandSlopeZeroEnergyLaw; B:BandExtremalLineLaw.
- **Established [L], positive-slack receivers:** RH iff the universal spline packet has an eventual upper bound `Kβe^(βτ)` for every `β>0`. For `0≤α<1/2`, either one-sided deficit-primitive bound `±D(T)≤B(1+T)e^((1/2+α)T)` forces zero width `≤α`; α=0 suffices for RH. HF's prime-window ceiling `≤e^(L/2)+O(L)` lands in this deficit receiver; its source estimate remains open. E: R:CrossRouteReceiverBridges; B:DeficitPrimitiveOneSidedWidth; B:PrimeWindowCeilingRHCriterion.
- **Established [L], unconditional deficit envelope:** For every `0<a<3/5`, some `C≥0,κ>0` give `|D(T)|≤Ce^(T−κT^a)` for all sufficiently large `T`. Leading exponent remains `1`, not the RH target `1/2` plus arbitrary positive slack. E: B:PrimeWindowCeilingVinogradovKorobovRemainder.
- **Established [L], exponent frontier:** Actual zero width equals the infimum of admissible integrated-deficit and mean-square-band rates; this identifies the exact receiver exponent, not a smaller arithmetic rate. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/TheZeroWidthIsALandauExponent.lean.
- **Established [L], graded pole compression:** Pole cross-term differs from integrated Chebyshev deficit by `O(e^a)`. For `0≤α<1/2`, `|cross(a)|≤Ce^((1+2α)a)` forces `Δ≤α`; diagonal plus the same allowance suffices. Conversely `Δ≤α` gives every rate `β>α`. At α=0, `Ce^a` cross-term allowance already implies RH; exact nonnegative compression is stronger. Arithmetic bound open. E: B:GradedPoleCompressionWidthCriterion.
- **Open:** Source-derived one-sided prime-lag, Chebyshev-deficit or universal spline-packet bound at every positive exponential slack (the exponent-zero bound is stronger); alternate no-off-line-chain plus bounded progression/density-zero exception. Receiver equivalences do not supply these arithmetic estimates.
- **E:** R:PrimeLagLowerBoundRH; R:ChebyshevDeficitPrimitiveOneSidedRH; R:WeilLagDenseSampling; R:WeilLagPowerGapExceptionalSampling; R:WeilLagPrimeWindowMeanReceiver; R:PaperLagCayleyDeterminantReconstruction.

<a id="rw"></a>
### RW — Restricted Weil ground energy

- **Path:** Complete prime−Gamma−polar form → nonnegative constrained ground energy at every aperture → RH.
- **Established [L], positive-floor obstruction:** The actual Gaussian ceiling `μ(A)≤K exp((11/2)A−πe^{2(A−2)})` rules out any fixed positive floor on unbounded apertures and forces every positive transported floor to lose at least `πe^{2(A−2)}−(11/2)A+O(1)` in its exponent. Half-summable relative-step transport cannot be the source producer. This does not rule out `μ(A)≥0` or the graded negative-floor receiver. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCompensationTheory/TheApertureFloorMustDecayDoublyExponentially.lean.
- **Established [L], reverse twisted-arc support:** Nonnegative summable shifted-Gamma weights with `w₀>0` annihilate every prime-power lag; weighted truncated zero energy →0 while weighted pole term →`w₀/2>0`. Master declaration packages pole-compression/prime-blind/prime-polynomial receiver equivalences and the `β<1` pole-weight no-go; it supplies neither all-aperture compression sign nor a truncated-prime-sum limit. Supporting RW/EWU/OWU construction, not a separate route. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ReverseTwistedArcPoleCompression/MasterReverseTwistedArcPoleCompressionTheorem.lean.
- **Established [L], signed sampling model:** For distinct nodes, `Q(f)=Σf(γ_i)²−w f′(t)²`; polynomials of degree `<#nodes` have `Q≥0` when `wΛ(t)≤1`, `Λ=Σℓ_i′(t)²`. Degree `#nodes+1` has a negative witness for `w>0`, t outside the nodes. Smooth transfer: `2wΛ≤1`, `2≤d≤#nodes`, `|γ_i−t|≤δ`, `|f^(d)|≤M` give `Q(f)≥−2#nodes(Mδ^d/d!)²`; finite trigonometric sources of aperture A have `M≤A^dΣ(|a_k|+|b_k|)`. Open: actual ζ companion/depth price, complete-source passage and global error payment. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SignedArithmeticEnergyIncidence/OnLineCompanionsPayOffLineDepth.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SignedArithmeticEnergyIncidence/SmoothSourcesPayAtTheTaylorPrice.lean.
- **Established [L], fixed-aperture compactness:** Actual restricted form is `‖u‖²+Re⟨RJu,Ju⟩`, with compact form-domain embedding J. A unit-bounded weakly convergent sequence with energy limit `μ<0` has nonzero limit u and `W(u,u)≤μ‖u‖²`; adverse mass cannot disappear at fixed aperture. For abstract compactly negative forms, bounded weakly convergent sequences have asymptotically nonnegative escaping remainders. No aperture-uniform compactness or source positivity follows. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SignedArithmeticEnergyIncidence/TheRestrictedWeilFormIsCompactlyNegative.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SignedArithmeticEnergyIncidence/AdverseProfilesCannotVanish.lean.
- **Established [L], conditional rate refinement:** Full restricted-ground floor `μ(A)≥−depthFloorConst(W(A),A)` with W subexponential gives exponential floors at every rate `σ>Δ` and `polarGroundEnergyRate=Δ`; neither arithmetic hypothesis is supplied. If the polar rate exceeds Δ, some ε>0 makes every floor with factor `C e^(εA)` fail at some aperture. This is failure of subexponential control, not growth faster than every exponential. A finite zero window `[T−1,T+1]`, `|T|≥4`, with squared-depth mass >W and Δ>0 forces `180|T|>exp(W/(40Δ²))`; no height upper bound or RH follows. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/DilutionCrystallizationPincer/MasterDCIPincerTheorem.lean.
- **Established [L], unsigned depth sampling:** At any fixed positive aperture, a horizon-independent L² bound for `Σ|û(−z_q)−û(−conj z_q)|²` over all finite zero-copy sets is equivalent to uniform height-window squared-depth mass (`∃W, OffLineDepthLocallyBounded W`). Height-modulated smooth probes detect local depth with height-independent norm/lower bound. Neither equivalent arithmetic estimate nor signed full-form positivity is supplied. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/UnsignedDepthSamplingDetectsLocalMass.lean.
- **Established [L], energy-normalized width converse:** For every `β>Δ`, `Re Wζ(F,F)≥−Cβ(1+A)e^(2βA)(∫F²+D)` on real compact `C²` tests supported in `[-A,A]`, where `|gaugeDerivative(autoCorr F)|≤D`. No off-line clustering premise. Mass-only floor follows on classes with `D≤K∫F²`, fixed K; unrestricted L² converse remains open; the logarithmic Fourier-energy floor is supplied below. E: S:TheWidthBoundsTheEnergyNormalisedWeilFloor.
- **Established [L], actual-ζ logarithmic-energy floor:** `J(f)=∫|F̂(y)|²log(|y|+3)dy`; compact complex C² tests supported in [-a,a], a>0, with F̂ and J integrable satisfy `Re Wζ(f,f)≥−C₀(8a+a⁻¹)²e^(6aΔ)J(f)`, using unconditional multiplicity-weighted local zero counts; Δ≤1/2 gives an unconditional fixed-aperture floor. No clustering premise. Uniform off-line weighted count per sample window additionally gives a plain Fourier L² floor; on-line terms are nonnegative. Not a sharp width-rate equality, aperture-uniform sign, or identification with the exact Gamma quadratic form. E: S:TheUnconditionalWeilFloorInTheArchimedeanNormalisation.
- **Sampling support [L]:** Compact-source Fourier reproduction; multiplicity-weighted logarithmic-density Plancherel–Pólya bound. Complex samples with |Im z|≤σ cost `C_a A₀e^(12πaσ)∫|F̂(y)|²log(|y|+3)dy`; uniform weighted window count gives the plain Fourier L² bound. Actual ζ multiplicities and unconditional local count supply RW's logarithmic-energy floor. [S] Arbitrary plateau cutoff supported in [-R,R] gives the same mechanism with exponential factor `e^(4πRσ)`, conditional on its quadratic-decay kernel bound; no sharper cutoff/floor constructed. Fourier integrability hypotheses retained. E: H:LocalSamplingForCompactlySupportedSources; H:LogarithmicDensitySamplingSums; H:LogDensityPlancherelPolya; H:ComplexLocalSamplingAtDepth; S:TheUnconditionalWeilFloorInTheArchimedeanNormalisation.
- **Established [L], positive-floor transport:** Generic differentiable μ,β with `μ>0` throughout `[a,c]` and `−μ′≤β′μ` satisfy `μ(c)≥μ(a)e^(−β(c)+β(a))`; β′≥0 gives an interval-wide positive lower bound. Positivity on the entire closed interval is a premise; no actual-CCM first-crossing exclusion or source derivative bound. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticContinuationAndCriticalEnergy/GroundFloorNoCrossing.lean.
- **Established [L], cutoff tails:** Fixed first/second smooth-transition derivative constants discharge Barta far-defect bounds; weighted tail `≤C exp(−πe^(2b)−b−1−|u|)`, `b≥1`. Tail control is not full-form positivity. E: R:CCM/UniformConstantDischarges.
- **Relative-step transport [L], scoped obstruction:** `μ(A_J)≥μ(A₀)∏_(j<J)(1−c_j)` from `μ(A_(j+1))≥(1−c_j)μ(A_j)`, `0≤c_j≤1`. Certified `A₀≤3/10` and `Σc_j≤1/2` force `μ(A_J)≥1/10000`; this premise cannot hold along unbounded apertures, by the unconditional vanishing upper envelope. Harmonic losses `c_j=1/(j+2)` give product `1/(J+1)`; decaying floors are not excluded by the uniform-gap obstruction. E: C:TheApertureFloorTransportFromTheCertifiedEndpoint.
- **Established [L], shared:** Compact doubly collared factorial tests have complete Weil/full-zero-form coercivity on sufficiently high polynomial scale windows, uniformly in far truncation; explicit dyadic/truncated/lacunary thresholds are available; EWU records the exact scope. Arbitrarily long support in this restricted family is not all-test ground-energy positivity. E: A:CompactCriticalPolynomialWindowAndTemperednessBridge; A:ExplicitBlockThresholdsOfTheCriticalFamily.
- **Established [L], multiscale transfer:** Low/high critical unions have complete-form lower bounds `A_high E_high−K_low E_low`. For low window `[1,L₀]` and lacunary high blocks separated by `L₀⁸≤M₀`, cross-window prime cost is scale-independent; total low-window prime penalty is `O(log L₀)`, uniformly in block count/truncation. Positive high sources transfer to their grid-tent approximants, not arbitrary grid coefficients. E: A:MultiScaleLowModeAndGridTentSchurBridge.
- **Established [L], ground-energy/abscissa bridge:** Closed-support Rayleigh bound `μ(A)‖g‖₂²≤Re Wζ(g,g)` holds for smooth tests on `[-A,A]`. For `σ≥0`, eventual all-aperture floor `μ(A)≥−C exp(2σA)` forces `|Reρ−1/2|≤σ`, without an attainment premise; any deeper zero forces arbitrarily late violations for every `C`. Thus every-positive-slack ground floors suffice for RH. On a generic nonresonant aperture grid `A=kℓ/2+w`, RH iff `ZerosApproachLine` and eventual boundedness below of `μ(A)`. Direct Rayleigh transfer also gives ordinary lag floor `−C′e^(σv)` from ground floor `−Ce^(2σA)`, without using zeros. No source floor or `ZerosApproachLine` proof follows. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/GroundEnergyAbscissaAndLagGridAttainmentBridge.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/TheWallsMeasureOneZeroWidth.lean.
- **Established [L], explicit negative-ground threshold:** For `w>0`, an actual off-line zero of depth `δ>0`, transform amplitude `≥η>0`, and global depth ceiling `δ+1/V` forces `μ(A)<0` for every `A≥V/2+w` once `V≥oneParamLag(w,η,δ)`. Conditional visibility, not an actual off-line zero or source ground floor. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/NearTopZerosForceNegativeGroundEnergyBeyondAnExplicitAperture.lean.
- **Established [L], pole-vanishing ground receiver:** `μ₀=ccmRestrictedWeilPolarGroundEnergy`, distinct from unrestricted `μ`. Smooth closed-support polar-neutral `g` satisfies `μ₀(A)‖g‖₂²≤Re Wζ(g,g)`. RH iff all-aperture `μ₀≥0`, iff eventual constant lower bound, iff eventual `μ₀(A)≥−Cσe^(2σA)` for every `σ>0`; no attainment or `ZerosApproachLine` premise for continuous apertures. A fixed rate excludes zero depths `>σ`. The nonresonant sampled-grid equivalence still requires `ZerosApproachLine`. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/PolarGroundEnergyAndPoleFreeLagAbscissaBridge.lean.
- **Established [L], all-aperture arithmetic floor:** Every `A>0` has `μ₀(A)≥−(10+68e^A)`; for every `ε>0`, some `Cε≥0` gives `μ₀(A)≥−[Cε+(4+ε)e^A]` uniformly in `A`. Spectral-symbol comparison removes spatial boundary loss; Chebyshev/PNT give `Σ_{n≤⌊e^(2A)⌋}Λ(n)/√n≤34e^A` and `≤(2+ε)e^A+Cε`. Full prime sum retained; exponent `1` in aperture remains too large for RH. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/PolarGroundEnergyAndPoleFreeLagAbscissaBridge.lean.
- **Established [L], CCM ground-energy receiver:** Restricted Weil ground energy `μ(A)≥1/30` for `A≤21/200`, including completed form-domain vectors; any nonpositive/contact aperture is larger. Unconditionally `μ(A)<ε` for all large `A`, from compact theta plateaus with zero-form energy tending to `0` and nonvanishing mass. RH iff `μ(A)→0` at infinite aperture; under RH every `μ(A)≥0`, while a negative value persists under aperture enlargement. Neither asymptotic upper bound nor small-aperture floor proves the required global sign. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCriticalityTheory/ExplicitSmallApertureCCMGroundEnergyFloor.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCriticalityTheory/RestrictedWeilGroundEnergyCriticalAtInfinity.lean.
- **Established [L], window-gauge floor:** If `2A<log 2`, `μ(A)≥w(A):=−log(2A)−3A/2−2·archConstantReal−4A³`; at small aperture `0≤μ(A)−w(A)≤suzukiLimitingLogGroundEnergy+CA`. The full zero form has the corresponding compact-support lower bound. Local logarithmic growth does not control `μ(A)` at unbounded aperture. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCriticalityTheory/WindowGaugeLogarithmicGroundEnergyFloor.lean.
- **Established [L], Gaussian upper bound:** Theta-plateau tail control gives `μ(A)≤K exp((11/2)A−π exp(2(A−2)))` for `A≥3`; under RH, `0≤μ(A)` also holds. Doubly exponential upper decay strengthens criticality at infinity but cannot establish the all-aperture sign. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCriticalityTheory/GaussianCriticalityRestrictedWeilGroundEnergy.lean.
- **Established [L], local positivity:** Full real/complex compact `C²` Weil form and completed restricted ground energy have margin `1/5000` through radius `3/10`; stronger margin `1/1000` holds through `29/100`. Full real odd zero form has margin `1/1000` through `181/500`, retaining prime `2` and negative pole mass. No all-aperture sign. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCriticalityTheory/PastPlasticFoldWeilPositivity.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCriticalityTheory/ThreeTenthsSharpGaugeWeilPositivity.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticIncidencePositivity/SchurLendingOddWindowThreeHundredSixtyTwoThousandths.lean.
- **Established [L], bridge:** For actual ζ, full zero form/complete Weil energy is `≥‖F‖²/25` on real compact `C²` tests supported in aperture `≤1/100`. The compact source graph Weil form equals its Doob source energy; RH iff polar energy is bounded by graph edge energy on every source-graph vector. Depth-gap zeros and equal-depth clusters yield negative factorial-grid detectors only under their explicit zero-configuration hypotheses. These are nonselective local signs or equivalent receivers, not an all-test source sign. E: A:FactorialTentDepthGapClusterAndPolarGraphBridge.
- **Established [L], alternate receiver:** RH iff every compact-profile factorial-tent *single-scale uniform grid* has eventually nonnegative complete energy. Any off-line ζ zero yields negative full zero form, a single-shift origin-vanishing profile, and negative sufficiently fine grid detectors. Exact full-zero-form/Weil bridge retains pole and prime terms. Open: unconditional sign for all grids; finite or sparse high-scale positivity does not suffice. E: A:FactorialTentActualZetaDetectionAndFullZeroFormBridge.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** On the actual Suzuki compact core, complete Weil quotient energy equals both restricted Weil form and screw-operator quadratic form; complex compact source energy and even/odd interior energies pull back through the doubled real signed quotient with pole terms retained. Completion norms are not identified, and no sign follows. E: R:SuzukiRWCHMDQuotientCoordinates; R:CHMDComplexQuotientSourceCoordinates.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Corrected factorial smoothings have absolutely summable squared zero readouts with total mass `O((n+1)⁻¹)`; their weighted Gamma jump and logarithmic Fourier energies are `O((1+log(n+1))/(n+1))` across the stated Mellin strip. These bounds coexist with the corrected-tail positive complete-Weil energy growth relative to `L²` mass; no all-source sign follows. E: A:FactorialCorrectedSampling; A:FactorialTailLevyEnergy; A:FactorialTailFourierEnergy.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Compact Lipschitz smoothing approximates the literal full prime–Gamma–polar form with explicit error, including translated finite factorial patterns. Native compact `C²` factorial period-difference tests give strict negative Weil margins and reflection-defect gaps on synthetic off-line pairs, finite clusters and runs while annihilating the on-line lattice; literal arithmetic full-form approximation carries no sign for actual ζ. E: A:CompactFullWeilQuantitativeSmoothing; A:FactorialTranslatedTentApproximation; A:FactorialTentOffLineDetection; A:PeriodDifferenceOffLineDetection; A:FactorialTentClusterAndLatticeDetection.
- **Established [L]:** RH iff all-aperture negative index vanishes. Compact self-adjoint threshold pencil computes exact finite index and minimal positive repair. Odd/primitive positivity, full mixed Φ explicit formula, and exact Doob identity retain every prime cross-term; small apertures are positive. Literal Fejér box energy gives another one-sided receiver. Any finite family of distinct nonreal Xi orbits yields an equally dimensional compact polar-zero negative subspace with an L²-relative gap at sufficiently large support; threshold existential. Compact-polar Xi evaluation is injective and dense in its folded divisor Hilbert target; induced Weil pairing has zero radical. Exact Weil form splits into Markov creation/jump energy plus `½⟨cosh,g⟩²−½⟨sinh,g⟩²`; for real compact `C²` tests the archimedean functional is exactly `Ω(0)h(0)+∫₀∞[(h(0)−h(x))e^(x/2)/sinh x+4h(x)cosh(x/2)]dx`, with nonnegative jump integrand and factorized cosh moment. RH iff the sinh-capacity bound holds for every compact test/level. Absolute-value contraction lowers Markov energy but raises the pole part; finite-dimensional capacity is a `Q⁻¹` bound only when `Q` is positive definite. A zero-annihilating, prime-blind twisted-arc null family has dilation energy `a(1)=0`, `a′(1)=−2S` for its explicit prime slope `S`; if `S≠0`, the dilated blind cone has negative energy on one side of `1`.
- **Established [L]:** On real primitive-hyperplane tests, clamped first-cell quadrature bounds the high-frequency defect by `min((7/480)S,(91/50)L)` plus a resolvent term; the resulting `markovEnergy≥0` theorem retains finite-difference and `sinh`-dominance premises. No all-aperture ground-floor sign follows.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** For every real compact source, without parity or polar premise, `Re WeilFullFunctional(Λ)=Ω(0)h(0)+biScaleLevyEnergy−2·fullPairing(h)`; support-scale cutoff makes literal prime/polar terms exact. Signed packets retain all cross terms and have an exact source-window Plancherel energy; full-form Chebyshev-work bounds keep that energy cost. Prime-free full-form lower bounds use archimedean packet, derivative-free correlation-collar, or boundary-free Laplace-energy producers, including odd and primitive-hyperplane tests; none has a proved nonnegative right side. Finite causal-response energy is the squared critical-line Fourier norm of the pole-subtracted truncated `ΣΛ(n)n^(−s)` with an explicit high-frequency tail bound. The all-scale positive `wΛ(n)=(Λ(n)/log n)√2 n(1/√n−1/√(n+1))` bound adds `Δlog n·wΛ(n)·h(log n)`; it does not pay the negative full `Λ(n)/√n` term. E: B:SignedPrimePolarPacket; B:PacketEnergyPlancherel; B:AllParityWeilPoleWeightFunctionalAndChebyshevSynthesis; B:ChebyshevCellAndArchimedeanWeilProducer; B:LaplaceEnergyAndRoughCollarWeilProducer; B:CoprimeFactorialLiftAndSpectralLaplaceProducer; B:SpectralLaplaceEnergyCriticalLine; B:HighFrequencyLaplaceEnergyMeanValue.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** A continuous clamped exponential ramp has an exact polar integral. A compact continuous factorial producer preserves every positive integer atom, has full pairing `O(log N/√N)` and squared `L²` norm in `[1,16]`; it is not an autocorrelation because its origin value is zero but its top value is nonzero. Neither supplies a full-Weil positivity test. E: B:ClampedExponentialRamp; B:SignedPrimePolarContinuousFactorialEnergy; B:SignedPrimePolarContinuousFactorialProducer.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Integer-centered Chebyshev-cell bounds give another prime-free lower bound for the full Weil form; its right side retains Chebyshev work and an archimedean producer cost, without a proved sign. A continuous coprime `(2,3)` factorial profile has admitted `O(log N/√N)` full pairing but zero origin and nonzero top value, so it too is not an autocorrelation. E: B:ChebyshevHeadAndShearCellProducer.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** For compact `C¹` sources, band-split packet bounds retain an `O(log N)` remainder and derivative budget; derivative-free collar versions retain collar cost. Full-Weil lower bounds on real, even-polar, odd and primitive-hyperplane tests are not nonnegative. E: B:SawtoothPhaseCancellationAndBandSpectralProducer.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Causal-tent factorial profile is not a single autocorrelation (zero origin, nonzero top band). It is exactly half the difference of two compact-source autocorrelations, with equal origin energies in `[6√(N+1),46√(N+1)]`; its contrast preserves factorial integer-log atoms, has `L²` energy in `[2,32]`, and has admitted complete prime−polar pairing `O(log N/√N)` with an explicit bound. Signed cancellation between the two autocorrelations does not give positivity of either complete Weil form. E: B:SignedPrimePolarCausalSource; B:SignedPrimePolarSourceFactorization; B:SignedPrimePolarSourceEnergy; B:SignedPrimePolarSourceCertificate.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** For actual compact-source autocorrelations, the full Weil form is `Ω(0)h(0)+Lévy energy−2·fullPairing`; equal-origin factorial sources cancel `Ω(0)` in their contrast. Divisor-sampled response is exactly the factorial pairing; its `(1,−4,4)` bi-null combination is `O(log K)`, and normalized signed prime−polar contrast is `O(log N/N)`. These are difference estimates, not sign bounds for either source. E: B:FactorialSourceWeilContrastAndDivisorResponseProducer.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Both factorial sources have admitted Lévy jump energies and normalized lower bound `(3/23)e^(−1/2)log(N+1)−C`; their jump-energy difference is an exact signed causal integral. Small normalized prime−polar contrast does not make each complete Weil energy small or signed. E: B:SignedPrimePolarSourceArchimedeanCertificate.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Real factorial bi-null stencil obeys `|B(v)|≤9log(4v+1)+1`; clamped prime sums have factorial-cutoff error ≤`2log((1+ε)v+1)` under width/cap conditions. On the specified physical collar, actual factorial-ramp atomic and polar packets have an integrable `O(e^(u/2)(1+log(13N)−u)/√N)` envelope; reflected narrow tent has zero atomic packet there and signed-packet squared integral ≤`9/N`. For both actual reflected factorial sources, origin energy lies in `[6√(N+1),46√(N+1)]`; on `J=[−3δ,log(4N+1)]`, signed packet energy ≤`300000√N` at every cutoff `K≥10N`, scalar pairing squared ≤`50000h(0)²`, and increment pairing squared ≤`600000√N(h(0)−h(y))` for `|y|≤δ`. These are local packet controls, not complete-form positivity. E: B:SignedPrimePolarRealFactorialStencil; B:SignedPrimePolarClampedPrimeSum; B:SignedPrimePolarFactorialAtomicPacket; B:SignedPrimePolarFactorialPolarPacket; B:SignedPrimePolarFactorialPacketEnvelope; B:SignedPrimePolarReflectedTentPacket; B:SignedPrimePolarLocalizedPacket; B:SignedPrimePolarFactorialLocalEnergy; B:SignedPrimePolarFactorialLocalCertificate.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Compact continuous finite-jump sources have exact spectral full-Weil identity retaining all prime atoms and both polar branches. For each reflected factorial source the complete pairing stabilizes at cutoff `10(n+1)` and obeys `|fullPairing|≤224h(0)`; normalized complete energy is bounded below by `Ω(0)+factorialSourceNormalizedJumpLower−448→+∞`, so both individual Weil energies are eventually positive. This excludes eventual negative-energy production by this two-source family, not negative directions in general or RH. E: B:CompactFiniteJumpWeilFormula; B:FactorialCompleteFormCoercivity; B:FactorialWeilCoercivity.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Actual ramp–tent span has exact full-Weil Gram `Q(c,d)=c²A+cdM+d²D`, eventually `D≥n+1`, `M=O(log(n+1)/√(n+1))→0`; positivity reduces to `A≥M²/(4D)`. E: B:FactorialWeilGram; B:FactorialTentWeilCoercivity; B:FactorialWeilMixedDecay; B:FactorialWeilGramDecoupling.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Pure-ramp prime pairing is a finite log-weighted clamp integral; polar integral factors into source moments. Signed pole term is asymptotic to `−(1/2)log(n+2)/(n+1)`, positive polar loss to `(1/4)log(n+2)/(n+1)`, mixed Gram error `o(1/(n+1))`; mixed error alone cannot pay the pole. - **Established [L], auxiliary:** A compact zero-integral BV factorial seed has an arithmetic Mellin-zero completion agreeing with the actual ramp for `x≥0`; omitted negative tail has squared `L²` mass ≤`100/(n+1)`. At every fixed zeta zero with `Reρ>0`, the reflected compact-source readout is ≤`10/(√(n+1)Reρ)` and tends to zero, regardless of the zero's position. The completion retains a nonzero negative-end pole; its unique polar-admissible correction has exact zero-readout `c/ρ≠0`. Neither approximation nor repaired completion excludes off-line zeros. E: B:FactorialMuntzSeed; B:FactorialMuntzCompletion; B:FactorialMuntzTail; B:FactorialMuntzReadout; B:ClampedRampLatticeSum; B:FactorialMuntzBoundaryPole; B:FactorialMuntzBoundaryRepair.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Canonical polar repair differs from compact ramp by a tail bounded by `12e^(−3|x|/2)/√(n+1)` and has analytic readout on `Re s>−1`; tail readouts tend uniformly to zero on `Re s≥−1+δ`, compact-ramp zero readouts are uniformly `O(1/√(n+1))`. Smooth repairs retain both polar branches and full Abel prime pairing; their zero-side Weil sum is strictly positive for every `n` without RH, and sufficiently fine smoothings have positive complete energy. Correction tail has squared mass `Ω(1/(n+1))` and prime/polar budget `O(1/(n+1))`. This family gives no all-source sign; separate committed result closes only ramp–tent span. E: B:FactorialMuntzUniformTail; B:FactorialMuntzAnalyticRepair; B:FactorialMuntzWeilSmoothing; B:FactorialMuntzZeroReadout; B:FactorialMuntzZeroSideLimit; B:FactorialMuntzTailSmoothing; B:FactorialMuntzTailPrimePolarBudget; B:FactorialMuntzTailPhaseCells; B:FactorialMuntzTailMass.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Corrected factorial tail is a bounded-primitive derivative; its smoothings have weighted Fourier primitive mass `O((n+1)⁻³)` and decaying fixed-zero readouts. Archimedean energy grows logarithmically relative to tail `L²` mass, while prime/polar loss stays bounded relative to mass. For large `n` and fine smoothing, complete Weil energy is `≥log(n+1)/(2048(n+1))>0` and its energy/mass ratio exceeds every fixed bound. Small primitive/readout norms do not make this family an RH-selective witness. E: B:ClampedLatticePrimitive; B:FactorialMuntzTailPrimitive; B:FactorialMuntzTailReadoutDecay; B:FactorialMuntzTailPrimitiveEnergy; B:ExpDecayFourierMass; B:FactorialMuntzTailArchimedeanGrowth; B:FactorialMuntzTailWeilGrowth.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** The corrected factorial tail has weighted square mass `O((n+1)⁻¹)` uniformly for `−1/4≤Re s≤5/4`, linear translation-energy control down to displacement `1/(n+1)`, and a logarithmic translation budget. These source estimates do not orient the complete Weil form or turn the factorial family into an RH selector. E: A:FactorialTailPhase; A:FactorialTailWeightedMass; A:FactorialTailTranslation; A:FactorialTailLogTranslation.
- **Established [L]:** The actual constrained even-polar form has a self-adjoint ground operator with compact resolvent; a normalized ground eigenstate exists at every positive aperture. Operator realization does not establish nonnegative ground energy. E: R:CCMRestrictedWeilEvenPolarGroundOperator.
- **Established [L], finite-stage CCM:** Literal `θ_S` restricts to a bounded linear isomorphism between archimedean and semilocal Sonin spaces at every finite prime set/aperture, intertwines Fourier, and has Hardy–Titchmarsh image `c·σ_S·υ∞f` with exact finite Euler symbol. No infinite-prime limit or Weil sign follows. E: R:FieldOneFiniteStageThetaSoninIsomorphism; R:FieldOneFiniteStageThetaHardyTitchmarsh.
- **Established [L], CCM oscillator identification:** Exact `sl₂` triples and even-Hermite synthesis intertwine finite-core Jacobi/number/dilation operators; `ℓ²≃L²_even` is unitary. Cyclic Jacobi moments equal Gamma moments and uniquely determine the Gamma probability measure. No integrated group representation or all-aperture ground sign claimed. E: R:CCMMetaplecticOscillatorTriple; R:CCMMetaplecticRepresentationIdentification.
- **Established [L], CCM Sonin trace:** For `GΛ=1−PΛ−P̂Λ−SΛ`, `GΛ` is trace-class, self-adjoint, `‖GΛ‖<1`, `Tr GΛ=0` (`Λ>0`). For compact logarithmic `g`, `−W∞(g★⋆g)=2logΛ‖g‖₂²+Tr(ϑg SΛ ϑg†)+Tr(ϑg GΛ ϑg†)` (`Λ>1`); each shell contributes `logΛ‖g‖₂²`. Sonin domination holds on a sufficiently small support depending on `Λ`; at unit cutoff it is exactly weighted generic-part positivity. Tracelessness does not supply that weighted sign. E: R:CCMArchimedeanSoninGenericDecomposition; R:CCMArchimedeanSoninCutoffTraceFormula; R:CCMArchimedeanSoninGenericTraceless.
- **Established [L], CCM Toeplitz/time-band reduction:** `τΛ(u)=Tr(GΛϑu)` is bounded, continuous, real, even, with `τΛ(0)=0`; weighted generic trace equals `∫∫conj(g(s))g(t)τΛ(t−s)dsdt`. At `Λ=1`, fixed-test Sonin domination iff this quadratic form is nonnegative. With `Q=1−P`, `Q̂=1−P̂`, `G=M−A`, `M=QQ̂Q−S≥0`, `A=PP̂+P̂P−PP̂P`; both trace-class. Hence `Re Tr(TGT†)≥−Re Tr(TAT†)` for bounded `T`; the time-band sign remains unpaid. E: R:CCMArchimedeanSoninToeplitzKernel; R:CCMArchimedeanSoninTimeBandSplit.
- **Established [L], CCM time-band kernel:** `T(u)=Tr(PP̂ϑu)=∫₀¹∫₀¹4e^(u/2)cos(2πxz)cos(2πe^u xz)dzdx`. Differentiation under the integral gives `T′,T″`; `T″` is continuous with explicit compact-interval bounds, and `T′(0)=1`. Weighted generic-part/Toeplitz positivity remains open. E: R:CCMArchimedeanSoninTimeBandKernel; R:CCMArchimedeanSoninTimeBandSlope.
- **Established [L], mean-zero tent calculus:** For continuous compact mean-zero `g` with primitive `Γ`, kernel `(|t−s|−w)₊`, `w≥0`, has quadratic form `−2Re∫conj Γ(x)Γ(x+w)dx`; at `w=0`, `−2‖Γ‖₂²`. Continuous tent-mixture modulus is `≤2(∫₀^δ|h|)‖Γ‖₂²`; time-band trace `T(|u|)` has an exact tent-mixture Taylor formula. E: H:MeanZeroTentQuadraticForm; R:CCMArchimedeanSoninSmallSupportDomination.
- **Established [L], CCM local domination:** Put `B=ccmTimeBandBound₂(0)≥0`, `ε₀=1/[2(B+1)]>0`. For `CCMCompactSchwartzLogTest g`, `supp g⊆(−ε,ε)`, `0<ε≤ε₀`, `∫g=0`: `Re Tr(ϑ(g★⋆g)S₁)≤−Re W∞(g★⋆g)`. Every nonzero real nonnegative test in the same support class has the strict reverse inequality; mean-zero cannot be dropped. Local unit-cutoff archimedean/Sonin comparison, not full prime−polar positivity at all apertures. E: R:CCMArchimedeanSoninSmallSupportDomination (`ccmSoninTraceDomination_of_small_support`).
- **Established [L], common-domain signs:** Suzuki screw positivity iff restricted Weil positivity on the completed form domain; fixed-aperture CHMD graph and Weil-form core norms are equivalent. Neither comparison proves positivity or an aperture-uniform bound. See SZ/CHMD. E: R:SuzukiScrewCompletionInsideTheWeilFormDomain; R:CHMDGraphNormIsTheWeilFormNormOnAnAperture.
- **Established [L], finite CCM determinant:** Complex-shift Lerch continuation gives circle-Dirac zeta determinant `1−exp(−2iaz)` for `a>0, Im z<0`; rank-one determinant correction is exact. Finite published metric has a constant-source log-Dirac/real-zero packet branch or a centered-source real-spectrum branch. A simple even kernel selects the first and gives, for `Im z<0`, determinant `−i exp(a)^(−iz)·packetTransform`. No identification with the full Ξ divisor or infinite-cutoff sign. E: H:ComplexShiftLerch; R:CCMCircleDiracZetaDeterminant; R:CCMLogDiracRankOneDeterminant; R:CCMLogDiracDeterminantTheorem.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** `coshMultiplier`/`sinhMultiplier` obey Lorentz boost and translation laws; their autocorrelation difference has an invariant Casimir and exact nonnegative Krein defect identities. The squared Minkowski form factors as `cosh(αx)·h_f(x)·(h_cosh(x)+h_sinh(x))`; its null locus includes either zero factor, not only `h_f=0`. These identities do not sign the actual prime−Gamma−polar form. E: B:HyperbolicLorentzBoostAndMinkowskiLightCone; B:ClassicalFoundationBoundaries.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Corrected factorial tails have multiplicity-weighted absolute zero-square mass `O(log(n+1)/(n+1))`; global zero-disk overlap and strip/vertical energy have matching logarithmic control. Fine smoothings have positive Weil diagonal bounded below by a fixed fraction of zero-square mass, so absolute sampling does not make this tail negligible or establish unrestricted Weil positivity. E: A:FactorialTailSampling; A:ZeroDiskOverlap; A:FactorialTailVerticalEnergy; A:FactorialTailStripEnergy; A:FactorialTailRelativeMargin.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Full zero-side mixed pairing obeys `|W(f,g)|≤√(Z(f)Z(g))`. Its universal Cauchy–Schwarz lower bound for subtracting a corrected source from a positive-margin tail is positive iff square-mass ratio `ε<(√(1+δ)−1)²`; a two-mode involution attains this threshold. Corrected factorial combinations have rank-one pole readout `F_k(ρ)·Λ_pole/ρ`; unsmoothed zero-null combinations are exactly `ker Λ_pole`, where compact-ramp and negative-tail readouts coincide. These are source/zero-side classifications, not an unrestricted arithmetic Weil sign. E: A:SourceRelativeSchurMarginAndPerturbationCalculus; A:RankOnePoleReadoutAndZeroNullspace.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** On `ker Λ_pole`, every smoothed corrected-factorial combination has zero readout, zero zero-side square mass and zero zero-side Weil pairing; its compact-ramp and negative-tail combinations have identical zero-side square mass and Weil diagonal. Two distinct scales give an explicit nonzero pole-free coefficient vector. Zero-side nullity is not positivity of the complete arithmetic form on arbitrary tests. E: A:RankOnePoleReadoutAndZeroNullspace.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Factorial collar shifted frequencies are irrational and four harmonic branches at fixed layer have gap `≥k/15`. The dyadic Bernoulli stencil matching harmonics `1,2,4` is uniquely `(4,−2,1/4)` with first-harmonic dominance margin `≥1/4`; this does not pay full prime/polar energy. ### FU — CCM-FU signed prime scheduling

- **Path:** Signed Möbius/prime scheduling → completed Abel moment A(M)/M bounded above → RH.
- **Established [L]:** One-sided Abel receiver retains polar/lattice terms. Prescribed finite prime blocks admit a helper core with positive Boolean energy differences. A prime-exhaustive history can decrease complete clock energy at each block's cutoff. Compact-cutoff critical Abel clocks are continuous in the full BV prime-jump graph; their closed positive clock cone contains every cutoff-fixed continuous BV target. After a target-dependent cutoff fixed before scale, distinct actual primes approximate every complete graph vector at all sufficiently large common scales with arbitrarily small diagonal cost. Landau continuation of a literal positive tail retains its compact head, and any real-analytic meromorphic Laplace continuation with a nonreal pole excludes an eventually one-signed source tail. Cone membership and realization do not orient the signed energy.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Distinct deep terminal-prime clocks approximate any fixed `SourceGraph` vector with vanishing total diagonal norm; their purely off-diagonal `sourcePair` and bounded-operator sums converge quantitatively to the target quadratic energies. For a resolvent eigenvector `μ>1/2`, such clocks have strictly negative off-diagonal `sourcePair` at all sufficiently large scales. This realizes a conditional negative certificate; no arithmetic theorem makes the required off-diagonal form nonnegative. E: B:PrimeGraphClockOffDiagonalSynthesisAndStationaryEnergyProducer.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Exact two-clock `sourcePair` is normalized edge correlation minus mass correlation at common center `log(N/q)` and lag `log(q/p)`; all cutoff and arithmetic edge terms remain. No lag-only sign or completed scheduling bound follows. E: B:PrimeGraphClockCorrelationKernels.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** At fixed clock cutoff, bounded total prime amplitude gives compact full-graph packet closure; cofinal clock centers then decay to zero. Bounded-norm, vanishing-diagonal actual-prime packets can nevertheless stay pairwise separated when cutoff grows, with signed energy tending to zero. Thus low diagonal cost is not complete-graph compactness or a scheduling sign. E: B:PrimeClockGraphCompactness; B:PrimeClockGraphTailTightness; B:PrimeClockGraphEscape.
- **Established [L], receiver:** The completed outgoing charge differs from the normalized Möbius–divisor Abel action by a uniformly bounded amount; one-sided power bounds force `Θ≤1/2+max(0,c)`. No completed scheduling estimate is supplied. E: R:QuasiRHDilationTailHyperbolaAndOutgoingPotentialAbscissaLaw.

### PA — Prime–archimedean source passivity

- **Path:** Continued exact prime−archimedean trace → high-frequency passivity, subexponential Bernstein mismatch, or signed compact moment completion → RH.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** On `Re s>1/2`, the continued actual prime–archimedean relative trace is the weighted WL lag-pole series plus its explicit affine mass term. Transport is unconditional; high-frequency current sign remains open. E: R:PrimeArchWeilLagReceiverIdentity.
- **Established [L]:** Finite-frequency trace and source recovery are exact. Signed current multiplier positivity on all upper half-strip slices is RH-equivalent; frequency cutoffs converge in norm. Arbitrarily thin exponential envelopes for the Bernstein packet mismatch between one nondegenerate coupled-Sonin aperture and the endpoint prime–Gamma source imply RH, with all Hausdorff positivity premises discharged unconditionally. For each fixed Hausdorff order `k`, calibrated moment differences are nonnegative at every sufficiently large shift `r`; RH reduces to the finitely many shifts below an order-dependent threshold for each `k` (no order-uniform threshold established). Absolute-current observations retain magnitude but erase orientation. Sharp prime-weight logarithmic-energy floor and rigidity do not give the sign. For the critical Stokes carrier, passive deletion and signed correction coincide at all degrees and survive unconditionally at the two-jet level via a canonical one-atom countercarrier; at all degrees, each distinct prime/depth node has raw singleton mass `<2x`, while every completed Xi moment witness has mass `≥2x` at its thermal nodes, so any exact completion must increase infinitely many singleton masses and, under the supercarrier constraint, also remove mass. Passive deletion and finite atom repair fail.
- **Established [L]:** Bounded-support finite signed-measure representation of every normalized prime–archimedean moment is RH-equivalent: off-line reciprocal-square Xi atoms obstruct a real-support resolvent. At the fixed endpoint scale, source Bernstein coefficient-index probability measures are defined at every degree; if RH fails, their energy growth and weak limit recover the finite maximal off-line reciprocal-square packet's amplitude, occupation coordinates and fibre multiplicities, with coordinates determined up to conjugation. This is an unconditional RH-or-tomography alternative, not a sign or exclusion theorem.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** At radius `1`, one fixed-width, nonempty actual Xi ordinate band centered at `T=√(r/(k+1))` contributes at least half its absolute Hausdorff atom mass with positive real sum whenever `r≥A(k+1)²`, uniformly in `k`. The full normalized prime–archimedean moment difference is this band plus an absolutely convergent complement; any negative full difference requires a larger opposite complement deficit. No sign for that complement or all-order Hausdorff positivity follows. E: B:CompensatedHausdorffPhase; B:HausdorffSaddleScale; R:PrimeArchQuadraticSaddleBands.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Every prescribed finite block of Hausdorff orders is simultaneously nonnegative beyond the maximum of its order-dependent shift thresholds. This finite maximum supplies neither a uniform threshold over all orders nor a sign at earlier shifts. E: R:PrimeArchFiniteOrderHausdorffPositivity.
- **Open:** Arithmetic sign of the high-frequency current numerator, unit-collar exponential envelope for the coupled-Sonin/endpoint Bernstein mismatch, or source sign at all remaining low shifts across unbounded Hausdorff orders. Positive quadratic saddle bands still require control of their signed complement. Constructing the signed compact moment completion would itself prove RH; divisor tomography observes an off-line packet rather than excluding it.

<a id="tt"></a>
### TT — Theta-tail and heat–Cassini currents

- **Path:** Theta source → directed signed covariance/Wold decay or odd Weil-form sign → RH.
- **Established [L]:** Exact seam/tail and odd-four identities; sharp Wold boundedness threshold t<1/2 and natural-cutoff RH energy criterion. Odd/primitive Weil positivity has an exact half-line reflected-prime inequality and Φ-Doob form. Translation-averaged entire strip-decaying densities are independent of a zero's transverse depth; this observation cannot orient off-line zeros. A nonzero continuous periodic Kelvin-chirp profile cannot give a positive-definite separator when support ratio `e^L>4+2√2`; unbounded combs remain outside this bound. Finite-prefix, sign-blind, and masked convergence do not control global sign.
- **Established [L], auxiliary only:** For a positive Hölder roof over a mixing subshift, with summable periodic-orbit series on every `σ>1`, the Ruelle reciprocal has a holomorphic continuation to some `Re s>1−ε`. Transfer, trace-splitting and quasi-compactness modules discharge the abstract continuation; no equality with ζ or RH-strength strip follows.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** The actual theta-cusp enriched clock current is positive in the inner chamber `0<t≤min(2,u)` when action `Z(u,t)≥710e⁴`. Bounded-action inner region and any transfer to the global signed TT/Weil receiver remain open. E: B:ThetaInnerLargeActionCurrent.
- **Open:** Signed nonlocal covariance or full odd/primitive form positivity across all rapidities and test scales.
- **E:** R:XiHeatCassiniWoldNaturalSquareEnergySubhalf; R:PaperWoldDirectedCovarianceRH; R:WeilMarkovOddPositivity; R:WeilMarkovPrimitiveHyperplane; R:TranslationAveragedDensityDepthBlindness; R:KelvinChirpSeparatorBound; H:SymbolicTransferBounds; H:SymbolicTransferHolomorphy; H:SymbolicRuellePollicottBounds; H:OuterSpectralContinuation; H:SymbolicTraceSplitting; H:SymbolicQuasiCompactness; H:SymbolicRuellePollicott.

### LA — Line anchor

- **Path:** Literal theta source → all-height Laguerre/Hadamard or Jensen sign → real-rooted Ξ → RH.
- **Established [L]:** Exact sign/zero-count receivers (`RH ⇔ Re(ξ'/ξ) ≥ 0` on `Re s > 1/2`), fixed-time heat-slice Taylor positivity equivalence to the de Bruijn–Newman threshold (`newmanConstant ≤ λ`), exact negative index of the slice coefficient operator, and finite-height/slice controls. For `S(T)=xiZeroCount(T)−routeKSmoothRVMCount(T)`, `∫_A^T |S(t)|dt ≥ cT/log T` eventually; `S` is not `L¹(0,∞)`. Near-double off-line pairs and exceptional heights evade presently controlled finite data. E: R:ZeroCountRemainderNotIntegrable.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Unconditional Balazard–Saias–Yor identity identifies the critical-line weighted `log|ζ|` integral with an absolutely convergent, multiplicity-weighted sum over right-of-line ζ zeros. This detects off-line mass but supplies no source sign making that mass vanish. E: R:BalazardSaiasYorZeroSum.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** The actual Xi theta moment source has all positive normalized fluctuation moments tending to zero at its increasing saddle; finite Laguerre/Jensen–Hermite identities, recurrences and fixed-degree coefficientwise Xi Jensen–Hermite limits hold. Fixed-degree convergence is not all-degree uniform hyperbolicity. E: R:ThetaJensenPolynomialWard; R:ThetaJensenSaddleCoercivity; R:ThetaJensenCenteredMomentBounds; R:ThetaJensenCenteredMomentLimit; R:XiThetaPolynomialExpectation; R:XiThetaConcentration; R:LaguerreJensenRecurrence; R:LaguerreHermiteNormalization; R:XiThetaHermiteMixedLimit; R:XiJensenHermiteIdentity; R:XiJensenHermiteLimit.
- **Established [L], lag-to-heat bridge:** Actual zero width `Δ=supρ|Reρ−1/2|∈[0,1/2]`; RH iff `Δ=0`. For all but countably many widths `0<w<log 2/2` and every `σ≥0`, eventual lag floor `Re P_w(v)≥−Ce^(σv)` iff `Δ≤σ`. Heat zeros satisfy `(Im z)²≤max(Δ²−2t,0)` for `t≥0`, hence real-rootedness for `t≥Δ²/2` (corpus normalization). RW's ground-floor theorem bounds the same zero width via the abscissa bridge. Unconditional lag exponent remains `1/2`; no smaller width or RH-scale heat bound. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/LagRateBoundsTheDeBruijnNewmanHeatTime.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/GroundEnergyAbscissaAndLagGridAttainmentBridge.lean.
- **Open:** All-height arithmetic sign, including remaining low-order slices and an arithmetic separator for near-double off-line pairs.
- **E:** R:XiLogDerivReCriterion; R:XiSliceFiniteOrderReduction; R:PaperHeatSliceCriterion; R:PaperSliceCoefficientInertia; R:PaperSliceCoefficientRepair.

<a id="hf"></a>
### HF — Positive Hodge form on arithmetic cohomology

- **Path:** Arithmetic cohomology/full-positive weight → RH; alternate prime-window discrepancy ceiling `≤e^(L/2)+O(L)` → WL's one-sided deficit primitive → RH.
- **Established [L], full signature law:** For every tempered zero configuration, supremal Weil negative index on compact `C²` test families equals the number of distinct right-half-plane zeros in `ℕ∞`; no finiteness premise. For actual ζ, intersection form has `1+n` positive squares on some family iff at least n distinct zeros lie right of `1/2`; infinite right-zero set iff unbounded Hodge defect. Multiplicities weight the form, not index. Classification supplies no arithmetic bound forcing index zero. Tempered vertically recurrent configurations have index `0` or `∞`; recurrence = every zero's real part approximated arbitrarily high. Positive vertical periodicity implies recurrence; no ζ recurrence or general finite-Kronecker-rank Euler-product theorem supplied. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCastelnuovoSeveri/TemperedConfigurationPontryaginLaw.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCastelnuovoSeveri/TheHodgeDefectCountsTheRightZeros.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCastelnuovoSeveri/TheKroneckerRankDichotomy.lean.
- **Established [L], parity index:** On tempered functional-equation-symmetric configurations, maximal negative index on compact complex `C²` tests is `#{Reρ>1/2, Imρ>0}` in even sector and `#{Reρ>1/2, Imρ≥0}` in odd sector. Actual ζ has no real nontrivial zeros: each conjugate right-zero pair contributes one negative square in each sector. Multiplicity does not increase index. No arithmetic proof of zero index. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCastelnuovoSeveri/TheReflectionSectorsOfTheHodgeDefect.lean (`sector_pontryagin_law`, `zeta_sector_hodgeDefect_iff`).
- **Established [S], Deninger dimension:** For algebraically closed characteristic-zero C, `dim_ℚ((C×/μ(C))⊗ℚ)=#C≥ℵ₀`; non-torsion premise supplied. Algebraic dimension, not an arithmetic cup/trace realization or positive form. E: R:Deninger/CharZeroUnitRankDischarges.
- **Established [L], aperture filtration:** `hodgeDefectAt(Z,a)` is monotone, bounded by distinct right-zero count and exhausts it as `a→∞` for tempered Z; actual ζ defect is zero for `a≤3/10`. Every finite right-zero count is detected at some finite aperture; no effective detection horizon or all-aperture zero-defect bound. - **Established [L], scaling-surface calibration:** On cycles `aH+bV+f`, local intersection is polar pairing minus actual Weil form; Hodge index/one-positive-square is RH-equivalent, and an effectivity satisfying Riemann–Roch plus ampleness would suffice.
