### CHMD — Causal Hyperbolic–Modular Duality

- **Path:** Exact Weil source → causal prime-wave, Chebyshev-discrepancy derivative pairing, or Möbius-rest one-sided bound, or theta-Doob odd-sector half-bound → RH.
- **Established [L], unconditional but nonselective:** Actual ζ zero configuration is nonempty, hence its CHMD Klein–Gordon energy moment, quartic moment and origin wave are strictly positive. Fixed-aperture graph/BV constants are discharged; none controls signed prime-wave at every rapidity, primitive eigenvalues or odd ground energy. E: R:CHMDZeroMeasurePositivityUnconditional; R:UniformConstantDischargesCHMDDoobGraph; R:UniformConstantDischargesCHMDPolar.
- **Established [L], bridge:** On the compact source graph, Weil form equals source Doob energy and RH iff every graph edge pays polar energy; on odd profiles, polar energy is cosh-square mass plus twice the sinh-moment square. This identifies the exact pole charge, not its arithmetic payment. E: A:FactorialTentDepthGapClusterAndPolarGraphBridge.
- **Established [L], bound-state/abscissa bridge:** Odd bound-state space is finite-dimensional iff the off-line zero set is finite; finiteness implies zeros approach the line. RH iff odd bound-state space is finite-dimensional **and** `AsymptoticAbscissaLaw`; under that law, a nonzero odd bound-state space must be infinite-dimensional. Neither finiteness nor the abscissa law is source-proved. E: R:CrossRouteReceiverBridges.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Actual complex RW and EWU/OWU interior energies descend exactly to a doubled real signed quotient; Suzuki screw and restricted Weil agree there on their common compact core. Finite sieve prime-chain energy has a rank-one quotient-chain factorization and terminal-octave quantization. Neither identifies completed norms nor signs the actual all-scale prime-chain form. E: R:CHMDComplexQuotientSourceCoordinates; R:SuzukiRWCHMDQuotientCoordinates; T:PrimeChainSieveBridgeAndSpectralCutoff.
- **Established [L], fixed-aperture norm comparison:** For every aperture `a>0`, constants `c_a,C_a>0` give `c_a‖u‖_W²≤‖testLift u‖_graph²≤C_a‖u‖_W²` on the real Suzuki core. Signed-quotient coordinate is Weil-form-norm continuous; no lower quotient bound or aperture-uniform constants. RW sign transfers to the closure of this core's quotient image; source sign remains open. E: R:CHMDGraphNormIsTheWeilFormNormOnAnAperture (`exists_testLift_norm_sq_equiv_weilFormNorm`); R:SuzukiScrewCompletionInsideTheWeilFormDomain.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Critical Abel prime-clock `sourcePair` equals normalized edge-kernel minus mass-kernel at its common cutoff center and logarithmic prime lag. The exact kernel retains archimedean and prime-power edges; it has no proved arithmetic sign. E: B:PrimeGraphClockCorrelationKernels.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Actual quarter-strip zero counts inject into height-box counts and inherit unconditional Ingham–Huxley density and high-abscissa zero-free bounds; prime-window Chebyshev discrepancy inherits Vinogradov–Korobov endpoint rates. Density bounds do not exclude a single fixed off-line zero or prove the CHMD nodal sign. E: T:WindowedQuarterZeroDensityAndCHMDBoundStateCount; A:InghamHuxleyHeightBoxZeroMajorant.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Fixed-cutoff bounded-amplitude prime-clock families have compact full-graph closure, but growing-cutoff actual-prime packets with bounded norm, vanishing diagonal and energy tending to zero can escape every norm-compact set. E: B:PrimeClockGraphCompactness; B:PrimeClockGraphTailTightness; B:PrimeClockGraphEscape.
- **Established [L]:** Toeplitz/sech positivity, Λ-prime-wave and Λ-free Möbius-rest one-sided bounds, and absence of odd resolvent eigenstates above 1/2 are RH-equivalent. Stieltjes integration by parts expresses the Klein–Gordon prime wave via the classical discrepancy `Δ(s)=Σ_{log n<s} Λ(n)/√n−2e^(s/2)` and the exponentially localized odd derivative kernel `∂_s K_y(s)`; RH holds iff `∫₀∞ ∂_s K_y(s) Δ(s) ds` is bounded above (or bounded below) for `y≥0`. Integrated deficit has a bounded transient head. Exact folded Möbius-wave/tail/remainder and signed `(Λ(n)−1)` lattice-replacement waves give RH-equivalent one-sided bounds. Half-time secant single-square even Weil sign for every rapidity is RH-equivalent; polar anti-nodes remove pole terms but their nonnegativity is only RH-conditional. On one-signed odd tests the prime cross term is a nonnegative opposite-side bonus minus a nonnegative same-side term. For a profile supported through `a` and antitone only on `[c,a]`, prime terms with `log n ≥ max(2a/3,(a+c)/2)` are nonnegative; the low-prime window and Lévy form remain. The arithmetic resolvent has simple eigenvalue `1`; every nonzero primitive eigenvalue is `<1`, not necessarily `≤1/2`. RH iff the odd bound-state space is zero iff the even bound-state space is `span{1}`. If off-line zeros are finite, odd/even/total bound-state dimensions equal `(q, q+1, 2q+1)`, where `q` counts quarter-plane off-line zeros. One-signed odd eigenstates with eigenvalue `>1/2` vanish; sign-changing ones are not excluded. Even-Θ Doob on odd tests has a negative local pole term and an origin-crossing sum-of-values collar. The secondary odd profile `m_a=(Θ(·−a)−Θ(·+a))/(2sinh(a/2))` is positive on `x>0` and has positive sinh pole source. Its normalized shift differences span an infinite-dimensional signed radical of zero energy and resolvent eigenvalue `1/2`. For `f=m_a w`, `w` even, the pole-square pairing is nonnegative, folded edge terms are Dirichlet squares, and the absorbed archimedean kernel is pointwise nonnegative. Harmonic θ ground potential is `<1`; exact full source energy equals stationary energy minus archimedean diagonal and prime-shift quadratic terms. Stationary absolute-value contraction is strict off one-sign states; prime-shift is nonnegative on nonnegative pairs. Total edge energy `‖p.snd‖²` splits into a `2×2` archimedean/prime × envelope/nodal table giving a quantitative nodal lower bound on `(1−μ)/μ` for any nonzero primitive eigenvector; primitive ground subtraction `primGraph` satisfies `sourceEnergy(primGraph q)=weilForm q` on the even sector; eigenvector mass-space Fourier/cosine readouts and translated-cardinal pairing laws force polar moments and Ξ-zero Fourier readouts of threshold eigenvectors (`μ=1/2`) to vanish, and give an exact finite-family eigenvector projection decomposition of `sourcePair` and `sourceEnergy`. Orthonormal eigenvector families satisfy exact Pythagorean decompositions and unconditional edge/graph Bessel inequalities, a shift-uniform odd-polar Bessel bound on `⟨w_i, tanhL⟩²`, an exact cardinal spectral deficit at nonreal Ξ-zeros, a universal Rayleigh-to-energy lower bound and primitive-contraction nodal inequality, and a shift-independent odd cardinal Fourier-Re mode. A universal Riesz–resolvent and stationary-generator dictionary on `SourceGraph` (`(∀ p, sourcePair p q = ⟨p.fst, v⟩) ⇔ q = rieszLift(v + 2q.fst) ⇔ (I − 2·resolvent)q.fst = resolvent v`) identifies `signedRadical` with `ker(resolvent − ½I)` and gives `k×k` spectral readout Gram matrix identities, individual nodal-lobe three-term nonnegative decompositions, Courant nodal-lobe negativity for bound states (`μ ≥ 1/2`), exact odd half-mass lobe laws, universal primitive-contraction resolvent polarization, and bilateral stationary + prime-shift reinforcement in `-4 sourcePair(p⁺, p⁻)` forcing `ν ≤ μ_max` (and strict `ν < μ_max` when the primitive `oneL`-defect is dominated by `4 ⟨primeShiftOperator p⁺.fst, p⁻.fst⟩`). None is a full prime+Gamma sign.
- **Established [L]:** The stationary resolvent is a strict contraction on nonzero vectors; exact second-resolvent and ground-perturbation identities separate stationary energy, prime jump energy, and local diagonal potential. Prime generator satisfies Beurling–Deny nodal contraction; for eigenvectors, the absolute-value edge norm gives `−4 sourcePair(p⁺,p⁻) ≤ ((1−μ)/μ)‖q.fst‖²`, and each lobe obeys an exact five-term jump-versus-diagonal balance. A balanced nonnegative nodal graph is mass- and edge-orthogonal to a primitive eigenvector. Native finite Xi-Jordan packets have signed arithmetic radical exactly at lower Jordan layers after polar subtraction; this identifies a kernel, not a sign.
- **Established [L]:** For secondary odd profiles `m_a w`, midpoint folding absorbs the archimedean origin collar into a nonnegative direct-minus-collar kernel; the full divisor-chain variance identity retains every reflected-prime term. Theta-Doob crossing square plus secondary-profile collar difference square equals a nonnegative mixed collar integrand. Prime collar difference vanishes under midpoint symmetry, not for arbitrary even `w`; convex odd-polar interpolation is an identity, not a sign proof. All-scale `coshMultiplier` Nyquist coercivity accepts `wΛ(n)=(Λ(n)/log n)ϖ_n`, but retains an added node sum and does not pay the reflected-prime collar. The signed prime−polar packet has an exact Chebyshev-error gauge representation, not a bound on its required one-sided sign. E: B:VonMangoldtWeilExplicitFormulaAndChebyshevBridge; B:HyperbolicGaugeChebyshevEnergyRepresentation.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Purely off-diagonal deep-prime clocks approximate `sourceEnergy(q)` and every bounded operator quadratic form on a fixed `SourceGraph` vector, with diagonal norm cost arbitrarily small. Any resolvent eigenvector above `1/2` forces strictly negative off-diagonal clock interaction; nonnegative interaction on arbitrarily accurate low-diagonal approximants would force `sourceEnergy(q)≥0` and exclude that eigenvalue. Nonnegativity is a premise, not a theorem for actual primes. E: B:PrimeGraphClockOffDiagonalSynthesisAndStationaryEnergyProducer.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Balanced nodal graph of an eigenvector has a three-term nonnegative edge decomposition and exact five-term jump-versus-diagonal balance; its primitive, doubly orthogonal projection has an off-diagonal prime-clock approximation with vanishing diagonal cost. The Rayleigh bound retains stationary-resolvent and nodal-cross premises; no sign of the projected complete source energy follows. E: B:BalancedNodalStationaryAndPrimeShiftDecompositionProducer.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** `|thetaGroundPotential(x)|≤C/cosh(x/2)`; exact `thetaToVolume`/arch-diagonal collar domination confines negative Doob energy and gives a positive volume floor for approximate resolvent eigenvectors above `1/2`. Four-term nodal-jump excess or cofinal nonnegative off-diagonal prime-clock interaction excludes such eigenvalues only as an explicit premise. No unconditional nodal-cross or clock sign follows. E: B:CoshDecayGroundPotentialAndThetaVolumeCollarProducer.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Completed secondary-profile shift jump is nonnegative after adding the mixed origin-crossing collar; low-prime window remains signed. Nodal-lobe five-term bounds, dyadic physical trace defect, and factorial prime−polar contrast are exact auxiliary identities, not a bound on local diagonal potential or all odd energies. E: B:OddProfileCollarDominanceAndUnimodalPrimeWindowProducer; B:NodalLobeEnergyAndDyadicDilationDefectProducer.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Odd bound-state lobe jumps have explicit `cosh(R/2)` collar/far-field bounds. RH follows from a *premised* four-term positive-lobe jump excess over the theta ground potential (or its collar split); arbitrary-depth Bézout shell iso-state cancellation and Möbius-wave transport do not establish that inequality. E: T:CoshCollarNodalDefectBezoutShellAndMobiusWaveBridge; T:NodalDefectMarkovSubinvarianceAndCenteringPronicGramBridge.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** `stationaryResolvent` preserves nonnegative mass inputs and dominates the modulus of signed graph inputs. For `resolvent w=μw`, `0<μ≤1`, both lobes share an a.e.-nonnegative nodal defect; exact graph pairings bound squared nodal cross-pair by defect and absolute-graph energies. The literal antisymmetrized prime kernel defines a bounded odd self-adjoint drift; directed endpoint difference equals its quadratic form, and `(primeShift quadratic)²+(chiral drift quadratic)²≤(primeDiagonal quadratic)²`. Parity-definite states have zero total drift with opposite lobe drifts; pointwise majorants `w≥Λ` with one strict coefficient and convergent weighted kernels break the exact even/odd Euler–Poisson identities. These identities do not sign the nodal interaction or exclude sign-changing `μ>1/2` states. E: B:StationaryResolventMarkovPositivityAndNodalDefectProducer; B:EulerPoissonChiralDriftAndPrimeShiftRigidity.
- **Established [L]:** Quotient by `signedRadical` has nondegenerate signed form; descended full Weil radical is exactly the two-pole plane, and the polar-constrained quotient is nondegenerate. Nonzero ground class precludes strict coercivity on the unrestricted quotient. Xi/pole readouts separate quotient classes; cardinal-orbit projections are closed-range idempotents, not an eigenstate-exclusion theorem. Odd eigenvalues above `1/2` lie in `(1/2,1)`; RH iff the complete odd archimedean-plus-prime-chain Poincaré inequality holds. This is the existing CHMD receiver in quotient coordinates, not its arithmetic proof. E: R:CHMDSignedRadicalQuotient; R:CHMDQuotientWeilForm; R:CHMDQuotientSpectralCoordinates; T:QuotientSpectralGapAndOddChainCoercivity.
- **Established [L]:** Quotient reflection splits even/odd Weil forms; coercivity transports exactly on source images. Original source mass does not descend through the signed radical, and full quotient Weil energy cannot be the squared norm of an injective realization. Zero-indexed translated cardinal projections span each finite-dimensional `(1/2,1)` eigenspace; their closed span equals the primitive bound-state band, without assuming finitely many off-line zeros. Spanning identifies the RH obstruction; it does not exclude or sign it. E: R:CHMDQuotientParityAndCoercivity; R:CHMDProjectedCardinalSpanning; R:CHMDCardinalBoundBandIdentification.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Finite sieve profiles have an exact prime-chain `H↦H/p` renormalization; chain variance vanishes for `p>H`, and squarefree-supported profiles collapse above the first excited level. This spectral cutoff of finite sieve data does not bound the complete actual-prime-chain energy. E: T:PrimeChainSieveBridgeAndSpectralCutoff.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** For an inserted off-line radial profile `n^β` and its reflected `n^−β`, symmetrized finite and global von Mangoldt-weighted prime-chain variance exceeds the critical-line phase baseline by explicit nonnegative hyperbolic terms, strictly on nonempty prime cuts when `β≠0`; a valuation-clock step gives a positive quantitative lower bound. This is a zero-parameterized comparison, not exclusion of any actual off-line zero or positivity of the complete signed CHMD form. E: T:OffLineHyperbolicPrimeChainExcessAndCardinalRecurrence; T:GlobalPrimeChainHyperbolicCoercivityAndClockStepBound.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Higher prime-power shells factor the same off-line radial jump. On ground cuts `0<u<log 2`, symmetrized excess dominates `β²/2·Σ_{p∣N}(log p)³`; multi-shell/all-cut estimates and exact continuous crossing-density pullback preserve a nonnegative excess. Continuous compact-windowed off-line waves have admitted chain integrals and ground-step lower bounds for nonnegative windows. All are inserted-mode lower bounds, not source-derived upper constraints on actual zeros. E: T:MultiShellPrimePowerHyperbolicSumAndGroundCutCoercivity; T:AllCutPrimeChainCoercivityAndContinuousDensityBridge; T:CompactWindowedOfflineChainIntegralAndOriginCollarDuality.
- **Established [L]:** Every odd resolvent eigenvector above `1/2` obeys an exact nodal-modulus/cross-jump identity; RH iff each such eigenvector satisfies `sourceEnergy(absGraph)≥4·sourcePair(posGraph,negGraph)`. A primitive `1/2` Rayleigh ceiling plus a `oneL`-defect payment by the negative cross-jump implies RH, but both are premises on putative eigenvectors. E: T:OddEigenvectorNodalModulusBalanceAndFourthBandStepLaw.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Actual theta/Γ/pole terms isolate the arithmetic source: on admissible signed weighted-`ℓ¹` coefficients, integer-wave readouts on any unbounded integer set determine every coefficient; the first differing coefficient fixes eventual source order. The complete even/odd Euler–Poisson parity laws uniquely force actual von Mangoldt weights even among signed replacements. This source rigidity does not orient the actual Weil/odd-Doob energy. E: R:CHMDArithmeticThetaIsolation; R:CHMDArithmeticThetaOrder; R:CHMDSignedEulerPoissonRigidity; R:CHMDSignedParitySourceRigidity.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Odd eigenvector identity `sourceEnergy(absGraph q)−4 sourceEnergy(posGraph q)=((2μ−1)/μ)‖q.fst‖²` gives an RH-equivalent `absGraph≤4·posGraph` criterion for every nonzero odd resolvent eigenvector. Nonnegative positive-halfline odd tests dominate their even fold in compact prime-chain integrals; two-channel off-line waves have nonnegative odd-collar excess. For even compact `w` supported in `(−R,R)` (`a≥3R+δ+L`, `½ log 2≤δ+L`), a proved Schur ratio and support-disjointness pay every finite prime-collar sum over `log d+R≤a` by half-line jumps plus `supportArchBudget R δ L` times one absorbed archimedean integral, independent of prime cutoff or `a`. Midpoint reflection collapses `oddProfileCollarMixed` to a 1-point diagonal potential, giving a zero-archimedean-cost two-regime bound when paired with low-prime `primeCrossing (oddPolarWeight a w)` and a collective finite-window Chebyshev Stieltjes integration-by-parts transport law; global MVT `m_a(x)≤(2M/sinh(a/2))sinh(x/2)` bounds `phiDoobCoshMass(oddPolarWeight a w²)` by `coshSinhPolarRatio(a,M,R)` times the `2∫ m_a sinh(x/2)w²` potential without an origin cutoff, so `convex_doob_chain_sinh_interpolation` absorbs all prime collars and `phiDoobCoshMass` with net coefficient `θ≤1` whenever `coshSinhPolarRatio(a,M,R)≤θ`, while `θ*sourcePair(oddPolarVectorAt a,p)` remains on the right-hand side. E: T:OddEigenvectorHalfMassLobeAndStrictPrimeShiftClosure; T:OddPolarCollarParallelogramAndPrimeClockBridge; T:CompactWindowedOfflineChainIntegralAndOriginCollarDuality; T:OddProfileCollarToContinuumArchimedeanBridge; T:UnconditionalSchurRatioAndGlobalLowPrimeBudget; T:SupportRadiusCollarCutoffAndAllScaleObstacleBBridge; T:CollectiveLowPrimeChebyshevTransportAndSinhAbsorption.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Each integrable Klein–Gordon slice has zero positive-frequency temporal cosine atom; finite Möbius folds have only the pole atom at `√(7/4)` with explicit `e^(−v)` amplitude. The completed Möbius series equals the source prime/archimedean/pole wave; under RH its temporal coefficients equal positive zero-shadow masses. Unconditionally, every finite fold combination stays a positive uniform distance from the completed wave on `y≥0`; finite temporal data cannot replace the full arithmetic sum. E: R:CHMDTemporalCosineExtraction; R:CHMDKleinGordonTemporalSpectrum; R:CHMDFoldedTemporalSpectrum; R:CHMDTemporalCoefficientCalculus; R:CHMDFoldedPoleMode; R:CHMDShadowTemporalSpectrum; R:CHMDUniformFoldApproximationObstruction.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** The absolute Möbius-fold tail after cutoff `N≥1` is bounded by `C·foldRapidityEnvelope(y)·N^{-(π−3/2)}` at each rapidity; the envelope grows in `|y|`, so this is not uniform approximation on `y≥0` and gives no one-sided sign. E: R:CHMDLightConeFoldTailBound.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** On compact even multipliers of the odd-polar profile, complete Markov energy plus `θ·oddSinhPotential` plus exact nonnegative local defect equals nonnegative archimedean/same-side/reflected-chain jump energies plus reserve. For `0<a≤(log 2)/2`, every finite fixed `θ` fails to absorb cosh mass by sinh potential on some smooth compact even multiplier; derivative-majorant ratio `≤1` fails for support radius `R≥log 2`. - **Established [L], auxiliary:** Canonical smooth compact odd source cutoffs converge in graph norm to odd-polar vectors with energy `1/2`. Two-shift primitive differences are nonzero, have zero cosh/sinh moments and exact complete-chain identity, but their full energy tends to zero with `O((n+1)⁻²)` absolute bound. This excludes uniform strict coercivity relative to the original source-graph norm on the primitive compact class, not positivity or an eigenstate-exclusion estimate. E: T:CanonicalOddPrimitiveHorizonBounds.
- **Established [L], shared signed-gauge frontier:** ACT's quadratic folded-potential identity allows signed odd gauges and exact origin jump-moment cancellation. - **Established [L], source minors:** Stein-ratio certificates give strict first `2×2` normalized minor, raw cubic moment, Gaussian aperture balance at zero and central-cumulant discriminant; sixth-moment Hankel/three-temperature certificates, size-biased susceptibility/Pearson χ²/mean-lift windows and single-Gibbs relaxation bounds also hold. Eighth full-reflection curvature is positive on its stated short interval. Low-order/local signs, not all-degree source positivity. E: R:ThetaCuspSteinCertificates.
- **Established [S], source witnesses:** Cofinal theta likelihood convexity and slope-square taxes at zero are positive at some positive time; center compensation source has a positive transverse zero x with `x·source′(x)<−3·fullSource(x)²`. Local witnesses, not a global compensation sign. E: R:ThetaCusp/WitnessPremiseDischarges.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Native quartic theta Schur-mode charge is exactly the squared norm of a nonlinear two-coordinate feature. Its degree-four scaling forbids any linear norm-square receiver on the original degree-one source coordinates; feature positivity alone does not prove all-degree theta moment sign. E: R:ThetaQuarticHilbertReceiverBoundary.
- **Established [L]:** Moment and heat-current receivers, finite-rank sign regions, and exact theta-source identities. Fixed-rank or bounded-window positivity does not select the all-rank branch.
- **Open:** All-degree positivity at one positive heat time or equivalent same-time phase/current sign with full discrete-prime remainder.

Intrinsic one-channel projection has kernel equal to the Xi-multiplicity pencil range; exact confluent moments, all-power defects/solvers, BV repeated-resonance profiles, and complete signed arithmetic value are constructed. Literal causal theta renewal kernel is Hilbert–Schmidt and cone-positive; its positive rank-one feedback yields a compact cone-positive operator with global `det₂(zK)=exp(−dz)D(z)/D(0)` for the actual renewal denominator `D`. Every renewal resonance is an eigenvalue with matching algebraic multiplicity; cone positivity alone does not confine complex resonances. Theta-ratio reduced slope has positive physical value but negative conjugate value, norm and trace on a boundary strip; a strict coarse-cone interior path retains negative norm/trace with positive source error. First causal Xi classes vanish in the original source topology along vertical escape while native endpoint jets stay norm-bounded below; no continuous unregularized extension of finite native synthesis exists. Fixed Cauchy-pole quartet defect vanishes iff RH, with Krylov cyclicity and source-entropy Cauchy tomography proved on the reciprocal-square source; this is a zero-side/Cauchy-moment criterion, not a boundary source-current sign. No global source orientation follows.
- **Open:** Mixed prime+Gamma≥pole inequality with all cusp terms, global seam/cusp gluing, and arithmetic boundary-current orientation; a cone-positive compact Fredholm realization does not yield a real spectrum or the missing sign. Source-faithful native completion requires changed topology, regularization, or normalization, beyond fixed-Gaussian realization.

E: T:EvenFlipOfTheFermiSquarefreeWeightBreaksTheReceiver; T:FermiRowsAgreeInEveryTranslatedMomentAndSplitTheReceiver.
- **Established [L], positive-source classification:** For `c≥0`, `Σc(d)/d=m`, bounded centred pronic energy iff `Σ_k dyadicMass(c,k)²/2^k<∞`; equivalent to a uniform bound over finite dyadic rows and horizons. No cancellation theorem for signed Möbius rows. E: T:DyadicMassesGovernPositiveReceivers; T:HorizonLawOfPositiveRows.
- **Established [L], pole cancellation:** For nonnegative ε-damped positive-drift q, `0<ε≤1/2`, critical-Wiener `k⋆q` is unbounded unless `Σk_jj^(ε−1)=0`. For smooth powers below half, pole cancellation also pays all centred dyadic rows; half damping instead requires finite signed logarithmic partial-pole energy (MC). Finite kernels suffice for coprime-indicator/odd-squarefree powers and bounded nonnegative multiplicative `b(p)=1`. General positive-drift sufficiency and actual μ small-damping admission remain open. E: T:CriticalWienerKernelsRepairAPositiveDriftOnlyAtItsPole; T:CriticalWienerPerturbationsOfTheSmoothPowerHaveUniformlyPaidRows; T:TheHalfDampedReceiverIsTheLogarithmicEnergyOfThePartialPoleSums; T:FinitePerturbationsOfCoprimeWeightsAreDecidedAtThePole; T:FinitePerturbationsOfEveryPositiveWeightPretendingToOneAreDecidedAtThePole.
- **Established [L], inverse detection:** MC's oscillating-power probes are admitted exactly at ζ zeros; RH iff all sub-half probes are unbounded. This detects forbidden admission, unlike PM's actual-μ cofinal-admission criterion; neither supplies the other's source estimate. E: T:TheOscillatingSmoothPowerIsPaidExactlyAtTheZerosOfZeta.
- **Established [L], prime-one classification:** Bounded multiplicative `f(p)=1` has unbounded damped receiver for `0<ε≤1/2` if every local factor `L_p=Σ_e f(p^e)p^(−e)` is nonzero; in particular if `|f|≤1`. A vanishing factor with finite prime-power support suffices for all `ε>0`; with uniform finite prime-power degree, boundedness iff some `L_p=0`. No classification of the actual prime-minus-one Möbius source. E: T:MultiplicativeWeightsPretendingToOneArePaidOnlyByAVanishingLocalFactor; T:AVanishingFiniteLocalFactorPaysAWeightPretendingToOne.
- **Established [L], exact double-pole repair:** For finite `k` and `q_n=log n·n^(−ε)`, `0<ε≤1/2`, `ReceiverBounded(k⋆q)` iff `Σk_jj^(ε−1)=Σk_jlog j·j^(ε−1)=0`; sufficiency holds for all `ε>0`. Same criterion for bounded nonnegative multiplicative `b(n)q_n` with `b(p)=1` on primes. The first Fermi kernel repairs `n^(−ε)` but not logarithmic drift at `ε≤1/2`; its convolution square repairs both for every `ε>0`. Bounded undamped signed partial sums also give log-weighted damped receiver bounds. These source classes do not close actual Möbius energy. E: T:APoleOfOrderTwoNeedsAZeroOfOrderTwo; T:DoubleZeroKernelsAndLogarithmicSmoothPowerAtThePole.
- **Established [L], exact even-flip calibration:** For `0<ε≤1/2`, finite k vanishing on multiples of 4, `c=k⋆oddSqfWeightε` and its even flip share Ramanujan magnitudes; `Pε(k♭)=Pε(k)+(1−2^ε)Pε(evenPart k)` and `Pε(k)=Pε(oddPart k)+2^(ε−1)Pε(evenPart k)`. Both receivers bounded iff both odd/even pole values vanish. If total pole vanishes but even-part pole does not, the original is bounded, its flip unbounded, and some logarithmic pronic moment separates them. Equality of every logarithmic moment pins both weights on n≥2 and their boundedness. Two-point boundedness lines meet only at zero. This class-specific calibration does not bound the actual small-damping Möbius source. E: T:EvenFlipPairsOverTheOddSquarefreeWeightAreClassifiedAtThePole; T:TheEvenFlipShiftsThePoleValueByTheEvenPartOfTheKernel; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SmoothBernoulliPoleCalibration/EvenPoleCalibrationAndLogMomentPinning.lean.
- **Established [L], literal damped readout:** `Mε=firstPronicMoment(pmPowerDampedRealWeight ε)=Re(−ζ′(1+ε)/ζ(1+ε)²)>0`, `Mε→1` as `ε↓0`; the positive parity twin has readout `−Mε`. Under receiver boundedness, energy is `Mε²+variance` and dual residual `(1−Mε)²+variance`; unconditional for actual PM at `ε≥1/2`. Damping derivative equals minus the log-weighted centered prefix; log-power receiver bounds hold for `ε>1/2`. Small-damping variance/signed backward-work control remains open; integrable tangent control is a stronger sufficient target. E: T:DampedMoebiusFirstMomentAndChebyshevTangentBridge.
- **Established [L], receiver-preserving source changes:** Möbius↔Liouville at every `ε>0`; cofinal Liouville boundedness as `ε↓0` implies RH. Critical-Wiener convolution preserves boundedness; prime-sign flips preserve it when `Σ_{flipped p}p^(−1/2−ε)<∞`. Bounded multiplicative sources matching a nonprincipal real character at primes are bounded for all `ε>0`; prime values `−1` give half-damping boundedness; nonnegative sources with prime values `+1` diverge for `0<ε≤1/2`. These classes do not prove the actual small-damping source bound. E: T:MoebiusAndLiouvilleReceiversAreEquivalent; T:CriticalWienerConvolutionsPreserveTheReceiver; T:LocalEulerFactorsAndPronicDilationContraction; T:SparsePrimeFlipsPreserveTheReceiver; T:ReceiverOfMultiplicativeWeightsIsDecidedAtThePrimes; T:PretentiousMultiplicativeWeightsAndTheReceiverTrichotomy.
- **Established [L], phase recovery:** First pronic moment is `−Σ_eΛ(e)W_c(e)`; it orders a positive twin strictly exactly when a negative prime-power coefficient occurs. Negated Möbius and its positive twin match all prime-power coefficients and first moment, but their first logarithmic moments separate. All logarithmic moments determine a damped source at every `n≥2` and determine receiver boundedness; some logarithmic moment separates every receiver-distinct positive twin. Cubic translated moments determine χ₄ rows within Selberg sign twists; squarefree PM rows only up to the even flip. That flip preserves boundedness when its even-part receiver is uniformly bounded; the Fermi pair refutes automatic transfer. No actual PM bound follows. E: T:FirstPronicMomentIsTheVonMangoldtPairing; T:FirstPronicMomentOrdersPositivePairs; T:FirstMomentBlindParityPairOfTheNegatedMoebiusWeight; T:SecondVonMangoldtMomentSeparatesTheBlindPair; T:LogarithmicMomentsAreACompleteReceiverInvariant; T:LogarithmicMomentHierarchySeparatesEveryPositivePair; T:CubicMomentsPinTheParityOfOddRows; T:CubicMomentsPinSquarefreeRowsUpToTheEvenFlip; T:EvenFlipIsTheFermiTransformOfTheEvenPart.
- **Established [L], FZZ-P scope:** Fixed-`ε` cofactor rows have horizon-uniform variance/reserve ceilings tending to zero with `ε`; literal-μ variance/reserve retain positive cofinal floors, and damped prefix energy stays below literal-μ energy on each fixed trans-horizon band. For the actual PM cofactor row, normalized renormalized defect at horizon `(B+1)Q` tends to `−(B+1)R(ε)` [L]; `R(ε)/ε→1/2` [L]. Raw complete sieve pronic energy tends to `1/2` for literal μ and commutes with fixed-damping cofinal/small-damping limits [L]. Neither is centred prefix/BD energy or a complete PM/Haar bound. E: T:ReceiverRowParsevalSpectrumAndHorizonUniformVariance; T:ReceiverRowLinearDissipationRate; T:DampedPrefixEnergyLinearityAndBandNonCommutation; T:PronicEnergyCommutesWithDamping; T:HalfDampingDissipationLaw.
- **Established [L], fixed source receiver:** For the actual power-damped Möbius cofactor row, the Báez–Duarte residual squared equals `(1−energyMean(ε,Q))²+energyVariance(ε,Q)`; making this arbitrarily small along some `ε>0,Q` implies RH. Every such residual has an unconditional `κ/log(Q+1)` floor. Neither a small-residual sequence nor global Haar approximation is proved. E: V:BaezDuarteEnergyOfThePowerDampedMobiusRows.
- **Established [L], receiver calibration:** Fixed-damping cofactor energy mean converges to `−ζ′(1+ε)/ζ(1+ε)²`, tending to `1` as `ε↓0`. Complete-period divisor variance has `V(ε)=ε/2−ε²+o(ε²)`, dissipation `R(ε)=ε/2+o(ε²)` and lag-one covariance `−ε²+o(ε²)`. These limits do not bound cofactor centred variance jointly as cutoff grows. E: V:EnergyMeanOfThePowerDampedSourceTendsToOne; T:DampedVarianceAndDissipationSecondOrderLaw.
- **Established [L], fixed-lag structure:** Damped cofactor covariance, normalized by squared density, converges jointly as damping vanishes and cutoff grows to the Hardy–Littlewood singular series minus `1`; odd-lag autocorrelation is `−ε²+o(ε²)`. Normalized Barban–Vehov period covariance has the same fixed-lag singular-series limit; odd-lag covariance tends to `−1`, while zero-lag variance diverges, so nonzero-lag covariance/variance tends to `0`. Exact damped singular-series Euler product and lag-summed prefix discrepancy retain all modes. Fixed-lag whitening does not control cofinal complete centred energy. E: V:HardyLittlewoodSingularSeriesOfTheDampedSource; V:ExactDampedSingularSeriesAndGallagherPrefixDiscrepancy; T:SelbergRowPeriodCorrelationsSingularSeries.
- **Established [L], cutoff geometry:** For sufficiently large literal-μ sieve cutoff `N`, renormalized defect is strictly negative at every `M≥2N`; at fixed `ε>0` and `N≥2`, it is nonpositive beyond a quadratic horizon. Damped prime-step slope changes sign across the exact scale `Λ(ε)=log(1/(2ε))/ε+γ+o(1)`; energy steps have opposite signs at `log p≤Λ(ε)−ε` and `log p≥Λ(ε)+ε` for sufficiently large primes and prefix horizons. Defect/step signs do not bound complete centred pronic energy or Haar error. E: T:QuadraticLongWindowThreshold; T:DampedCrossoverEulerConstantAndAdditiveWindow.
- **Established [L], FZZ-P auxiliary:** Fixed-`ε>0` damped response has a convergent complete-period variance; its `ε↓0` limit is zero, while literal Möbius period variance has a positive cofinal floor. Fixed-damping convergence cannot be passed to the undamped cofinal row or substituted for the complete small-damping pronic/PM bound. E: T:DampedPeriodVarianceAndNonCommutingLimits.
- **Established [L], FZZ-P auxiliary:** For each fixed damping `ε>0`, the actual PM cofactor row has nonpositive renormalized defect at every prefix once cutoff `Q` is large. Its divisor-response truncation has explicit `Q^(−ε)` error on polynomial horizons. This settles the cofactor-row defect sign, not the positive reserve, complete pronic/PM variance, or Haar approximation. E: T:PMCofactorRowGlobalDefectSign; T:PMCofactorRowRamanujanTruncation.
- **Established [L]:** **(FZZ-P, Live):** For `φ_a(t)={1/(at)}−2{1/(2at)}` on `(0,1]`, approximation of the dyadic Haar target `h=−1_{(0,1/2]}+1_{(1/2,1]}` in `L²(0,1]` is exactly RH-equivalent, with measurable `L²`-witness self-similarity discharged via unconditional dilation averaging; exact Fourier `L log L` reconstruction retains all cross-terms. A witness whose Mellin transform analytically continues to the known zero-free-region mirror is dyadically self-similar; the continuation is a premise, not established for every witness. **(PW-B / Hawking–BM, Candidate — zero-indexed by `q : XiZeroCopy` and missing receiver landing):** Weak retarded realization, compact-cutoff two-response contact identity, complementary-rate wedge-exhaustion limit, and absolutely admitted native-corrected action for each zero copy `q`. Original reflected principal kernel is uniformly bounded for physical exponents; all three principal response channels have polynomial growth. Full original cusp forcing and unchanged forced response have absolutely admitted Laplace integrals and weighted time-`L²` admission for every positive damping `c>0`, matching the literal causal frequency response. Eisenstein value at `i` factors as `2Γℝ(2s)ζ(s)L(s,χ₋₄)` with Hecke Hermitian resonance identity proved at every strip zero of `Λ`; because zeros can come from either L-factor, this value alone does not isolate ζ.
- **Established [L], FZZ-P:** Every `L²` witness orthogonal to the alternating generators is determined by its germ at `t=0`: vanishing near `0` forces global vanishing. The generators are dense on each `(δ,1]`, `δ>0`; the Haar target admits arbitrarily accurate finite approximants there. Their Mellin factors share a unimodular dyadic factor on the critical line. Local density does not supply a compatible global approximant.
- **Established [L], FZZ-P:** For an `L²` witness `w`, dyadic self-similarity iff `m_w(x)=∫₀ˣ|w|²≤Cx` on `(0,1]`. RH iff every `L²` witness has this linear origin mass (equivalently bounded `2^k m_w(2^(−k))`); RH also iff every continuous witness has zero top-octave log-mean `∫_(1/2)^1 w(t)dt/t`. Off-line-zero power witnesses violate the bounds; these are receivers, not source proofs.
- **Established [L], FZZ-P:** Dyadic shell folding gives `RH ⇔` every fold-free `L²(0,1]` function is approximable by finite Fermi–Nyman generator sums; Haar is fold-free. Finite Haar error splits exactly into fold-free residual plus `½|Σ c_j/a_j|²`; alternating Franel Gram entries have an exact gcd formula. Witness tail/head pairing and Cauchy–Schwarz bounds separate origin mass from bulk error; the log-Möbius head is bounded by centered Chebyshev-error squares. None produces global approximation or small-`t` tail control. E: B:FoldFreeKernelGramAndFranelGcdCancellation; B:WitnessGermOriginMassAndJointTailHeadSynthesis.
- **Established [L], FZZ-P:** For finite natural-scale Fermi combinations, origin-tail Haar error is at most `2(1+9‖c‖₁²)/(N+1)`; witness pairing splits into this coefficient-dependent origin cost and bulk approximation error. The log-Möbius Chebyshev head has a closed upper bound `O(N/log²N)`, not a vanishing error. Local completeness plus these bounds does not construct global Haar approximation. E: B:LocalToGlobalTailStabilityAndWitnessIndicatorPairing; B:ChebyshevHeadAndShearCellProducer.
- **Established [L], FZZ-P:** Dyadic form is half lower-half energy minus half top-octave energy; RH iff every Fermi–Nyman witness has top-octave energy ≤ lower-half energy. Reciprocal-cell Fermi combinators give exact Kronecker cells; finite LCM Gram has nonnegative Selberg diagonalization. The power-damped Fermi source is unconditionally `L²` with analytic Mellin transform for damping `ε≥1/2`; for all `ε>0`, Ramanujan weights have total norm `1` and bounded energy mean, but `0<ε<1/2` source admission still assumes a uniform centered-sawtooth/shifted variance bound. E: B:MultiOctaveStepBrickPartitionAndWitnessDyadicPositivity; B:RoutePMVarianceToWitnessSynthesisAndCriticalStripBridge; B:CenteredRamanujanVarianceAndAbelCofactorCancellation.
- **Established [L], FZZ-P:** Finite signed LCM mean square is exactly Selberg diagonal minus fractional-part defect; Möbius fibres `L≤N` equal `μ(L)`, with excess on boundary divisors `>N`. Selberg and periodic Franel forms cancel their `e=1` harmonic-shadow square in their difference; `ker P` has a sharpened Cauchy lower bound. Endpoint centering kills first-moment drift while preserving bulk coefficients `k<N`, including the Barban–Vehov `Λ` head; remaining fractional defect has only fixed-cutoff `ℓ¹` control. Normalized diagonal has logarithmic scale, not cofinal critical-strip variance or Haar approximation. E: B:SignedLcmFractionalDefectAndCompleteFibreCollapse; B:BarbanVehovLogarithmicDiagonalAndKerPCauchyPinch; B:KerPCenteredSieveAndFirstSecondMomentReciprocity.
- **Established [L], FZZ-P:** For `ε>0`, Fermi-source `L²` admission is equivalent to centered-source admission and to finite weighted divisor-remainder energy. Cofinal uniform centered-sawtooth or mean-centered Gram variance implies RH and yields cleared Mellin zero translation; the cofinal variance is a premise, not a proved bound. Dyadic brick pairing equals a causal-response chain input; an exact top-octave witness integral transfer gives a lower-half-energy times combinator-tail bound, not top-octave energy domination. E: B:SawtoothCharacterExpansionAndCriticalStripPoleBridge; B:FermiNymanBrickDictionary; B:WitnessCellTransferAndTopOctaveTailInequality.
- **Established [L], FZZ-P auxiliary:** On reciprocal cells `N+1≤n≤2N`, the actual Möbius combinator equals `−(1+Σ_{k=N+1}^n μ(k))` and has cell energy `(1+Σ μ)^2/[n(n+1)]`; its jump products coincide with BVI successor correlation. No global origin-tail estimate follows. E: V:FermiCellJumpReciprocityAndPronicTailBridge.
- **Established [L], FZZ-P auxiliary:** BVI cell scaling extends to all integer dilations and gives exact second-octave `μ(m)−1_{2∣m}μ(m/2)` jump switching. Power-damped Möbius divisor coefficients are the literal BVI sieve response, their adjacent product is the pronic coefficient, and finite discrete energy has an exact pronic Abel decomposition. A cofinal uniform Abel budget implies RH only as an unproved premise. Repaired Haar and Fermi prefixes agree on sub-cutoff cells; the first tail cell at even cutoff equals `−μ(2M+1)`. The centered sawtooth gradient is sieve response minus harmonic mode; on `ker P` its Dirichlet energy is the same-node sieve energy. These are exact bridges, not a global Haar approximation or critical-strip variance bound. E: V:DilatedCellScalingAndSecondOctaveFermiJumpBridge; V:PowerDampedPronicMultiplicativityAndRoutePMBridge; V:EndpointHaarCellDecompositionAndWitnessPronicTransfer; V:SawtoothBezoutSuccessorGradientAndRouteFZZPBridge.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Abstract producer–consumer projection and a correlation-window residual inequality connect blind-test polar charge to producer approximation error; the window `L²` consumer bound uses integer-log zeros. No identification or approximation theorem for the FZZ-P arithmetic generators follows.
- **Established [L], FZZ-P auxiliary:** BVI decomposes the literal power-damped divisor energy into diagonal squares plus twice a predictable signed martingale sum; a uniform bound on that *complete* sum at arbitrarily small positive damping implies RH. For `ε≥1/2`, its normalized martingale drift has the exact limit `−variance(ε)/2`; this endpoint law and shifted-exponent local prime-deficit summability do not provide the small-damping uniform bound. E: V:PredictableMartingaleDecompositionAndCovarianceCancellationBridge; V:ActiveDeletedMartingaleAndShiftedEulerConvergence.
- **Established [L], FZZ-P auxiliary:** Horizon-free energy splits exactly into active-deleted diagonal plus inactive predictable martingale, then predecessor-peak plus doubly-inactive Bézout interior. Shifted-exponent product has exact Euler/Cesàro density; its small-damping rate is `1`, active-sawtooth density rate `1/2`, and active predictable log drift exists for every `ε>0`. Inactive log drift is proved only under Fermi `L²` admission (unconditionally `ε≥1/2`); each RH cancellation theorem assumes a uniform bound on the *complete* small-damping energy. Pronic/gradient identities and three-step `6`-divisibility ceilings do not supply that bound. E: V:ShiftedExponentAsymptoticDensityAndActiveMartingaleRate; V:HorizonFreeActiveDeletedEnergyAndShiftedCovariance; V:ShiftedExponentSmallDampingCovarianceRateAndActiveDrift; V:BezoutPronicInteriorSawtoothAndPredecessorPeakSplit; V:BezoutPronicTotientInteriorMartingaleSynthesis; V:PostStepInactivePrefixAndSixDivisibilityPronicDefect; V:PostStepInactiveEnergyAndPronicGradientConservation.
- **Established [L], FZZ-P auxiliary:** Active-pair plus predecessor-peak martingale has an exact dissipation decomposition and upper bound `1/8 + 1/2·(uncentered shifted-exponent difference sum)` for `ε>0`; both terms are unbounded at sufficiently small fixed damping, so this upper bound is not a uniform payment. E: V:ActivePairMartingaleDissipationAndShiftedTotientReduction; V:SummationByPartsActivePairAndUncenteredShiftedReduction; R:PMActivePairLogarithmicDrift.
- **Established [L], FZZ-P auxiliary:** Adjacent power-damped divisor correlation has an Euler-product Cesàro density. Equal-exponent density is `o(ε)`; shifted `(ε,1+ε)` density has first-order coefficient `pmAdjacentArtinConstant>0`. Active-pair plus predecessor-peak sum has exact logarithmic drift; its small-damping slope is `1/4−pmAdjacentArtinConstant/2≥1/24`, hence the uncentered active pair and shifted-difference sum are unbounded for sufficiently small fixed `ε>0`. Complete-energy identity isolates interior counterterm `variance/2+activePairLogDrift`, with small-damping slope `(1−pmAdjacentArtinConstant)/2∈[7/24,1/2)`. E: R:PMAdjacentDivisorCorrelation; R:PMAdjacentDivisorEuler; R:PMAdjacentDivisorSmallDamping; R:PMActivePairLogarithmicDrift.
- **Established [L], FZZ-P auxiliary:** Doubly-inactive Bézout interior product reduces exactly to a boundary difference, nonpositive interior-gradient square and local active×interior impulse product. Complete energy splits into diagonal, active-pair and interior brackets after arbitrary logarithmic drift subtraction; uniform upper bounds on all three brackets at arbitrarily small damping imply RH, but are unproved. E: V:InteriorMartingaleQuadraticGradientAndRenormalizedDriftBudget; V:MultiLagBezoutLayerHierarchyAndFourStepInteriorCalculus.
- **Established [L], FZZ-P auxiliary:** Active-pair pronic energy is bounded by `1/4`; complete power-damped discrete energy and pure Bézout-interior pronic energy bound each other up to factor `2` and additive `1/2`. A uniform bound on the latter at arbitrarily small positive damping implies RH, but is not proved. Exact compensated harmonic/predecessor-peak formulas isolate logarithmic drift; second Bézout shell decomposes the centred divisor prefix into first/second layer and a four-step impulse. E: V:PronicPotentialSelfCancellationAndInteriorGradientConservation; V:CompensatedHarmonicPredecessorPeakDriftAndDiagonalBudget; V:ArbitraryDepthBezoutShellsAndRoutePMSecondLayerProfile.
- **Established [L], FZZ-P source band:** Actual discrete and pure-interior pronic energies have horizon-uniform bounds for every `ε≥1/2`, including the endpoint. One budget also works simultaneously for large `T` and `1/4≤ε≤1/2` with `ε≥1/2−1/(80√log T)`. Uniform centered-prefix bound: `|Aε(n)|≤Kn^(1−ε)e^(−√log n/40)`. This shrinking band supplies no horizon-uniform estimate at a fixed `ε<1/2`. E: R:PMPowerDampedMobiusDiscreteEnergy; V:PronicPotentialSelfCancellationAndInteriorGradientConservation; V:ReceiverBoundedInTheZeroFreeBandBelowHalfDamping.
- **Established [L], wider FZZ-P band:** `V(T)=(log T)^(3/5)(log log T)^(−5/3)`; some `κ>0` gives the same uniform energy budgets when `1/4≤ε≤1/2` and `ε≥1/2−κV(T)/(480log T)`. Still shrinks to `1/2`, not the required `ε↓0` control. E: V:ReceiverBoundedInTheVinogradovKorobovBand.
- **Established [L], actual-source parity separation:** Actual PM source and its positive parity twin have identical Ramanujan magnitudes. On the same Vinogradov–Korobov band, actual energy is uniformly bounded while twin energy is `≥c(log(T+1)−log 100)`, `c>0` independent of `T,ε`. Magnitudes do not certify the actual small-damping bound. E: V:ParityPairSeparatedOnTheVinogradovKorobovBand.
- **Established [L], fixed-sub-half growth:** At each fixed `1/4≤ε<1/2`, actual PM energy is eventually `≤Cε T^(1−2ε)e^(−κV(T)/270)` and `≤Cε e^(−κV(T)/270)` times its complete-period primitive energy. Relative saving, not horizon-uniform boundedness; no `ε↓0` estimate. E: V:ReceiverGrowthBelowHalfDampingAgainstThePeriodEnergy.
- **Established [L], prime-sign classification on the band:** For multiplicative `|f|≤1`, `f(p)=−1` at every prime, the receiver of `f(n)n^(−ε)` is bounded by one `E_f` for every `T`, `1/4≤ε≤1/2`, `ε≥1/2−κV(T)/(480log T)`. For multiplicative `0≤f≤1`, `f(p)=+1`, energy is `≥[log(T+1)−log 100]/1024` for every `T`, `0<ε≤1/2`. Higher prime-power values do not change this bounded/divergent split; no small-damping bound for actual μ. E: V:MultiplicativeWeightsAreDecidedAtThePrimesOnTheBand.
- **Established [L], FZZ-P auxiliary:** For each `0<ε<1/2`, active-boundary correction between pure-interior and actual centred-source discrete pronic energy is absolutely summable, hence total energies differ by `O_ε(1)`; neither is thereby bounded. Five-piece Vaughan expansion keeps all 25 signed Gram entries, while the available logarithmic energy majorant is nonsummable. E: V:CompletePronicVaughanCalibration.
- **Established [L], FZZ-P auxiliary:** For actual power-damped cofactor weights, BVI energy-centered, mean-centered and raw pronic Gram forms identify existing PM Ramanujan variance/energy receivers. Each uniform-in-`Q` Gram bound at fixed `ε>0` implies Fermi `L²` admission and Mellin analyticity on `Re s>1/2`; no such small-`ε` bound is proved. E: V:MeanCenteredPronicGramRecurrenceAndCenteringDiscrepancyBridge; V:RoutePMCofactorSieveBridgeAndUnifiedEnergyEquivalence.
- **Established [L], FZZ-P auxiliary:** Interior jump orbit is conditionally centered only for coprime or fully shared moduli; proper shared factors leave an explicit positive mean. Prime-packet energy has unavoidable coherent prefix mass/log-moment cost before its nonnegative centered tail. Finite compensated bounds do not pay the complete small-damping variance. E: V:InteriorJumpOrbitCenteringAndCompensatedBounds; V:PrimePacketConditionalTailAndCoherentPrefix.
- **Established [L], FZZ-P auxiliary:** All-depth Euler–Dirichlet factorization cancels the finite local singularity and identifies a hypercube PM Gram with a dilation-profile quadratic form; no uniform cofinal bound is obtained. E: V:CrossDepthEulerOperatorFactorizationAndPrimePairGramIdentity; V:AllDepthHilbertDilationCubeAndHyperCubeGramFactorization.
- **Established [L], FZZ-P auxiliary:** At every natural cutoff `Q`, complete power-damped Ramanujan variance equals finite head energy plus forced conditional-head-mean square plus nonnegative centered tail variance. Multi-depth prime-cofactor packets before the first prime multiple retain an explicit linear/log head. Birth-delayed interior centering gives a modulus-uniform *pair* compensated bound only after initial-value and mean drifts are kept; summing coefficient-weighted pairs and paying the full head/mean remain open. E: V:NaturalCutoffMultiDepthCofactorAndConditionalTailBridge; V:BirthDelayedInteriorJumpCenteringAndUniformPairBounds.
- **Established [L], FZZ-P auxiliary:** At each fixed `ε>0`, active-deleted diagonal, predecessor peak, single-divisor and adjacent-divisor fields have horizon-uniform `O_ε(1)` remainders after their exact harmonic counterterms; the renormalized interior bracket equals actual power-damped discrete energy up to `O_ε(1)` with coefficient one. Strict interior contraction is equivalent to bounded actual energy at that `ε`, not proved by this identity; no bound uniform as `ε↓0` follows. E: V:UniformActiveDeletedCompensatedRemainder; V:UniformAdjacentDivisorCompensatedRemainder; V:UniformRenormalizedInteriorEnergyRemainder.
- **Established [L], FZZ-P auxiliary:** Odd/four-divisible Fermi boundary jumps recover the complete sieve sequence exactly iff four-divisible coefficients vanish; on that class selected reciprocal-cell energy `Q` satisfies `Q≤P≤4Q` for paired sieve energy `P`, with sharp constants. For arbitrary coefficients, the selected physical trace is injective but its optimal inverse cost grows as `(4/π²)(log₂N)²`, even on nonnegative coefficients. Exact coefficient recovery and conditioning do not bound the cofinal Haar error. E: T:FermiValuationSamplingAndPronicObservability; T:FermiSelectedCoefficientTransportAndRecovery; T:FermiCompleteTraceValuationStability; T:FermiPhysicalOptimalConstants.
- **Established [L], FZZ-P auxiliary:** On dyadic-antichain coefficient packets supported over allowed odd primes `Π`, horizon-uniform selected-trace inverse `P≤CQ` (also restricted to nonnegative packets) holds iff `Σ_{p∈Π}1/p<∞`; divergent capacity gives nonnegative packets with `P≥(L−1)/2`, `0<Q<5/6` for every `L≥2`. This classifies a restricted packet family, not literal PM coefficients or global Haar approximation. E: T:FermiDyadicAntichainPrimeCapacityClassification; T:FermiDivergentCapacityAntichainPackets.
- **Open:** **(FZZ-P):** Unconditional `L²(0,1]` Haar approximation, or arithmetic proof of top-octave domination/linear origin-mass bound for every witness. Need actual-energy bound/cofinal *log-renormalized complete* pronic budget at arbitrarily small `ε>0` (budget may depend on ε), paying positive active-pair drift, conditional-head mean and interior orbit means; equivalently a horizon-uniform signed damping-integral budget from admitted c to cofinally small a. Scalar work is paid; physical cutoff-shell summability follows from admission [L]. Hard obstacle: actual signed covariance/dilation cancellation, retaining both linear, quadratic and polar terms, controlling horizon profiles and any drifting-origin tail cost, preventing an interior damping-energy escape; absolute majorants fail at/below half damping. Compactness on c₀ and the ordered zero-damping work limit do not pay critical pronic energy, boundary flux/forcing or limit interchange. Absolute transition-row control fails below half damping; signed reciprocal-source control survives. The positive linear residual floor forbids an o(ε) uniform residual ceiling, not cofinal admission. Local `(δ,1]` density does not control `δ↓0`. **(PW-B):** Pre-divisor source formulation (eliminating `XiZeroCopy` indexing) and small-damping `c→0⁺` complete-energy ceiling; PW-B source-contact action must still imply optical magnitude, ordered cap, and all-divisor extinction.
- **E:** R:FermiNymanBeurlingHaarTarget; R:FermiNymanHaarApproximationEquivalence; R:FermiNymanWitnessContinuationRigidity; R:PaperFermiFourierCriterion; R:EisensteinHeckeFactorizationAtI; R:EisensteinHeckeResonanceAllZeros; R:ThetaCuspHawkingBMPWRetardedWeakNative; R:ThetaCuspHawkingBMPWTwoResponseAction; R:ThetaCuspHawkingBMPWTwoResponseActionLimit; R:ThetaCuspHawkingBMPWNativeTwoResponseAction; R:ThetaCuspHawkingBMPrincipalReflectionAdmission; R:ThetaCuspHawkingBMPrincipalSmallDamping; R:ThetaCuspHawkingBMForcedSmallDamping.

<a id="jas"></a>
### JAS — Joint ancestry

- **Path:** Arithmetic prime-product Weil form → level-one positivity on sufficiently large-prime tests → RH; or square-shell arithmetic Riesz sums → finite-power-window subpower bound → RH.
- **Established [L]:** Level-one full-product/dyadic constant-vector positivity, cosine-kernel positivity on large primes, harmonic Euler Weil positivity on large primes, PSD at infinity at every level `σ>1`, and torus-density nonnegativity are all RH-equivalent without quasi-RH or no-chain side conditions. Full signed L² torus density and finite-coordinate conditional expectations exist; typical phases and small prime budgets are positive unconditionally.
- **Established [L]:** RH iff literal arithmetic Riesz sums on infinitely many square prime shells satisfy subpower bounds for every exponent on the finite power window `m<k≤m+m/log(m+1)`; failure of RH violates a power in this window on every sufficiently large shell. Loud-mode rank is paid by actual shell half-mass and reciprocal Weil weights. Synchronized Gamma–theta coefficients over all finite prime supports, arbitrary complex formation parameter, independent phases and every actual Mellin grade define one nonzero `L²` source; squarefree Gaussian domination and prime-reciprocal bounds give simultaneous admission. E: H:WeightedPowerWindowCompression; H:WeightedPowerWindowAsymptotics; B:PrimeShellMellinMassBudget; R:JointAncestrySquareShellFinitePowerCriterion; B:SquarefreeGaussianCompletion; R:GammaThetaSupportWeightBounds; R:GammaThetaFullSupportSource.
- **Established [L]:** Forward creation covariance on the completed Gamma–theta source and strong finite-prime cutoff exhaustion; neither gives inverse/preimage orientation or source sign. E: R:GammaThetaFullSupportCreation.
- **Established [L], scoped:** Native prime-product form is nonnegative for every complex vector on finite prime sets above a cutoff with budget `Σ_P p^{-(σ−1/2)}≤((log y)/(128·10⁸))^(3/2)/(log log y)^4` for large `y`; cutoff/budget remain coupled. This exceeds every fixed `(log y)^A`, `A<3/2`, but not the level-one RH receiver's `y^(1/2)` budget. E: R:JointAncestryEndpointBudgetProducer; R:JointAncestryBudgetZeroFreeLaw.
- **Open:** Source sign at the level-one power budget on sufficiently many prime shells, all-amplitude damped-shell positivity, or subpower square-shell Riesz bounds on the finite power window. Logarithmic-budget positivity and Gamma–theta admission do not pay the power-budget gap.
- **E:** R:JointAncestryLevelOneCapstones; R:JointAncestryLevelOneDensityCriterion; R:JointAncestryDampedShellCriterion.

<a id="ctf"></a>
### CTF — Chronological tail and Báez–Duarte approximation

- **Path:** Bounded chronological Bose/Möbius rows (or finite Báez–Duarte dilates) → vanishing L² error → RH.
- **Established [L], source-chain boundary:** Centred damping chain's first column lies in receiver `ℓ²` iff its damping is admitted; no norm-bounded backward continuation follows from residue-mode completeness. This restates the required source-vector control, not vanishing Báez–Duarte residual. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SignedArithmeticEnergyIncidence/TheFirstColumnOfTheChainIsTheReceiver.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SignedArithmeticEnergyIncidence/ResidueModesAndBackwardContinuation.lean.
- **Established [L], structure:** The discrete Báez–Duarte Gram at reciprocal-integer arguments has an exact finite Vasyunin-sum formula, including diagonal and cross-tail formulas. This computes finite approximation geometry; it does not show `d_N→0` or bounded chronological-row approximation. E: V:VasyuninFormulaForTheDiscreteBaezDuarteGram.
- **Established [L], alternate receiver:** For every finite real row `c`, squared Báez–Duarte residual equals `Σ_{M≥1}(1+sievePrefixDiscrepancy(N,M,c))²/[M(M+1)]`. It splits exactly into `(1−Λ-readout)²+energy-centred pronic Gram`; RH iff finite rows make this sum arbitrarily small, equivalently the readout²/energy ratio is unbounded. Every row at level `N≥2` has residual `≥κ/log N` and satisfies `κ·readout²≤(log N−κ)·energy`. These are receiver geometry and a floor, not qualifying arithmetic rows. E: V:PronicBaezDuarteCriterion; V:VonMangoldtReadoutFormOfTheBaezDuarteCriterion; V:ReadoutEnergyUncertaintyPrinciple.
- **Established [L], Möbius-row floor:** The `Λ` readout equals `Σ_{d≤N}c(d)log d/d`. For truncated literal μ this tends to `−1`; its centred pronic energy is eventually `≥κ/(4log N)` and the signed-row Báez–Duarte residual is `≥κ/log N`. This lower floor neither proves nor excludes vanishing residual. E: V:LogHarmonicReadoutAndTheMobiusRowEnergyFloor.
- **Established [L]:** Exact two-sided Báez–Duarte equivalence (`d_N → 0 ⇔ RH`), bounded-row chronological equivalence (`‖r_a‖_{L²(0,∞)} → 0` with `|a_k|≤1` ⇔ RH), and dual sampling equivalence; unbounded rows are dense. Sharp coefficient-budget dichotomy: if RH fails, every coefficient budget `exp(ε^(−θ))` with `θ≤2` fails at some accuracy. Bounded vanishing-error rows force Möbius coefficients; off-line zeros force sharp-row escape.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Finite Nyman floor error admits an exact sieved-divisor sum and truncated LCM Gram mean-square; Selberg totient diagonalization bounds the origin tail by a smooth positive Gram plus an `ℓ¹` square minus the exact bulk head. E: B:SelbergSieveTailQuadraticFormAndTruncatedDivisorGram.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Exact signed fractional-part defect replaces that `ℓ¹`-square relaxation in finite prefix, interval and origin-tail mean squares; Möbius complete LCM fibres collapse to `μ(L)`. The signed boundary defect still needs a uniform cofinal bound to make the error vanish. E: B:SignedLcmFractionalDefectAndCompleteFibreCollapse.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Barban–Vehov sieve coefficients equal `Λ` on the prime-power head; periodic Franel minus Selberg diagonal cancels the harmonic `e=1` mode exactly. Endpoint `ker P` projection preserves that head and deletes first-moment linear drift; cumulative sums remain bounded by the coefficient `ℓ¹` norm at fixed cutoff. Normalized Selberg diagonal is `Θ(1/log X)`, but signed LCM defect and origin-tail square remain uncontrolled for vanishing chronological error. E: B:BarbanVehovLogarithmicDiagonalAndKerPCauchyPinch; B:KerPCenteredSieveAndFirstSecondMomentReciprocity.
- **Established [L], Selberg-row period law:** Normalized Barban–Vehov period covariance at each fixed nonzero lag tends to the Hardy–Littlewood pair singular series minus `1`; odd lags tend to `−1`. Zero-lag variance diverges, so correlation divided by variance tends to `0` at every fixed nonzero lag. This is not vanishing chronological error or control of all lags jointly. E: T:SelbergRowPeriodCorrelationsSingularSeries.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Any row retaining literal Möbius coefficients through `⌊N/2⌋` has Selberg diagonal `≥1/2359296` for `N≥2`; the actual `growingMomentSource N` therefore cannot have vanishing Selberg diagonal. The sharp universal coefficient-mass/Selberg-energy constant is `Σ_{e≤N} P(e)²/φ(e)`, attained by Liouville-signed inverse coordinates. These facts exclude diagonal-only decay for this source, not signed-defect cancellation or other bounded rows. E: R:ChronologicalSelbergCoefficientMass; R:ChronologicalSelbergSharpMass; R:Sieve/TotientCubicMean.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Weighted bivariate Selberg convolution factors through finite Möbius-weighted harmonic sums. For the existing logarithmic-taper row, `Q_Sel≤K/(log N−log M)` when the log span is at least `1`; a squareful tail mask has harmonic charge `≥1/16` and Selberg cost `≤9(1+log N)`. The taper result does not bound its signed LCM defect/chronological error; the mask does not erase retained literal-μ diagonal mass. E: R:Sieve/SelbergWeightedConvolutionModel; R:ChronologicalSelbergTaperEnergy; R:ChronologicalSquarefulChargeCorrection.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Explicit log-Möbius total-error bound `(1+3N)²/(N+1)+(log 4+4)²N/log²N` is unconditional but does not tend to zero. Fold-free error equals total error minus half squared harmonic shadow; specialized Selberg-tail bounds retain `N²` or squared floor-summatory remainders. No bounded row with vanishing global error is constructed. E: B:DyadicRenewalStirlingAndLogMobiusTotalEnergyProducer; B:FoldFreeLogMobiusAndPlancherelCausalProducer.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Fermi tail Gram equals global Gram minus exact finite reciprocal-cell head; single-cell combinator tail energy has the same exact subtraction. Báez–Duarte discrete distance is bounded by an explicit Gram quadratic; reciprocal Gram certificates imply RH, but no such cofinal arithmetic certificates are produced. E: B:VasyuninReciprocityAndExactTailGramProducer.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** For any finite sieve row, successor correlation is `M Q_Bez` plus signed Bézout sawtooth defect; on `ker P`, finite mean square is bounded by additive Dirichlet energy plus signed LCM and Bézout defects and an endpoint square. The only uniform Bézout-remainder bound is `(Σ|c(d)|)²`; no bounded row with vanishing global error follows. E: V:BezoutSelbergDiagonalCoercivityAndHalfDecorrelation; V:ChineseRemainderBezoutPeriodicityAndSawtoothDefect.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** The actual Möbius Fermi-combinator first-octave cell error has exact pronic-weighted energy `(1+Σ_{k=N+1}^n μ(k))²/[n(n+1)]`. This identifies a signed tail target but supplies no cofinal decay of chronological or Báez–Duarte error. E: V:FermiCellJumpReciprocityAndPronicTailBridge.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Uniform bounds `|response−1|≤Cδ(n+1)^(1/2+δ)` for every `δ>0`, all `1≤N<n≤2N`, are equivalent to RH through the proved Mertens equivalence. This supplies an exact first-octave cancellation receiver; no such arithmetic bound or vanishing global chronological error is proved. E: V:PowerSavingIncrementCriterion; R:MobiusMertensLittlewood.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Repaired Haar/Fermi prefixes and BVI responses agree on every sub-cutoff reciprocal cell; exact pronic cell energies, first-tail-cell `−μ(2M+1)` and centred-sawtooth gradient/Dirichlet laws expose the finite chronological error. A cofinal power-damped pronic Abel budget implies RH, but is not proved. E: V:PowerDampedPronicMultiplicativityAndRoutePMBridge; V:EndpointHaarCellDecompositionAndWitnessPronicTransfer; V:SawtoothBezoutSuccessorGradientAndRouteFZZPBridge.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** BVI sub-horizon nonpositivity extends to arbitrarily large *rough checkpoints* `M=r−1` (including primes `r>N`), for arbitrary weights; it does not hold as a uniform trans-horizon bound. Optimal successor compression loses `3/4` to all above-parity variance; true translations recover modes only on valuation-clock lags. Selberg-damped Möbius rows have bounded coefficients and pointwise μ limits, but no global error decay. The positive Burnol mass lower-bounds `liminf d_N² log N` and raw μ rows fail to converge; neither rules out `d_N→0`. E: T:TransHorizonRoughAbsorptionAndReset; V:SharpSuccessorPhaseLossAndClosureClassification; V:ArithmeticLagSchedulesAndSharpRecoveryHorizon; R:SelbergMobiusApproximation; R:BaezDuarteClassicalBaseline.
- **Open:** Construct bounded rows with vanishing chronological error (or prove `d_N → 0`), or prove the dual sampling bound from arithmetic data. Literal `−μ_N` aligns the readout [L]; its centred pronic energy lacks the needed vanishing bound.
- **E:** R:ChronologicalRiemannEquivalence; R:BaezDuarteCriterionHardDirection; R:BaezDuarteDistance; R:ChronologicalDualSamplingCriterion; R:ChronologicalMobiusEscape.

<a id="hcf"></a>
### HCF — Gaussian heat trace / critical feature variance

- **Path:** Exact arch−prime heat trace → one-sided vertical growth or all-degree positivity on two prime-log source grids after polar cancellation → RH.
- **Established [L], centered trace:** Multiplicity-exact Gaussian heat trace centered at any ordinate is summable, with time-one and later exterior-tail bounds polynomial in the center; Gaussian explicit-formula prerequisites (zero summability, Xi growth, digamma identity, divisor separation) are discharged. Fixed positive theta-tail constants also discharge quadratic, log-linear and exponent-4/3 upper-theta Mellin/completed-ζ₀ growth bounds. Bounds do not give one-sided vertical sign or all-degree Hankel positivity. E: R:XiCenteredHeatBounds; R:RiemannGaussian/GaussianPremiseDischarges; R:RiemannGaussian/ThetaTailConstantDischarges.
- **Established [L], cross-route receiver:** RH iff `EventualFilteredCentralGapSubexponential` and `AsymptoticAbscissaLaw`; the first excludes high off-line persistence, the second excludes an escaping off-line chain. Neither condition is established from the arithmetic source. E: R:CrossRouteReceiverBridges.
- **Established [L]:** Seven-way heat-trace capstone at any fixed heat time `a>0` (vertical boundedness, vanishing vertical Cesàro mean, right half-plane zero-freeness, vertical heat Gram PSD, heat Hankel PSD, arch−prime Hankel PSD on the pole hyperplane `P(−1/4)=0`, and positivity of all Hankel determinants `det(M_{i+j}(a))`); fixed-rank positivity is unconditional and vertical growth is bounded at Vinogradov–Korobov strength. Singular Hankel blocks have exact negative-index law (counting off-line zero quadruples), Toda contact orders, escaping root branches, regular-head transfer, and signed kernel projections at every positive heat time, without genericity assumptions. On the critical-feature side, compact-source boundary alias and phase variance obey an unconditional phase-uniform budget controlled by the Ξ phase-histogram entropy deficit.
- **Established [L]:** Complete Hankel positivity on two heat-value grids with spacings `log p`, `log r` for distinct primes, after arbitrary finite starting offsets, is RH-equivalent; `(2,3)` suffices. The literal archimedean-minus-prime source differs from each sampled heat sequence by one geometric polar atom; on the polynomial hyperplane vanishing at that atom, the square forms coincide, so positivity of both literal source grids is also RH-equivalent. A nonreal conjugate packet inside the natural spectral parabola can mimic a positive atom on one complete grid and on every grid in any finite family of offsets sharing its step. These are abstract aliases, not actual Xi counterexamples.
- **Established [L]:** Two full marginal heat grids do not reconstruct arbitrary conjugation-closed four-atom packets; mixed two-step moments determine summable packets under distinct paired coordinates and escape hypotheses. These are information laws, not arithmetic positivity.
- **Open:** Source-derived positivity of both literal prime–archimedean grid forms at all polynomial degrees on their polar hyperplanes, or one-sided prime-chirp/vertical bound, cofinal Ξ histogram entropy-deficit bound, or both `EventualFilteredCentralGapSubexponential` and `AsymptoticAbscissaLaw`. Fixed-rank signs, one grid and finitely many same-step offsets do not close the all-height problem.
- **E:** R:XiGaussianHeatRouteCapstone; R:XiGaussianHeatBlockInertia; R:XiGaussianHeatTodaMultiplicity; R:XiGaussianHeatRegularKernelProjections; R:XiGaussianHeatTwoPrimeSampling; R:XiGaussianHeatTwoPrimeSourceSampling; R:HeatGridAliasingBoundary; R:HeatGridFiniteOffsetAliasing; R:HeatGridMarginalAmbiguity; R:HeatMixedMomentDeterminacy; R:PaperCriticalFeatureEntropyVariance; R:PaperCriticalFeatureXiHistogram.

Actual-Xi margin satisfies ΔQ=4(r²−|z−c|²)|ξ″(z)|²≥0 inside each disc; boundary positivity alone does not force interior positivity. Entire scalar damping `A(Ξ)` makes the Newton map globally holomorphic iff `A=0`; each fixed disc has a polynomial pole repair preserving reflection and first root jets, but not self-mapping.

### BAG — Deterministic zeta self-return

- **Path:** Fixed-base ζ strong recurrence on all compact right-strip sets, local recurrence at every strip point, or cutoff-uniform positive Haar mass of target-ζ head twists → RH.
- **Established [L], disc tails:** For `0<r<R`, `1/2<Re c−R`, `Re c+R<1`, both Dirichlet and Euler tails are uniformly small on `closedBall c r` off a height set of arbitrarily small upper density, for every sufficiently large cutoff. Finite covers remove the doubled-disc restriction. Finite-head almost periods alone give typical translated-base returns; a head zero with boundary margin still forces pole-shifted returns to density zero and head returns into the tail-bad set up to density zero. E: R:ZetaDiscRecurrenceOnEveryClosedDiscOfTheStrip; R:ZetaTypicalHeightPairRecurrenceAndHeadZeroObstruction.
- **Established [L], typical-orbit scope:** Isolated finite-head zeros with boundary margin are tail-bad up to a density-zero set; their upper density tends to zero as head length grows. For all head almost periods in a bounded shift window, ζ pair-return failures at translated base heights have one uniform small-density bound; such shifts are relatively dense. Typical-height ζ-disc sup norms have a power-law bad-height bound, and the translated orbit is precompact outside arbitrarily small-density exceptional sets. None is return to a prescribed base disc; a finite head zero still obstructs automatic pole-shifted overlap. E: R:ZetaPartialSumIsolatedZeroHeightsAndUniformPairReturns; R:ZetaTypicalHeightDiscSupTailLawAndOrbitPrecompactness.
- **Established [L], vertical spectrum:** For `1/2<σ<1`, ζ on `σ+it` has Bohr–Fourier coefficient `n^(−σ)` at `log n`, zero at other real frequencies, and mean `1`; it is Besicovitch mean-square almost periodic. These averaged identities do not give fixed-base-disc return or conditioned tail overlap. E: R:ZetaStripBohrFourierCoefficients; R:ZetaStripBesicovitchAlmostPeriodicity.
- **Established [L], finite Euler phase law:** Prime logarithms are rationally independent; finite phased Euler products on compact `K⊂{Re s>0}` obey a Kronecker–Weyl/Haar limit for continuous functionals. Any approximating phase target gives positive-density finite-product visits. E: R:FiniteEulerProductKroneckerWeylLimitLaw.
- **Established [L], torus phase laws:** Haar prime phases are orthogonal; finite Hilbert-valued prime sums have exact square mean `Σ‖u_p‖²`, Chebyshev tail bound, and independent events on disjoint prime-coordinate blocks. These are free-phase Haar facts, not a fixed-base ζ return on arbitrary right-strip discs. E: R:PrimeTorusPhaseSecondMomentAndBlockIndependence.
- **Established [L], prime-vector phase approximation:** Under `1/2≤Re z₀`, `2ρ−2Re z₀<−1`, `ρ<1−Re z₀`, square-summability and divergent nonzero projections give finite unimodular prime sums approximating any Hardy-disc target, with error `<ε(1−|q|²)^(−1/2)` on `|q|<1`; any fixed finite initial phase assignment can be preserved. Free-phase input to general nonvanishing-target universality; the target need not be ζ. E: R:FinitePecherskyUnimodularApproximation; R:PecherskyDensityPrimeVectorsHardyDisc.
- **Established [L], finite Euler target:** On Hardy discs satisfying `1/2≤Re z₀`, `2ρ−2Re z₀<−1`, `ρ<1−Re z₀`, phased finite Euler products approximate `exp L` uniformly on every smaller closed disc for each `L∈H²`; the higher prime-power logarithmic tail is controlled. E: R:AnalyticOnLargerDiscLiesInHardyDisc; R:PhasedEulerProductsApproximateExponentialsOfHardyFunctions.
- **Established [L], finite Euler target transfer:** Kronecker–Weyl converts phase approximation into positive-density physical heights for arbitrarily large Euler cutoffs. Actual ζ is an admissible target on a larger zero-free disc `R₂>1` with `Re z₀+ρR₂<1`. E: R:FiniteEulerProductUniversalityOnDiscs.

- **Established [L], cutoff-uniform target density:** On smaller closed discs inside a larger zero-free ζ disc, under the stated Hardy-disc margins and `Re z₀+ρR₂<1`, one positive lower-density constant works for Euler-target returns at **every sufficiently large cutoff**. Disjoint prime-block independence and square-mean control retain this uniform constant. E: R:UniformEulerTargetDensityOnZeroFreeDiscs.
- **Established [L], same-height recurrence:** If ζ is zero-free on `B(c,R)` with `0<r<R`, `1/2<Re c−R`, `Re c+R<1`, cutoff-uniform Euler-target density minus the small-density Euler-tail bad set gives strong ζ recurrence on `closedBall c r`. Local Abel-pole/head-target transfer is closed under this zero-free premise; no doubled-radius restriction. E: R:ZetaDiscRecurrenceOnEveryClosedDiscOfTheStrip; R:ZetaDiscRecurrenceOnZeroFreeDiscs.
- **Established [L], cap-free prime-log divergence:** Nonzero entire `Φ` with `|Φ(z)|≤Ce^(ρ|z|)`, `ρ,σ≥0`, `ρ<1−σ` has unbounded `Σ_(p<N)p^(−σ)|Φ(log p)|`; no `5/12` cap. Imported Hardy/Pechersky-to-Euler chain uses the midpoint theorem `ρ<5/12`, automatically satisfied by its strip margins `ρ<Re z₀−1/2`, `ρ<1−Re z₀`; no extra radius cap in that chain. - **Established [L], joint prime span:** Finite global exponential type plus negative-ray growth exponent `b<1−σ` gives divergent weighted prime-log sums. For finite Hardy-disc tuples with `Re zᵢ+ρᵢ≤b<1`, closed prime span equals closed span of common polynomial tuples, equivalently exponential tuples. Restrictions of one common analytic function belong; arbitrary independent component targets require this membership. Prescribed initial phases extend to unimodular prime approximants. - **Established [L], joint Euler targets:** Under common strip/Hardy enlargement margins, one prime-phase family approximates `exp Lᵢ` simultaneously on all smaller discs for every tuple `L` in the joint prime span, with arbitrarily large cutoff. Full higher-prime-power logarithmic tail retained. E: R:PhasedEulerProductsApproximateExponentialsOnDiscUnions.
- **Established [L], joint cutoff-uniform density:** Under strip/Hardy enlargement margins, `L` in joint prime span gives positive-density physical Euler approximation of `exp L` on the finite disc union, with one positive density constant for every sufficiently large cutoff. Finite unions of Euler-tail bad sets have same-height control. E: R:UniformEulerTargetDensityOnDiscUnions; R:ZetaStrongRecurrenceOnCompactSets.
- **Established [L], compact-set criterion:** RH iff ζ is strongly recurrent on every compact `K⊂{1/2<Re s<1}`; no connected-complement restriction. - **Established [L], compact-target universality:** Positive-density ζ approximation on arbitrary compact strip `K` for targets analytic/nonvanishing on one containing ball. If `Kᶜ` is connected, polynomial Runge approximation gives targets `exp F` with `F` analytic on an open neighborhood, or nonvanishing analytic targets on a simply connected open neighborhood. Distinct finite strip points admit simultaneous arbitrary nonzero target values. Interior-only analyticity/continuous-boundary universality is not asserted. E: H:PolynomialRungeOnConnectedComplement; R:ZetaVoroninUniversalityOnCompactSets; R:ZetaVoroninUniversalityOnConnectedComplementCompacts; R:ZetaJointValueDensenessAtFinitelyManyPoints.
- **Established [L], fixed-base boundary:** On each right-strip disc, strong ζ recurrence is equivalent to positive-density head approximation of the *base ζ disc*, uniform across all sufficiently large head cutoffs; this condition on every disc implies RH. For large heads with the stated Abel pole, head self-returns and head-to-ζ-target returns are disjoint. A nonempty head-target set has positive density only after enlarging tolerance at each fixed head, not uniformly in cutoff. Targets with an isolated zero and positive boundary margin have density-zero ζ-approximation heights. E: R:ZetaDiscRecurrenceIffUniformHeadTargetDensity; R:ZetaTypicalHeightApproximationExcludesZeroTargets.
- **Constraint [L]:** Unadjusted centered tails retain Abel base-point pole `M^(1−Re z)`; mean square diverges and return densities vanish as `M→∞`.
