E: R:BagchiCarlsonCenteredTailBridge.
- **Established [L], local universality:** Every `z₀` with `1/2<Re z₀<1` admits `r>0` such that every analytic nonvanishing target on `B(z₀,4r)` is uniformly approximated by `ζ(·+iτ)` on the closed radius-`r` disc at positive lower density, for every tolerance. No zero-free premise on ζ. Targets with an interior zero and positive boundary margin have density-zero approximation at sufficiently small tolerance. E: R:ZetaVoroninUniversalityOnSmallDiscs.
- **Established [L], exact recurrence locus:** A right-strip point has a strongly recurrent closed neighborhood iff `ζ(z₀)≠0`; equivalently it has cutoff-uniform Dirichlet-head target density. RH iff every such point qualifies. Exceptional heights on each fixed vertical line are exactly actual zero heights: countable, measure zero. Known zero-free regions admit explicit recurrent radii. E: R:ZetaRecurrenceAtEveryPointModuloItsZeros; R:ZetaDiscRecurrenceOnZeroFreeDiscs.
- **Established [L], recurrence modulo zeros:** At every right-strip point, `ζ=∏_(u∈S)(z−u)^D(u)·g` on some closed disc, finite actual zero divisor `S`, positive multiplicities, nonvanishing analytic `g`. ζ-shifts approximate `g` at positive lower density. Dirichlet heads also approximate `g`: for each tolerance one positive density constant works for every sufficiently large cutoff (height threshold may depend on cutoff). At a possible zero, `S` contains it; this is cofactor return, not ζ self-return. E: R:ZetaRecurrenceAtEveryPointModuloItsZeros (`exists_uniform_pos_density_headTarget_cofactor`).
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Continuous almost-periodic functions have positive lower density of near-returns. E: H:AlmostPeriodicReturnDensity.
- **Established [L]:** Finite Dirichlet polynomials have positive-density uniform self-returns on compact sets. Exact head/tail identity requires centered ζ-tail control on those same return heights. Arbitrary-order Euler–Maclaurin truncation and finite Dirichlet mean squares yield Carlson's exact *unconditioned* vertical `L²` tail mean for each `1/2<σ<1`, tending to zero with head length; this does not supply conditioned tail control or uniform disc recurrence. E: R:FiniteDirichletPolynomialSelfReturn; R:BagchiConditionedTailGap; R:ComplexEulerMaclaurin; R:IntervalMeanSquareTransfer; R:ZetaEulerMaclaurinArbitraryOrder; R:ZetaFiniteMeanSquare; R:ZetaSuperlinearTruncation; R:ZetaSuperlinearCutoff; R:ZetaDirichletBandMeanSquare; R:CarlsonZetaMeanSquare.
- **Established [L], return threshold:** For `D=closedBall(c,r)` in the right strip, `d=inf{‖ζ−g‖_D : g analytic/nonvanishing near D}` satisfies `d=0` iff ζ has no zero in the **open** disc; boundary zeros allowed. For `ε<d`, return measure on `[0,T]` is eventually `≤T^θ` for some `θ<1`. If the doubled closed disc lies in the strip, `ε>d` gives positive lower density, and strong recurrence iff `d=0`; no claim at `ε=d`. Pointwise `ζ(c)≠0` iff some doubled-strip disc has, for every `ε>0, θ<1`, return measure `>T^θ` at arbitrarily large heights. E: R:ZetaSelfReturnThresholdIsTheDistanceToNonvanishingTargets.
- **Established [L], target threshold/multiplicity:** For a nonidentically-zero target analytic near a disc, distance `d_h` to nonvanishing analytic targets is zero iff the open disc is zero-free. With doubled-strip margins, `ε>d_h` gives positive-density ζ approximation; `ε<d_h` gives power-saving rarity; no equality-case claim. At a possible ζ zero of order `m`, `ζ=(z−z₀)^m g`, small radii satisfy `|g(z₀)|r^m/4≤d_ζ≤4·3^m|g(z₀)|r^m`. No off-line zero is constructed. E: R:ZetaOrbitApproachesEveryAnalyticTargetWithTheHurwitzThreshold; R:ZetaSelfReturnThresholdReadsTheVanishingOrder.
- **Established [L], Haar-mass criterion:** On doubled-strip discs, ζ zero-free on the open disc ⇒ each tolerance has a positive lower bound for Haar mass of multiplicative-prime twists whose length-`M` Dirichlet head approximates ζ, uniform for all large `M`. This law on every such disc iff RH. For `ε<d_ζ`, mass is `O_(disc,ε)((M+1)^(1−2σ))→0`, `σ=Re c−2r>1/2`; explicit coefficient-tail bound retained. Fixed-head positive mass/existence of phases is insufficient. E: R:ZetaTwistedDirichletHeadsNearAZeroHaveVanishingHaarMass; R:ZetaTwistedDirichletHeadsApproximateZetaWithPositiveHaarMassIffZeroFree.
- **Established [L], finite head-conditioned rough decoupling:** Finite smooth-head/rough-tail products are injective. Weighted vertical mean-square differs from its diagonal by at most `4N(1+log N)Σ|w|²Σ|α|²`; normalized mean tends to rough coefficient mass. Prime-return kernel `∏p(2+2cos(tlog p))` is exactly the squarefree-head modulus square, giving weighted bad-height/good-mass bounds. Fixed finite polynomials only; no sharp return-event/infinite ζ-tail or cutoff-uniform recurrence bound. E: R:HeadConditionedRoughDecoupling.
- **Open / hard obstacle:** Source-derived fixed-base recurrence on a neighborhood of every right-strip point; equivalently cutoff-uniform positive Haar mass for target-ζ head twists on every doubled-strip disc, or returns exceeding every `T^θ`, `θ<1`, arbitrarily often at every tolerance. Need exclude actual-zero exceptional points, where small-tolerance returns and Haar mass decay. Compact-target universality, Runge approximation and cofactor returns do not remove target zeros.
- **E:** R:ZetaStrongRecurrenceReceiver; R:ZetaWeakRecurrenceLadderCollapse; R:PaperZetaReturnSpectralCriterion.

<a id="ewu"></a>
### EWU — Even pole-weight uniqueness

- **Path:** Actual even blind minimum nonnegative at every aperture → even positivity → λ=Λ → RH.
- **Established [L], parity index:** This parity sector of actual ζ has maximal compact-test negative index equal to the number of distinct right-half-plane zeros with positive imaginary part (HF); classification, not a source sign. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCastelnuovoSeveri/TheReflectionSectorsOfTheHodgeDefect.lean.
- **Established [L], even-fold boundary:** Even fold of a nonnegative even translation kernel has only nonpositive overlaps; for this finite model, PSD iff local payment. No arithmetic complete-form positivity follows: actual polar correction, low modes and all-aperture transfer remain. Odd prime folds can be frustrated (OWU/AIP). E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SignedArithmeticEnergyIncidence/OddFoldingIsTheSourceOfFrustration.lean.
- **Established [L], blindness versus atom payment:** Node `4` is atom-dominated but redundant in the dyadic blind grid; removing it changes the exact second cosine coefficient, while adjoining it to the ternary grid gives strict slack gain. Node `6` has zero Λ atom and positive polar collar mass, yet gives strict dyadic and ternary blindness gains, including zero-pole combinations. Blind-symbol improvement is not a complete prime−polar payment theorem. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCriticalityTheory/SignChangingTwoMomentAndAtomPoleIntegralLaw.lean.
- **Established [L], two-tower blind enclosure:** For integer towers `q₁,q₂≥2`, continuous even line certificates bound archimedean energy on their blind cone; product spectral designs bound every certificate above by `T(q₁,q₂)`. Valid slack `−m(q₁)−η(q₁,q₂)` and necessary floor `−T(q₁,q₂)` enclose the optimum; `η(2,3)>0`. No exact two-tower optimum or all-aperture even blind sign. E: B:TwoTowerBlindSlackEnclosure.
- **Established [L], scoped:** Endpoint-collared critical factorial sources have a uniform `CΣb²` prime bound and positive Lévy-minus-prime form on every nonzero sufficiently high dyadic-block pattern. Their *defined* complete collared energy, including Lévy, Gamma, both polar moments and prime channel, obeys `E_c≥(s_c(log M−c_c)−K)Σb_n²` and is positive above one absolute scale, without a support-cardinality cap. The collar changes the family; no density or sign transfer to arbitrary even-polar/prime-blind or RH-complete factorial-grid tests is proved. E: A:DyadicDilationCorrelationInequality; A:CollaredCriticalPrimeChannelUniformBound; A:CollaredCriticalArchimedeanLogCoercivity; A:CollaredCriticalCompleteFormPositivity.
- **Established [L], uncollared high scale:** On every dyadic block the original critical factorial prime, Gamma and polar channels cost `KΣb_n²`; its *defined uncollared* complete critical energy is `≥[s(log M−c₀)−K]Σb_n²` and strictly positive on every nonzero pattern above one absolute scale, with no support-cardinality cap. This is restricted to high-scale factorial patterns; it does not imply all-aperture even-polar positivity or RH. E: A:CriticalPrimeChannelUniformBound; A:CriticalCompleteFormHighScalePositivity.
- **Established [L], polynomial scale windows:** Original critical factorial prime channel on `[M,LM]` costs `O(3+log L)Σb_n²` without a support-cardinality cap. The *defined complete* critical energy is positive for every nonzero finite pattern on `[M,LM]` once `M≥M₀` and `1≤L≤M^δ` with explicit `δ=s/[4(W+1)]>0`, `s=criticalArchLogSlope`, `W=criticalWindowPrimeSlope`. This is not positivity on arbitrary even-polar tests, all scales or an RH-complete cone. Dyadic, collared, compact-truncated and lacunary thresholds are named `max(2,⌊exp(c+C/s)⌋+1)` in their respective constants; polynomial-window threshold is `max(2,⌊exp(2K/s)⌋+1)`, `K=sc+K₀+6W`. E: A:ExplicitBlockThresholdsOfTheCriticalFamily; A:CriticalPrimeChannelScaleWindows; A:CriticalCompleteFormPolynomialWindows.
- **Established [L], separated blocks:** For finite dilation families with cosine transforms bounded by `A` and `B/f²` uniformly in dilation, critical factorial cross-block bilinear prime energy is bounded in both dilation orders by `K(A,B)(1+log n)√(1/n+m√(m/n))‖b‖₂‖c‖₂` for blocks at `m≤n`; the harmonic constant is summable. This conditional cross-block estimate is not an all-scale complete-form sign or a density transfer to arbitrary tests. E: A:CriticalPrimeChannelCrossBlockDecay.
- **Established [L], lacunary unions:** For any finite number of doubly collared critical dyadic blocks with `M₀≥M_*` and `Mᵢ₊₁≥Mᵢ⁸`, the full prime channel has one support-size-independent `ℓ²` bound. The complete energy, hence the literal compact full Weil energy, is strictly positive on every nonzero real pattern for every truncation `V≥V₀`; the lower coefficient is `s_T(log M₀−c_T)−K_lac`. This covers sparse high-scale unions, not arbitrary even-polar tests or an RH-complete dense cone. E: A:LacunaryCriticalBlockPositivity.
- **Established [L], compact critical source:** Compact doubly collared critical sources have defined Gamma–Lévy–polar–prime energy `≥[s_T(log M−c_T)−K_T]Σb_n²`, uniformly in far truncation `V≥V₀` on each high dyadic block. For continuous Lipschitz compact critically filtered sources, this energy equals the literal full Weil functional; on sufficiently high dyadic blocks every nonzero real finite pattern has strictly positive literal full Weil energy at every `V≥V₀`, with unbounded support length `log(2V)`. No density/all-scale transfer to arbitrary even-polar tests is proved. E: A:DoublyCollaredCriticalSourcePositivity; A:CompactCriticalSourceWeilPositivity.
- **Established [L], compact polynomial windows:** For doubly collared tests with scales in `[M,LM]`, full prime energy is bounded by `C(3+log L)Σb²`, uniformly in `V>0`. For some `δ>0`, sufficiently large `M`, `1≤L≤M^δ`, and every `V≥V₀`, literal complete Weil energy equals ACT's full zero form and is `≥(s_T/4)log M·Σb²>0` on nonzero patterns. Fine smoothings retain actual-ζ square mass minus reflection defect `≥(s_T/8)log M·Σb²`; smoothing threshold may depend on the source and `V`. No density/all-scale transfer to arbitrary tests follows. E: A:CompactCriticalPolynomialWindowAndTemperednessBridge.
- **Established [L], low/high coupling:** On every finite support, including mode `0`, Gamma+polar is `≥−K_GPΣb²`. Disjoint low set below `LM` plus high window `[M,LM]` has `|P|≤2C(3+log(LM))²E_low+C(4+log L)E_high`, without a gap. Low window `[1,L₀]` plus lacunary high blocks with `L₀⁸≤M₀` has `|P|≤[C(3+log L₀)+650K]E_low+[K_lac+100K]E_high`, uniformly in `L₀`, block count and truncation; dyadic slicing gives scale-independent cross-window cost. Complete-form bound remains `A_high E_high−K_low E_low`, not all-coefficient positivity. E: A:MultiScaleLowModeAndGridTentSchurBridge.
- **Established [L], grid-tent transfer:** Spatial translation preserves complete Weil/full zero form. Each compact critical source converges in full energy under its envelope-rescaled grid-tent approximation; a positive source margin transfers to sufficiently fine tents and smooth detectors in all five form realizations. High polynomial windows and arbitrary-depth high lacunary families supply unconditional positive examples. Approximation thresholds depend on the source; no positivity for arbitrary grid patterns or low-mode unions follows. E: A:MultiScaleLowModeAndGridTentSchurBridge.
- **Established [L], coefficient-size boundary:** With `ε(L)=1/(2+log L)`, `ΣΛ(n)n^(−1−ε)≤6(3+log L)` pays the window majorant. For shifted-pair weights `Λ(n)(n^a+n^(−a))`, that series diverges whenever `ε≤|a|`. This separates ζ's admitted window estimate from the shifted-ζ countermodel, not positive/negative full Weil directions for ζ. E: A:CompactCriticalPolynomialWindowAndTemperednessBridge.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Even compact sources admit even polar-neutral distant-copy approximants preserving normalized autocorrelation on a prescribed window with arbitrarily close normalized archimedean energy; polar-neutral and unrestricted even finite-grid lower bounds coincide. This preserves the finite grid, not the complete prime term. E: B:EvenSourceSymmetricPolarEnforcement.
- **Established [L], scoped:** Complete translated critical factorial-prime limit is bounded below by any finite harmonic truncation containing `m=2`; `m=3,5` masses dominate its negative `m=2` term under an explicit conditional positivity criterion. Physical cumulative kernel is nonnegative for `u≥5`, bounded below by `−(log 2)/4` times its nonnegative weight globally. This restricted prime channel is not the complete even-polar Weil form. E: A:FactorialCriticalPrimeHarmonicTruncationAndThreeCellKernel.
- **Established [L]:** Even positivity at any finite pole weight forces λ=Λ and RH; even-polar and full-positive weight cones coincide. Single-source purification, exact fibres and blind minimizer attainment hold at every aperture. Blind minimum is antitone in aperture, at least `1/20` for `a≤(log 2+1/50)/2`, and nonnegative at every aperture iff RH. No real prime-weight sequence repairs the even cone at pole weight `β<1` without the polar constraint. Constant archimedean shifts preserve blind minimizers and subtracted ground residuals while changing the minimum's sign.
- **Established [L]:** On prime-blind even-polar tests, the pole vanishes and Euler–Maclaurin conservation is exact; every nonzero smooth test with finite spectral second moment obeys `S/L>27`. Clamped first-cell bounds localize the tail without a first-cell `S` charge; single-source purification preserves mixture energy and the conservation law, but only bounds it below by `evenBlindMinimum(a)‖u‖²`.
- **Established [L]:** For real even-polar tests the archimedean form is exactly its constant term plus a nonnegative Lévy–sinh increment integral. Prime-2 blindness yields dyadic deficit bounds and, on smooth tests, a strictly positive weighted folded-collar integral bound without a spectral-second-moment premise. No fixed positive deficit at every interior point follows even for smooth real even-polar, prime-2-blind tests: separated pulse trains give arbitrarily small deficit at `4 log 2/5`.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Finite integer-log grids have strictly positive integrated archimedean collar coefficient with one shared-origin charge, overlap-safe allocation, and explicit cross-coset Gram errors. Distant-copy polar enforcement keeps local normalized correlations exactly and normalized archimedean energy asymptotically; imposing the polar moment alone does not improve the best archimedean-only grid constant.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Cancelling any finite list of real exponential moments and allowing finite real vector-source rank leave the best finite-grid normalized archimedean lower bound unchanged. Clique-sphere duality, coupled-profile Gram gain with explicit errors, two-collar Schur–Krein coupling, secant-plus-dual Lévy splitting, and dyadic-clique square-root window bounds are exact; physical partitions transport collar mass and fibre duality quantifies gain. Hyperbolic-multiplier parity/intertwining and multiplicative–additive shear identities do not establish the full source sign.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Feasible finite spectral mixtures admit smooth compact polar scalar grid realizations arbitrarily close to spectral cost; any valid grid lower bound is no larger than each such cost. Admissible physical collar partitions have an exact nonnegative dual-gap identity, and every finite general/selected-grid coefficient attains its optimum, including finite prime-power grids. E: B:PhysicalCollarHilbertAttainment; B:SelectedCollarHilbertAttainment; B:PrimePowerGridCollarAttainment.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Integer-log zeros on a dyadic window yield derivative-free `L²` correlation control; producer–consumer residual still needs a separate producer bound. Schur-capped collars improve finite-grid energy certificates; signed Gram optimal coefficients have monotone cutoff limits bounded by actual source Lévy energy. Cap gain is positive exactly where activation index exceeds `1`; no automatic strict gain. All-grid correlation compactness needs uniform signed-grid tails. Energy convergence additionally needs uniform origin control: smooth sources with fixed short support, exact grid blindness and strong `L²` convergence can lose positive Lévy energy at the origin. Polar-balanced sources may converge uniformly in correlation and energy to a nonpolar limit; signed-Gram trials have exact dual gap plus cutoff error but bound only archimedean energy. E: B:SchurCappedCollarHierarchy; B:SignedGramCutoffHierarchy; B:SignedGridTraceCompactness; B:InfiniteGridStrictEnergyLoss; B:DistantCopySharpness; B:SignedGramTrialCertificate.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Signed-Gram physical responses give monotone finite-cutoff archimedean coefficients with exact dual-gap/curvature remainder. Compact correlation closure and uniform signed-grid tails do not bound polar moment for diffuse finite trains. Physical-energy completion repairs the nonclosed `ℓ²` divisor-grid range without making cumulative traces square-summable; bounded active runs exactly characterize `ℓ²`-trace recovery for every completed state. Nyquist residual masses are nonnegative with summable tail ≤`1/√(N+1)`, but the exact cell-average producer diverges on smooth diffuse compact-correlation families. Physical divisor projection has exact within-cell plus component-charge error; it does not orient the full Weil form. E: B:SignedGramResponseOptimality; B:SignedGramResponseLadder; B:FiniteIntegerTrainProducers; B:DivisorEnergyCompletion; B:FullDivisorEnergyEscape; B:NyquistPolarSourceEscape; B:DivisorPhysicalProjection.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** On real even-polar sources, `weilArchRe=Ω(0)h(0)+Lévy energy`; all-scale Nyquist lower bounds use one origin budget with `wΛ=(Λ/log n)ϖ_n`. Exact full Weil identity is `arch−2·signed prime−polar pairing`, for arbitrary real compact sources. Signed packets bound annular Chebyshev transport error; discrete-cell, derivative-free correlation-collar, boundary-free Laplace, and integer-centered shear-cell producers give prime-free full-form lower bounds, none proved nonnegative. Factorial bi-null, coprime `(2,3)`, continuous factorial, and continuous coprime factorial profiles have zero origin and nonzero top value: none is one source autocorrelation. The causal factorial profile is a signed difference of two actual autocorrelations with equal source energies `Θ(√N)` and signed full pairing `O(log N/√N)`; cancellation does not pay either full Weil form. - **Established [L], auxiliary:** For `β>0`, Nyquist cell weight of Taylor order `m` has sharp asymptotic `β^(m+1)/(2(m+2)!)(k+1)^(β-m-2)`; its mass is summable iff `β<m+1`. This controls a positive weight tail, not the complete negative `Λ` pairing. E: B:NyquistWeightCellAsymptotic.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** At critical Nyquist order, renormalized cell discrepancy is absolutely summable with finite-part tail `O(1/N)`; total autocorrelation mass is `(∫f)²`, and smooth compact zero-mean sources can have signed negative separating-lag correlation. - **Established [L], auxiliary:** Wide-source residual realizes the subcritical tail norm with vanishing normalized archimedean energy; at `β≥m+1` it diverges despite that energy ceiling. - **Established [L], auxiliary:** Strict paired-collar coefficient exceeds the independent limiting coefficient by an explicit positive uniform gap, inherited by sufficiently large smooth-grid Lévy-energy lower bounds. - **Established [L], auxiliary:** Paired cutoff optimum approaches its limiting coefficient with leading deficit `4/√(n+2)+O(1/n)`; delayed product hierarchy has sharp deficit `4/(n+2)^(1/4)+O((n+2)^(−1/2))`, with actual grid-source Lévy lower bounds. - **Established [L], auxiliary:** Bohr coercivity, literal tail independence and normalized-tail Riesz bounds hold for the factorial family; no even blind full-Weil sign follows. E: A:FactorialPhaseBohrCoercivityAndTailIndependence.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Pole-free smoothed factorial combinations are zero-null in the zero-side pairing; compact-ramp and negative-tail combinations have equal zero-side mass/diagonal. - **Established [L], auxiliary:** Raw corrected-factorial tail span has exact complex Weil formula and high-scale coercivity `Re W≥(c log M−C)Σ|b_n|²`; smoothed multiscale tails factor through one pole scalar. <a id="pbm"></a>
### PBM — Prime boundary monodromy / regularized log-ζ abscissa

- **Path:** Fixed pole-regularized prime or log-ζ coefficients → Dirichlet series convergence at every real `σ>1/2` → RH.
- **Established [L]:** RH iff the pole-regularized prime series `Σ_{n≥2}(1_ℙ(n)−1/log n)n^(−σ)` or log-ζ series `Σ_{n≥2}((Λ(n)−1)/log n)n^(−σ)` converges at every real `σ>1/2` (proved via Abel summation from the `Λ(n)−1` partial-sum exponent law; despite the legacy route name, no monodromy is involved here — punctured monodromy lives in `DR`). Raw prime Dirichlet sum diverges for `Re s<1`; regularized prime and log-ζ diagonal shift operators retain a nonzero compact-quotient (Calkin) gap `1/2`. The gap-weighted prime Dirichlet series `Σ_k (p_{k+1}−p_k)p_k^(−s)` continues meromorphically to `Re s > θ` as `1/(s−1)+G(s)` for any admissible prime-gap second-moment exponent `θ` (unconditionally `θ=71/100` by Hoheisel). Regularization is not cancellation.
- **Open:** Source-derived critical-abscissa (`σ>1/2`) cancellation of the fixed regularized prime or log-ζ coefficients (or admissible prime-gap second-moment exponent `θ≤1/2` plus zero-freeness); compact-ideal comparison supplies no bound.
- **E:** R:RegularizedLogZetaAbscissa; R:PaperPrimeRegularizedIdeal; R:PrimeGapDirichletContinuation.

<a id="rb"></a>
### RB — Robin/Nicolas divisor-sum criterion

- **Path:** Extremal divisor sums/primorial Mertens product → eventual Robin/Nicolas inequality → RH.
- **Established [L]:** Eventual Robin and Nicolas inequalities are exact RH criteria; fixed k-free and positive exponent-defect classes satisfy partial margins.
- **Established [L]:** For `N=⌊log n⌋`, the moving valuation defect `D(n,N)=Σ_{p≤N}p^{−(v_p(n)+1)}` bounds `σ(n)/n` by `e^γ log log n·exp((1+ε)/log log n−D)` eventually. Every sufficiently large Robin counterexample has `D≤(1+ε)/log log n` and is divisible by every prime power, hence every integer, `m≤η log log n` for fixed `0<η<1`. This growing lcm core is necessary, not a contradiction or Robin proof. E: B:MertensValuationBudget; B:RobinMovingValuationDefect.
- **Established [L]:** Eventual Robin inequality on all integers is equivalent to eventual inequality on colossally abundant integers; hence RH can be tested on that extremal class. Reduction does not establish the needed inequality there. E: R:RobinColossallyAbundantReduction.
- **Open:** Source sign on increasingly prime-saturated extremal integers/primorials at square-root remainder scale.
- **E:** R:RobinCriterionRiemann; R:NicolasInequalityRiemannConverse; R:RobinInequalityKFree.

- **Established [L], short-window scope:** Plateau gauge gives `Ẑ(F)≥(3/40)∫F²` on `[-r,r]`, `r<e^(−2)/2`; pole conductance and archimedean Lévy density give `Ẑ(F)≥(1/30)∫F²` on `[-21/200,21/200]`. ### AIP — Arithmetic incidence positivity

- **Path:** Actual prime incidence + canonical plateau gauge → all-aperture `GlobalIncidenceAllocation` → complete odd zero-form positivity ↔ RH.
- **Established [L], exact source ledger:** Lag Abel/second-order summation identifies prime−two-pole imbalance with Chebyshev defect; band tests read variation modulo affine terms. R:ArithmeticIncidenceNarrowBumpBridge retains the full Weil ratio/product resonances, polar term and regular archimedean interaction. Neither identity supplies global incidence payment.
- **Established [L], comb obstruction/survivor:** Unit-mass smooth odd tests attain raw prime-comb/cross-prime energy `∼4e^(2r)`; no polynomial aperture majorant pays it. Normalized comb translates slowly varying tests and tends to exponential-resolvent energy J(F) on fixed compact tests; first-power charge can vanish despite maximal comb energy. Compact Λ-Lipschitz tests obey `|E_r(F)−4J(F)e^(2r)|≤K(Λ‖F‖₁+A_F(0))e^(2r−c√r)`; normalized depth tails approach `e^(−u/2)` at the same PNT rate. Retain signed prime–pole cancellation; these rates do not give a closing sign. - **Established [L], exact rescaled comb deficit:** `J(F)=∫₀∞A_F(u)e^(−u/2)/2 du`; for `F_r(x)=r^(−1/2)f(x/r)`, f supported in `[-1,1]`, Λ-Lipschitz gives `0≤A_f(0)−J(F_r)≤Λ²(8/r²+48/r³)` and eventual normalized comb deficit `O(r⁻²)`. For `C¹` f, both `r²(A_f(0)−J(F_r))` and `r²(A_f(0)−E_r(F_r)/S_r²)` tend to `4∫|f′|²`; comb/horizon error is `O(e^(−c√r))`. No `O(r⁻⁴)` remainder or complete Weil sign. ## Route candidates

<a id="acs"></a>
### ACS — Arithmetic Castelnuovo–Severi effectivity

- **Path:** Construct source-defined arithmetic cycles outside Frobenius/Weil-test span with Riemann–Roch effectivity and ample-orthogonality → Hodge index on scaling surface → RH.
- **Established [L]:** Scaling-surface pairing equals pole plane minus actual Weil form; Hodge index, one-positive-square and abstract effectivity axioms are RH-equivalent. Curve Frobenius-span positivity is already RH for the curve datum; fake `𝔽₅` datum and finite-window hiding show point counts, functional equation and bounded spans cannot supply index. Any positive-pole-quadrant × test cone P fails Riemann–Roch when P is positively scaling-closed and omits an admissible real test; effectivity must couple pole and test coordinates, not impose independent test positivity. No arithmetic effectivity constructed. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCastelnuovoSeveri/TheScalingSurface.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCastelnuovoSeveri/FrobeniusSpanHodgeIndexIsRH.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCastelnuovoSeveri/HodgeIndexFromEffectivity.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCastelnuovoSeveri/FakeCurvesAndTheWindowLaw.lean.
- **Established [L], index/effectivity boundary:** Tempered configurations, including actual ζ, have supremal negative index equal to distinct right-half-plane zero count, finite or infinite (HF). Given Hodge index, Riemann–Roch + ample + nonnegative degree holds exactly for cones E containing every positive-square positive-degree class and contained in positive-degree classes ∪ numerical radical. This classifies cones after index is supplied, not arithmetic effectivity independently. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCastelnuovoSeveri/TemperedConfigurationPontryaginLaw.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCastelnuovoSeveri/TheHodgeDefectCountsTheRightZeros.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCastelnuovoSeveri/HodgeIndexFromEffectivity.lean.
- **Established [L], degree boundary:** Aperture-filtered defect exhausts the right-zero count; actual ζ defect vanishes through `3/10` (HF). Parity indices count upper-half right zeros (HF). Finite-degree positivity supplies no arithmetic effectivity or uniform zero-defect theorem. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCastelnuovoSeveri/TheDegreeFiltrationOfTheHodgeDefect.lean.
- **Established [L], integrality boundary:** Ghost counts uniquely reconstruct normalized zeta series via `XZ′=ZΣN_jX^j`. Integral Witt series/counts plus functional equation do not imply purity: `q=5,P=1−5X+5X²` is impure; pure `q=2,β=(1,1)` has nonintegral `N₁=3−2√2`. Integrality and spectral purity are independent; no effectivity producer. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCastelnuovoSeveri/WittIntegralityIsNotPurity.lean.
- **Open / promotion gate:** Produce an arithmetic section/effectivity theory with Riemann–Roch and ample axioms independently of Weil positivity; couple pole/test coordinates beyond proper product cones and certify source cycles beyond the formal test-plus-pole construction. Current Hodge index is an RH-equivalent receiver, not a source theorem.

- **Path:** Prime source → divisor exhaustion → Fourier-trace vanishing → RH.
- **Established [L]:** Fredholm index and actual cycle-trace Fourier reconstruction.
- **Open:** Zero-independent exhaustion/vanishing of all trace coefficients; the reconstructed coefficients encode the off-line zero terms.

- **Path:** Literal prime−Gamma section → mate identity → orientation.

- **Open:** Construct a pre-divisor arithmetic section and mate identity coupling the fibre parameter `s` to `Ξ(s)=0` without assuming the radial character is zero.

Logistic relative entropy bounds the native proof-exchange defect and satisfies an exact identity with Gamma-six curvature; along quadratically deep diffusive grades, individual defects vanish while total exchange work tends to `1/8`.
- **Open:** One source-defined pre-divisor potential descending on all closed error fibres and coupling `s` to `Ξ(s)=0` without assuming RH.
- **E:** R:ConstitutiveDaggerConfluence; R:PaperCompletionInformationCost.

<a id="dr"></a>
### DR — Dyadic Xi roots

- **Path:** Global compatible dyadic roots on strict-right domain → zero-free Ξ → RH.

<a id="egc"></a>
### EGC — Pole-safe contour

- **Path:** Shifted contour source → enclosed-pole cancellation plus outer source covariance/sign → RH.
- **Established [L]:** Full copy correction converges on fixed shifts; Fourier reconstruction and source-ray decay retain all copies. Admissible cofinal rectangles enclose both prescribed poles and any finite zero packet; after mass subtraction the inner source contour equals the exterior-copy tail, uniformly small on centered shifts. This inner cancellation is unconditional and retains all zeros.
- **Established [L]:** On the original source ray the pole/Gamma-subtracted arithmetic remainder retains every Λ prime-power coefficient, is uniformly exponentially small across frequencies, and after multiplication by `2^σ` tends to its prime-2 coefficient (negative real part on centered rays with `0<ε≤1`). The *complete* correction has nonzero Gamma floor: `(m+1)·correction→−ε²/2`; faster-than-reciprocal decay of the raw source extractor is impossible. Neither arithmetic-ray sign nor Gamma floor orients enclosed Xi copies. E: R:PaperEGCArithmeticRay; R:PaperEGCSourceRayBaseline; B:LogGridUniformDirichletTail; B:HorizontalGammaDifference.
- **Open:** Outer expanding-contour covariance/sign excluding off-line copies; inner mass cancellation and centered Euler-safe prime-2 sign are nonselective. Raw source-ray decay cannot beat its Gamma floor.
- **E:** R:PaperEGCCompleteCorrection; R:PaperEGCFourierReconstruction; R:PaperEGCEnclosedPoleCancellation.

All-divisor vanishing is equivalent to HCF's two literal source-grid signs. The original Hurwitz divisor vector is FE-odd and orthogonal to all FE-even vectors; an involutive orientation isometry transports its complete negative Weil energy to an FE-even vector. Signed Gaussian cutoffs converge to that isometry in operator norm, and eventual nonnegativity of their zero-indexed even witnesses is RH-equivalent. FE-even restriction does not remove the obstruction.

Contact and algebraic independence preserve divisor data but supply no pre-divisor sign. E: B:HurwitzAnchorContactAndPrimeLogRajchmanClocks.

## Supporting research frameworks

<a id="bvi"></a>
### BVI — Bézout–valuation incidence

- **Role:** Arithmetic incidence, successor, pronic-energy and tangent estimates supporting [PM](#pm), [MC](#mc) and [CTF](#ctf); receiver bridges are proved. No separate proof-route identity.
- **Established [L], successor calibration:** `∫_(log n)^(log(n+1))e^(−u)du=1/[n(n+1)]`, n≥1; first N cells have mass `1−1/(N+1)`. Successor n,n+1 has zero Hecke incidence for n≥2. Over finite fields, monic positive-degree f and f+1 are coprime with equal degree and zero degree-cell length. Coordinate identity, not a critical-energy estimate. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCastelnuovoSeveri/ThePronicWeightIsThePoleMassOfTheSuccessorCell.lean.
- **Established [L], norm/row boundary:** Centred damping entrance functional has finite pronic dual norm exactly for `η>1/2`; reciprocal size-biasing restores bounded dual norm at every `η>0`. Residue modes are dense in `c₀` but their backward inverse is unbounded; the first chain column is the critical receiver. Uniform actual centred-prime row bilinear range extends to `P^{1+δ}≤Q≤P^{30/17−δ}` via the corpus's `17/30` short-window estimate; balanced `Q≈P` and full signed cofinal energy remain open. E: V:PronicDualNormOfTheEntranceLaw; V:ResidueModeCompleteness; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SignedArithmeticEnergyIncidence/ResidueModesAndBackwardContinuation.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCompensationTheory/TheSeventeenThirtiethsWindowWidensTheCentredPrimeRowRange.lean.

| Receiving route | Established transfer | Open arithmetic estimate |
|---|---|---|
| PM | Interior pronic/cofinal Abel budgets or an integrable Chebyshev-tangent bound imply RH. | Horizon-uniform complete-energy control along `ε↓0`, or a tangent norm bound integrable near zero; retain logarithmic drift, conditional means, shared-start covariance, signed complement and endpoints. |
| MC | First-octave increment saving is the Mertens half-power receiver; exact renormalized-defect identities. | Global signed cancellation, including positive reserve, high-product remainder and cutoff drift. Defect sign alone is insufficient. |
| CTF | Báez–Duarte residual = readout defect² + centered pronic energy; literal `−μ_N` readout tends to `1`. | Centered energy tending to zero; for PM cofactor rows, joint cutoff/damping variance decay. |

#### Arithmetic tools, bounds and limitations

- **Established [L], pronic structure:** Prime-index Vasyunin kernel is positive semidefinite; cotangent reciprocity extends to all positive moduli. At all moduli the Ramanujan primitive Gram is the double Möbius transform of Vasyunin residue second moments. Exact reciprocity defect is nonnegative residue-difference energy, `≥m₁(m₂−m₁)/m₂` for `m₁<m₂`; separated-scale negligible-defect shortcut fails. Finite identities do not bound complete signed cofinal pronic/PM energy. E: V:PronicPositivityOfTheVasyuninPrimeKernel; V:PronicReciprocityLawForVasyuninSums; V:VasyuninFormulaForTheDiscreteBaezDuarteGram; V:MobiusVasyuninFormulaForThePronicGramAtAllModuli; V:ExactVasyuninReciprocityAsResidueDifferenceEnergy.
- **Established [L], receiver bridge:** Finite-row Báez–Duarte residual is `(1−Λ-readout)²+energy-centred pronic Gram`; RH iff this tends to zero along some finite rows, equivalently unbounded readout²/energy ratio. For the fixed power-damped Möbius cofactor row it is `(1−energyMean)²+energyVariance`, with a universal `κ/log(Q+1)` floor; arbitrarily small values would imply RH. This is a BVI-to-CTF/PM receiver for an existing PM source, not an independent BVI closing estimate. E: V:PronicBaezDuarteCriterion; V:VonMangoldtReadoutFormOfTheBaezDuarteCriterion; V:BaezDuarteEnergyOfThePowerDampedMobiusRows; V:ReadoutEnergyUncertaintyPrinciple.
- **Established [L], literal-μ specialization:** The `Λ` readout is the row's log-harmonic sum; for `−μ` it tends to `1`, while its energy-centred pronic form has eventual floor `κ/(4log N)`. Readout alignment is known; vanishing complete centred energy is not. E: V:LogHarmonicReadoutAndTheMobiusRowEnergyFloor.
- **Established [L], power-damped readout:** Actual PM cofactor energy mean tends at fixed `ε>0` to `−ζ′(1+ε)/ζ(1+ε)²`, then to `1` as `ε↓0`. Residual closure requires centred variance to vanish along a joint cutoff/damping path. E: V:EnergyMeanOfThePowerDampedSourceTendsToOne.
- **Established [L], damping scope:** Fixed-`ε` receiver-row variance/reserve have cutoff-uniform Parseval ceilings tending below literal-μ positive cofinal floors; positive-lag covariance is radical-state-invisible and relatively vanishes as `ε↓0`. Fixed-band damped prefix energy has an exact linear limit; renormalized defect has rate `−(B+1)R(ε)<0`, with `R(ε)/ε→1/2`. Complete-period primitive energy is uniformly bounded/convergent for `ε>1/2`, diverges for `0<ε≤1/2`; literal μ has an eventual linear lower bound. Neither controls complete small-damping pronic energy. E: T:ReceiverRowParsevalSpectrumAndHorizonUniformVariance; T:DampedAutocorrelationAndClockInvisibility; T:DampedPrefixEnergyLinearityAndBandNonCommutation; T:DampedLinearDissipationExactRate; T:HalfDampingDissipationLaw; T:DampedPrimitiveEnergyHalfThreshold.
- **Established [L], structure:** The prime–prime mean-centered pronic Gram is an explicit Vasyunin cotangent-sum reciprocity pair; rational prime-ratio dilation profiles reduce to four such finite Gram entries. Neither formula bounds complete signed cofinal pronic energy. E: V:VasyuninCotangentSumsInThePrimePronicGram; V:ExplicitDilationProfileAtRationalScales.
- **Established [L], source:** Fixed-band Möbius horizon steps have finite smooth-core averages and recover the fixed-ratio energy profile. For every fixed `ε>0`, damped period variance converges to the complete damped divisor variance, yet its zero-damping limit differs from the undamped Möbius cofinal floor. Prime-factor pairing collapses each finite response to a top-window divisor sum and gives a conditional exact band-step law; no full-history signed estimate follows. E: T:MoebiusBandAverageStepAndEnergyProfile; T:DampedPeriodVarianceAndNonCommutingLimits; T:PrimePairingCollapseAndPrimeFactorStepLaw.
- **Established [L], finite bands/raw energy:** Literal-μ fixed-band profile reaches `P(3)=16/π²`, `P(4)=41/(2π²)`, with negative mean steps in bands 3 and 4; finite-band dissipation is not a cofinal bound. Raw complete sieve pronic energy tends to `1/2` for literal μ and commutes with the small-damping cofinal limit; this nonselective observable differs from centred prefix/BD energy. E: T:MoebiusEnergyProfileThroughFourthBand; T:PronicEnergyCommutesWithDamping.
- **Established [L], primitive/spectral boundary:** Complete-period zero-mean primitive energy for power damping `0<ε<1/2` has sharp growth `Θ_ε(N^(1−2ε))`; literal μ has a linear lower bound and `O(N log N)` upper bound. Literal μ loses every fixed low-modulus spectral band while retaining positive total variance; fixed `ε>0` has spectral tail `O_ε(E^(−2ε))`. These describe noncommuting regimes, not the pronic-weighted centred receiver. E: T:DampedDiscrepancyEnergyGrowthLaw; T:MoebiusSpectralEscapeAndDampedTightness.
- **Established [L], second-order fixed-damping laws:** Complete-period divisor variance `V(ε)=ε/2−ε²+o(ε²)`, dissipation `R(ε)=ε/2+o(ε²)` and lag-one covariance `−ε²+o(ε²)`; `R−V∼ε²`. A singular-series/lag-one Bézout identity gives the same variance and dissipation expansions. These are fixed-damping/cofinal rates, not a cutoff-uniform centred-pronic bound. E: T:DampedVarianceAndDissipationSecondOrderLaw; V:SecondOrderSmallDampingLawsFromTheSingularSeries.
- **Established [L], singular-series lag law:** For each fixed lag `h≥1`, damped autocorrelation divided by `ε²` tends to the Hardy–Littlewood pair singular series minus `1`; odd lags have limit `−1`. Joint cofactor cutoff/damping covariance has the same limit after density normalization; singular-series Euler product and exact damped Euler product hold. Lag-summed autocorrelation and Gallagher sum equal Ramanujan prefix discrepancies. No uniform signed complete pronic bound follows. E: V:HardyLittlewoodSingularSeriesOfTheDampedSource; V:ExactDampedSingularSeriesAndGallagherPrefixDiscrepancy; T:SelbergRowPeriodCorrelationsSingularSeries.
- **Established [L], tuple extension:** Normalized translated moments of radical-closed damped rows converge, at fixed finite prime support as damping vanishes, to truncated Hardy–Littlewood `k`-tuple singular series. For injective shifts the singular-series product converges, vanishes exactly for inadmissible tuples and is positive for admissible tuples; pairs recover the existing Ramanujan series. These finite-prime/iterated-limit identities are not prime-tuple asymptotics or a cofinal PM energy bound. E: V:HardyLittlewoodTupleSeriesFromRadicalClosedRows.
- **Established [L], tangent receiver:** Damped centred Chebyshev tangent `E_η(n)=Σ_{k≤n}Λ_η(k)−n m(η)` is the damping derivative of the centred PM prefix. An assumed pronic norm bound by a function integrable as `η↓0`, uniformly in the cutoff, implies RH and dual residual decay; that source-specific bound is unproved. E: T:DampedMoebiusFirstMomentAndChebyshevTangentBridge; V:DampedChebyshevTangentFormOfTheReceiver.
- **Established [L], exact reciprocity order:** For all positive integers `p,h`, residue-difference energy/Vasyunin defect lies in `[h(1+log⁺(p/h))/18,12h(1+log⁺(p/h))]`; also `E(m₁,m₂)≥(m₂−m₁)/8` for `0<m₁<m₂`. For `h≤p`, the sharper symmetric cotangent-sum enclosure remains `hH_{⌊p/h⌋}+[−2h,4h]`. Nearby logarithmic and separated linear scales are classified; complete signed source cancellation remains open. E: V:ExactOrderOfTheVasyuninReciprocityDefect; V:LogarithmicReciprocityDefectForNearbyModuli.
- **Established [L], defect/prime-step limits:** Literal-μ renormalized defect is strictly negative for all `M≥2N` once `N` is large; positive damping yields nonpositive defect beyond a quadratic horizon. Prime-cutoff steps switch across exact scale `Λ(ε)=log(1/(2ε))/ε+γ+o(1)` as `ε↓0`, with opposite step signs outside `Λ(ε)±ε` at large primes/horizons. These signs are nonselective and do not bound the complete centred Gram. E: T:QuadraticLongWindowThreshold; T:DampedCrossoverEulerConstantAndAdditiveWindow.
- **Established [L], finite observability:** Degree-5/6 relation spectra determine a finite row only up to its canonical half-period companion; all-degree spectra retain that ambiguity. No unique phase recovery or cofinal energy bound follows. E: V:RelationSpectrumDeterminesTheRowUpToTheCompanion.
- **Established [L], structure:** All zero-product root-order relations are classified by layer admissibility; translated-moment visibility has an exact degree law, including sixth-degree detection of a two-prime relative sign. Mean-centered PM pronic Gram has a cyclotomic-log formula. Beyond quadratic horizon `H+4≥4N²`, weighted centered-sawtooth pronic tail lies between `E/[2(H+1)]` and `3E/[2(H+1)]`, `E=sievePrimitiveEnergy`; this does not bound `E` uniformly. E: V:CompleteClassificationOfZeroProductRelations; V:ValuationLawForZeroProductRelationsAndVisibilityDegree; V:CyclotomicLogarithmFormulaForThePronicGram; V:PronicEnergyTailAtTheQuadraticHorizon.
- **Established [L], source:** For literal Möbius rows, `Q_Bez(N;μ)→0` and successor reserve minus Selberg diagonal tends to zero; each fixed nonzero period shift decorrelates. For fixed `ε>0`, actual PM cofactor row has nonpositive renormalized defect at every prefix for large cutoff. None bounds the positive reserve or complete small-damping pronic energy. E: T:MoebiusBezoutDecorrelation; T:PMCofactorRowGlobalDefectSign.
- **Established [L], structure:** Mean-centered Gram has a cotangent-sawtooth form. Fixed-lag normalized Möbius response correlations vanish, while period variance/reserve retain positive lower floors; horizon steps depend only on bounded smooth core. These do not give complete cofinal energy control. E: V:CotangentSawtoothFormOfThePronicGram; T:MoebiusResponseWhiteNoiseLaw; T:MoebiusBuchstabStepSmoothCoreLaw.
- **Established [L], finite-window sandwich:** For `N≥2`, every real coefficient row, phase `A` and length `K`, `|Σ_(A,A+K]sieveSeq²−KQ_Sel|≤N(N−1)Q_Sel`. Any universal sandwich constant is `≥Φ(N)−2`, `Φ(N)=Σ_{q≤N}φ(q)`; exact optimal constant not identified. Arbitrary-phase observability has order `Θ(N²)`, not a cofinal source-energy bound. E: V:WindowSandwichWithTheMontgomeryVaughanConstant; V:PhaseUniformWindowSandwichAndQuadraticObservabilityHorizon; V:QuadraticOrderWindowDirichletAndDissipation; V:FlatSelbergRowAndExactQuadraticSandwichOrder.
- **Established [L], signed defect:** For all sufficiently large horizons `N`, `sieveRenormalizedPrefixDefect N M μ≤0` for every prefix `M`; the same holds uniformly for every power damping `0≤ε≤1`. Prime-boundary jump dissipation pays the renormalized *defect*, not the positive successor reserve or complete pronic/PM energy. E: T:GlobalRenormalizedDefectSignAtLargeHorizons; T:RoughPrimeBoundaryUniformDampingDefectSign; T:DampedPrimeBoundaryDissipationAndCofinalDefectSign.
- **Established [L]:** Pronic divisor bijection and coprime successor-pair expansion; `Λ(k(k+1))=0` for `k≥2` and every centred pair product is prime-power-free. First-octave Möbius and logarithmic-sieve identities, second-octave 2-adic switching, exact successor Dirichlet-energy polarization and CRT incidence beyond length=height are source-derived structural laws.
- **Established [L]:** The coprime main form diagonalizes in Selberg multiples-coordinates with `μ(e)` weights. `Q_Sel−Q_Bez` cancels the `e=1` pole mode, has prime weight `p`, and separates `e=4` from `e=6`; on `ker P`, `2Q_Bez≤Q_Sel`. For coprime `d₁,d₂≥1`, Chinese-remainder incidence has exactly one hit per period `d₁d₂` and sawtooth defect of absolute value ≤`1`. Every finite successor prefix equals `M Q_Bez` plus its signed sawtooth remainder, bounded only by `(Σ|c(d)|)²`; additive Dirichlet energy has an exact pole-free main term minus both signed same-node and successor defects plus endpoint squares. `ker P` coercivity retains those defects.
- **Established [L]:** With harmonic mode `H` and variance `V=Q_Sel−H²`, successor covariance obeys `−V≤Q_Bez−H²≤V/2`, and energy density obeys `V≤2(Q_Sel−Q_Bez)≤4V`. `Q_Sel=Q_Bez` iff all weights on `[2,N]` vanish; `Q_Sel+Q_Bez=2H²` iff weights on `[3,N]` vanish. Near maximal anticorrelation bounds variance above parity by twice its defect. These finite-form rigidity laws do not control the signed prefix defects.
- **Established [L]:** For every sieve level `N≥6`, a triple `(H,Q_Sel,Q_Bez)` is realized by finite weights iff `2H²≤Q_Sel+Q_Bez` and `2Q_Bez≤Q_Sel+H²`; modes at `1,2,6` realize every feasible triple. A universal inequality `αV+β(Q_Bez−H²)≥0` holds iff `α−β≥0` and `2α+β≥0`. Thus the variance–successor-covariance cone is sharp; no stronger universal two-moment coercivity is available.
- **Established [L]:** For arbitrary weights, Fermi-cell response is twice the half-scale sieve prefix minus its full prefix. For Möbius weights and `N+1≤n≤2N`, response is `1+Σ_{k=N+1}^n μ(k)`, each jump is `μ(m)`, adjacent jump product is the Bézout successor correlation, and actual combinator cell energy is its square divided by `n(n+1)`. This lands BVI in a first-octave Nyman error, not in a cofinal RH bound.
- **Established [L]:** The `e=6` coordinate is the sole positive half-decorrelation endpoint: variance away from `6` is at most twice the half-decorrelation defect; deleting `6` leaves the harmonic mean unchanged and yields the sharp quarter bound with an explicit six-square correction. For each fixed prefix `M`, discrepancy² ≤ variance × dual energy, with an attaining weight; dual energy vanishes exactly at common divisibility return times. The first `N−1` observations detect every nonconstant weight at level `N`; the horizon is sharp, observation determinant is `(-1)^(N−1)/N`, and its variance-dual Gram is positive definite. E: V:SixModeStabilityAndSharpQuarterDecorrelation; V:ExactFinitePrefixDiscrepancyDualityAndReturnTimes; V:SharpInitialPrefixObservabilityAndExactGramDeterminants.
- **Established [L]:** Integral prefix matrix has Smith form `diag(1,…,n)` and cokernel `∏_{j=1}^n ℤ/j`; prime-power interpolation obstructions are exact. Literal Möbius combined LCM+Bézout defect through `M≤N` equals `M·successorReserve−1`; at `M=N` its terminal-prime mass exceeds every eventual sublinear-power ceiling along even cutoffs. Terminal-prime reserve versus observed response has an attained sharp quadratic calibration. The universal two-lag period-moment cone has five sharp facets, realized by finite sieve weights. E: V:IntegralPrefixLatticeAndPrimePowerObstructions; V:PrimeTailCalibrationAndMobiusDefect; B:TwoLagArithmeticCone; B:TwoLagSievePeriod; B:TwoLagMomentCone.
- **Established [L]:** Additive orbit averaging is the Selberg-orthogonal gcd projection; active raw divisors determine the exact least period by their lcm. Common-return discrepancy windows have a two-moment kernel: `2N−3` centered samples detect all nonconstant weights, while `2N−4` leave a unique final second-difference direction. The zero-mean successor primitive has exact Jordan-`J₂/12` spectrum; for `N≥6` its minimum at prescribed variance `V` and successor covariance `C` is `max(5V+2C,6V+4C,6V+12C)/12`, with sharp `{2,3}`, `{3,4}`, `{4,6}` edges. Prime modes rule out a uniform primitive-energy/variance upper bound. These are finite classification laws, not cofinal Möbius cancellation. E: V:GcdOrbitProjectionAndExactTranslationPeriods; V:ReturnWindowTwoMomentKernelsAndSharpZeroBlocks; V:DiscretePrimitiveEnergyAndJordanComparison; V:SharpPrimitiveEnergySuccessorFrontier.
- **Established [L]:** BVI sieve response equals the power-damped PM divisor coefficient on its cells; all-dilation and second-octave Möbius jumps, first-tail-cell `−μ(2M+1)`, exact pronic Abel energy and centered-sawtooth gradient/Dirichlet identities bridge BVI to existing PM/FZZ-P receivers. A cofinal uniform pronic Abel budget implies RH as an unproved premise; it is not a BVI-specific signed-defect estimate. Full affine finite-period observations recover only divisor-coordinate products up to component signs; even adding every odd translated moment fails to recover absolute origin response. For explicit even modes `6,10`, all data through order five remain blind but one sixth moment recovers their relative sign. For literal μ it is `−1` on `1≤M≤N`, with nonpositive first-octave increments. For power-damped weights and `M+1≤N` it is `−½Σ(Δg)²+½(g(M+1)²−1)≤0` when `ε≥0`; consecutive products have the `2`-adic ceiling `1−2⁻ε`. Jordan upper bounds are `16·primitiveEnergy≤N²·variance` and `h²·variance` after gcd-stride projection; none controls unprojected cofinal history. - **Established [L]:** On a full arithmetic period, optimal one-step divisor-sieve successor prediction is the coprimality operator: it retains exactly squarefree Selberg modes and loses `3/4` to `1` of variance above parity. Actual lag-`h` prediction has Ramanujan multiplier; mode `q` survives iff `q/rad(q)∣h`, giving exact finite recovery schedules and horizon. Active log drift exists for every `ε>0`; inactive log drift requires Fermi `L²` admission, known unconditionally only for `ε≥1/2`. - **Established [L], auxiliary:** Dyadic-antichain selected-trace inverse is horizon-uniform exactly for allowed odd-prime sets of finite reciprocal capacity; divergent capacity defeats it even for nonnegative packets. Literal Möbius tapers have zero highest-dyadic coordinate for `N≥4`, so this exponent theorem does not classify them. Terminal-prime Möbius coordinate is nonzero exactly when its taper is; Barban–Vehov prime anchor survives exactly below its taper cutoff. None bounds the complete cofinal source. E: T:FermiDyadicAntichainPrimeCapacityClassification; B:SieveLocalVisibilityClassification; B:SievePrimePowerLocalRegularity; B:SieveArithmeticSourceAnchors.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** All-horizon literal-μ unsigned sieve energy and gradient/defect bounds cost `invSum(M)^3`; first-octave short-interval mean squares transfer exactly to `μ`. Vinogradov–Korobov Möbius-window bounds yield a sum over quotient endpoints for arbitrary band count, not a uniform half-power or complete signed Abel estimate. E: V:VaughanTypeIIShortAndAllHorizonMobiusL2Energy; V:VinogradovKorobovCofinalMobiusDiscrepancy.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** A nonzero terminal-prime Selberg anchor determines finite mixed rows up to common sign; known negative Möbius taper phase fixes that sign. Finite multiples inversion gives explicit full-graph and signed-energy error bounds; unit perturbations make the local linear inverse exponent sharp. At fixed clock cutoff `k`, one constant bounds actual-μ synthesis by `B√N·H_N`, Barban–Vehov synthesis by `B√N·log X·H_N`, and inverse cost by `BN√N·H_N`; these are growing upper budgets, not cofinal tightness or signed cancellation. E: B:PrimeAnchorMixedRecovery; B:PrimeAnchorMixedSharpness; B:PrimeAnchorCompleteGraphError; B:PrimeAnchorHorizonBounds.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Finite prime-anchor decoder gives graph/energy error enclosures for arbitrary rows, retaining the exact zero-anchor defect. Actual Möbius and Barban–Vehov prime-horizon anchors admit explicit decoding/noise budgets; their fixed-clock inverse cost grows with horizon. This is observation stability, not cofinal signed cancellation. E: R:FinitePrimeAnchorDecoder; R:PrimeAnchorArithmeticDecoding.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Squarefree-modulus coprime logarithmic Möbius sums approach the totient ratio with a moving-modulus `σ₋₁/₂` error; differentiated Barban–Vehov long kernels and omitted-head Selberg forms retain all log moments and min-log boundaries. Zero-twist mollified diagonal is exact product-fibre energy. Paper-scale sieve remainders have Rankin–Euler/logarithmic upper bounds. These estimates do not close the complete PM/BVI signed cross terms. E: V:DifferentiatedBarbanVehovKernelsAndSmoothPrimeSigmaRate; T:MatomakiTeravainenPaperScaleSieveAndRankinEulerTail.
- **Established [L], scoped:** For `N≥9`, every squarefree-supported row with a nonzero Selberg anchor fails the local pairwise Hölder estimate at each exponent `α>1/3`; this includes prime-anchored Möbius tapers and Barban–Vehov rows. Retained even anchors still give cube-root recovery of odd coordinates from fifth/sixth moments under their stated magnitude/error premises. This is an observability ceiling, not failure of source-specific cancellation. E: B:PrimeAnchorVisibilityAndSquarefreeInstability.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** PM cofactor weights are squarefree-supported and Möbius-twist factorable. Their finite sieve responses depend only on the horizon radical state; divisor-multiplication and prime-power absorption hold at every horizon. Four-free Fermi selection fixes these weights, recovers paired values, and sandwiches paired pronic energy between selected energy and `4` times selected energy, with exact nonnegative slack. Observation equivalence does not bound cofinal pronic energy. E: V:SquarefreeResidueInversionAndCofactorRadicalSupport; T:CofactorSquarefreeFermiSamplingAndPronicObservability.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Linear-band Vaughan block power costs `(1+log B)²` under its stated scale hypotheses; Type-II short-band real/chiral Kloosterman collision bounds retain width-squared cost. Ramaré geometric-ladder/Halász–Montgomery–Huxley estimates bound final-range Möbius energy by bilinear plus harmonic rough cost under block hypotheses. Fourth-root prime-window and Gallagher linear-transfer bounds give zero-density upper bounds, not a half-power signed source estimate. E: V:LinearBandPowerEnvelopeAndChiralKloostermanCollision; B:RamareGeometricLadderAndSharpHalaszError; T:FourthRootPrimeWindowSieveAndGallagherLinearTransfer.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** Literal moving-floor centred PM prefix has a five-piece Vaughan expansion; complete interior pronic energy is its full 25-entry signed Gram. At `0<ε<1/2`, its source-derived logarithmic upper majorant is nonsummable; all off-diagonal terms remain in the mixed remainder. Interior and centred-source discrete energies differ by a uniformly bounded amount for each such `ε`, without bounding either energy. E: V:CompletePronicVaughanCalibration.
- **Established [L], auxiliary:** A log-free large-sieve bound has constant `N+2Q²−1`; squarefree tuple structure factors force variance for admissible tuples. All-boundary Fermi traces have explicit inverse-conditioning costs; damping-dependent prime-horizon slope reverses across its crossover. These finite observation/prime-window laws do not control the signed complete PM energy. E: V:LogFreeLargeSieveFromWindowSandwich; V:TupleStructureFactorsAtSquarefreeModuli; T:FermiAllBoundaryTraceConditioningBound; T:FermiAllBoundaryTraceFourfoldConditioningGain; T:DampedPrimeHorizonSlopeReversalAndCrossover.
- **E:** V:PronicBezoutSplitAndPrimePowerIndecomposability; V:QuotientDualOctaveReciprocityAndValuationSwitching; V:BezoutSuccessorQuadraticFormAndDirichletEnergySplit; V:BezoutSelbergDiagonalCoercivityAndHalfDecorrelation; V:ChineseRemainderBezoutPeriodicityAndSawtoothDefect; V:SuccessorEnergyRigidityAndParityStability; V:FermiCellJumpReciprocityAndPronicTailBridge; V:ExactSuccessorMomentRegionAndUniversalInequalities; V:SixModeStabilityAndSharpQuarterDecorrelation; V:ExactFinitePrefixDiscrepancyDualityAndReturnTimes; V:SharpInitialPrefixObservabilityAndExactGramDeterminants.

## Shared constraints

- **Prime-pair bridges [L], existing receivers:** Boxed actual-prime mass is `θ(x)²`; RH iff its error is `O(x^(3/2)log²x)`. Centered von Mangoldt pair-kernel energy is the existing square-window RH receiver. Every prime-projected lag slice is `O_δ(M^(1+δ))`, uniformly in lag; deleting both twin-prime orientations preserves the RH criterion. Fixed-sum Goldbach positivity and positive gap-two tails remain separate unsupplied assertions. No new route/candidate. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/goldbach/RiemannHypothesisBridge.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/twin-prime/RHPairKernelBridge.lean.
- **Prime-pair readouts [L]:** Goldbach pairing = even-reflection energy − odd-reflection energy. With odd damping overlap `O_N(ε)` and prime-log pair weight `G_N`, `D_N(ε)=16O_N(ε)−O_N(2ε)` obeys `D_N(ε)/(12ε²)≤G_N≤O_N(ε)/ε²` for `ε≠0`; both readers tend to G_N as `ε→0`. Minorant `m(ε)=D_N(ε)/(12ε²)` increases under damping halving: `m(ε)−m(2ε)=[O_N(4ε)−20O_N(2ε)+64O_N(ε)]/(48ε²)≥0`. Positive D_N at some positive damping iff a prime pair sums to N. No all-even-N positivity bound. Joint damping `ε(X,h)=log⁻⁴(X+h+2)` makes projected pair averages converge to prime-log pair averages with error `≤1/log(X+h+2)`. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/goldbach/RiemannHypothesisBridge.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/twin-prime/DampedPairDiagonal.lean.
- **Prime-pair compensation [L]:** For `ε>0`, `aε(d)=μ(d)(1−d^(−ε))/ε`, `|aε(d)|≤log d`; response at `n≥2` is `−Σ_(d|n)aε(d)`. Equivalently response = `Σ_(dm=n) μ(d)d^(−ε)(1−m^(−ε))/ε`: unit-cofactor terms vanish; each remaining term has absolute value ≤log m for ε>0. Exact pair expansion uses only nonunit cofactors on both sides, with no inverse-damping coefficient loss. For cofactor cutoffs Z,W≥2, ZW≤X, every selected subset of the gap-two n-range has normalized damped/log-cofactor pairing error `≤6εL⁵`, `L=1+log(X+2)`. Clock `ε=L⁻⁶` gives error≤6/L and actual projected prime-pair error≤8/L³. Damping removal is closed on these rectangles; signed log-cofactor dispersion and full-height complementary ranges remain open. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/twin-prime/DampedPairDiagonal.lean.
- **Finite arithmetic boundaries:** [L] On `⌊X/2⌋+2≤d,e≤X`, gap-two CRT incidence equals `1_(e=d+2)`; odd block at `X=8T+2`, T≥12, has period-mean defect ≥T/2 and normalized defect ≥1/20. Full-height coefficient-blind averaging fails; exact shifted correlation survives. [L] Nonsingular non-affine integer projective maps have at most `2τ(|det|)` integral returns; inversion attains the cap. Neither gives a parity sign or new RH route. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/twin-prime/FullHeightCRTBoundary.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/goldbach/ProjectiveIntegralReturns.lean.
- **Balanced product profile [S]:** Complete residue-product counts `c_q(r)` have Fourier coefficients `q·gcd(q,h)` and centered energy `V_q=q(Σ_h gcd(q,h)²−q²)`, positive iff q>1. Balanced side-qk smoothing retains exact energy `k⁴V_q/L + within-fibre variance`; signed pairing splits into periodic product bias plus centered covariance. Width `L=4qk²+h+1` avoids quotient wrap. This obstructs flat-profile replacement, not source-specific prime/μ cancellation. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/goldbach/ProductResidueProfile.lean (`balancedProductSmoothing_energy`, `balancedProductSmoothing_pairing`, `balancedProductSmoothing_noWrap`).
- **Zero-width dictionary [L], not a route:** `Δ=sup|Reρ−1/2|=Θ−1/2`. For `0≤σ<1/2`, `Δ≤σ` iff every rate `β>σ` bounds mean-square/spectral/Laplace energy or either one-sided deficit primitive at exponent `1/2+β`; completed Suzuki formation energy has subpower exponent `1+2σ`, and Möbius–divisor Abel energy has infimal exponent `1+2Δ`. `Λ_Newman≤Δ²/2`; vanishing BSY integral, zero Newman constant, prime-blind positivity on prime powers, square-root deficit bound and large-aperture ground-energy decay have the same zero-width endpoint. Ground floor `−Ce^(2σA)` implies width `≤σ` and lag floor `−C′e^(σv)`; the graded converse is conditional on uniform unit-window off-line squared-depth mass. HF ceiling excess only satisfies `Δ≤θ*≤Δ/2+1/4`. Receiver dictionary, not arithmetic bounds. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/TheWallsMeasureOneZeroWidth.lean.
- **Signed transport width [L]:** For `1/2≤φ≤1/2+σ`, `Δ≤σ ⇔ ∀ε>0 ∃C≥0 ∀N≥2, mobiusDivisorDilationCovariancePrefix(φ,N)≤CN^(1+2σ+ε)`. Exact MC/SRT receiver, not a covariance bound. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/LagGridsAndTransportLawsReadTheZeroWidth.lean.
- **Landau exponents [L], not a route:** Continuous-lag negativity rates are `[Δ,∞)` for a good fixed width; integrated-deficit and mean-square-band rates have infimum `Δ`. PM signed-damping accumulation threshold is exactly `Δ`. Polar ground rate satisfies `0≤Δ≤r₀≤1/2`, `r₀=0⇔RH`; `r₀=Δ` under uniform off-line copy count or squared-depth mass on unit height windows. Unconditional reverse ground-rate inequality and source forcing of zero rate remain open. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/TheZeroWidthIsALandauExponent.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/TheReceiverDampingReadsTheZeroWidth.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/TheWidthIsTheGroundEnergyRateWhenOffLineZerosDoNotCluster.lean.
- **Power amplification [L]:** For integer `k≥1`, admissible excess exponential rates (above `k/2`) of the `k`th power of the absolute integrated Chebyshev deficit are `k` times the original rates; infimal rate `kΔ`. Scalar powers alone cannot lower the normalized width exponent. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/AmplificationMultipliesTheZeroWidthExponent.lean.
- **Route-width receivers [L], not producers:** `Δ≤σ`, `σ≥0`, iff PBM regularized prime/log-ζ convergence, WL `(Λ−1)` convergence/packet/spline bounds, FU/MC dyadic prime-window bound, HCF Gaussian Hankel-tail cone, SZ shifted zero-freeness, TH thermal-moment bound, HF/RW critical Mellin/inverse-tilted-source analyticity, PA Poisson-trace vertical type, or local BAG recurrence right of `1/2+σ`. For `σ<1/2`, also TT Wold energy/directed covariance at every `0<t<1/2−σ`. `Δ` equals the Xi projective reflection radius. Width bounds imply LA/XN/WD log-derivative sign right of that strip and LP cosine-shift Ξ for every shift `h>Δ`; these last implications are not stated as equivalences. No improved source bound on `Δ`. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/TheRoutesMeasureOneZeroWidth.lean.
- **Zero-indexed candidate dictionary [L], not promotion:** On `XiDivisorSupport`, HJ residual/orientation vanishing, SEC seam vanishing/time-one Fock boundedness, CDC calibrated dagger confluence, BSH unit mate gain, IRK completed-relation noninvertibility and W1C real Clifford energy are equivalent to `Reρ=1/2`; all-divisor versions are RH. Quantitative depth `δ=|Reρ−1/2|` satisfies seam norm `√2 log 2 sinh(δ log 2)` and absolute log mate gain `2δ log n`; reflection/Berry–Keating/Clifford/spectral defects have exact depth laws; depth supremum is `Δ`. These diagnose supplied divisor points; pre-divisor source forcing remains missing. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/TheZeroIndexedRoutesMeasureOneSide.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/TheSideIsTheDepthAndTheWidthIsItsSupremum.lean.
- **Aperture dictionary [L], not a route:** Write `W_A` for complete real compact `C²` positivity on `[-A,A]`. `μ(A)≥0 ⇔ ∀0<A′<A, W_A′`; `W_A⇒μ(A)≥0`. `W_A⇔even W_A ∧ odd W_A`; odd `W_A⇔sinhPairing²≤2markovForm(⌈e^(2A)⌉)` on every odd window test. Odd `W_A` implies nonnegative ACT shifted-ground Markov energy for even profiles supported there. `μ(A)≥0⇒evenBlindMinimum(A)≥0`; no finite-aperture converse asserted. Real/complex window signs agree; `μ(v/2+w)≥0` pays the admitted SRT lag. All-aperture `W_A` and all-aperture `μ≥0` are RH-equivalent. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/TheRoutesMeasureOneAperture.lean.
- **Finite-window arithmetic selectivity [L]:** Full Weil functional on kernel support `[-B,B]` reads only weights at `log n≤B`. For `B<log 2` it is weight-independent; every prime weight inherits the full complex `C²` aperture-`3/10` margin `‖f‖₂²/5000`. For `B<log 3`, `W_w(k)−W_w′(k)=(w′(2)−w(2))(k(log 2)+k(−log 2))/√2`. Every weight with `w(2)=log 2` inherits the real odd aperture-`181/500` margin `‖F‖₂²/1000`. Liouville twist adds `√2 log 2·Re(k(log 2)+k(−log 2))`, whose sign is not fixed. Applies to RW/EWU/OWU/SRT/ACT/AIP; finite-window signs do not control the full prime source. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/WindowCertificatesAndThePrimeWeight.lean.
- **Finite-window mimicry [L]:** Every finite `R` admits `λ≥Λ`, `λ≠Λ`, not full-Weil-positive, with exactly ζ's functional on all compact `C²` tests supported in `[-R,R]`; a single weight perturbation beyond `e^(2R)` suffices. In synthetic lattice models, position and off-line depth are both invisible exactly through Nyquist aperture `L/2`, and detectable above it. Neither result constructs a counterexample to ζ positivity. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/CertificateRadiusReadsAFinitePrimeWindow.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/PositionAndDepthShareOneNyquistAperture.lean.
- **Reading limits [L], abstract/tempered models:** Any positive bounded-aperture certificate has compatible width interval [0,1/2], even with arbitrary fixed coercive margin, bounded aperture families, families of configurations or monotone change of depth unit. For convex aperture-growth profiles, cofinal thinning preserves all secant-rate bounds, while a bounded range admits arbitrarily steep invisible ramps and certifies no global rate. Full all-test positivity forces width zero. No actual-ζ finite-data impossibility inferred. E: Q:TheApertureCertificateIsBlindInEveryUnit; Q:TheResolutionLimitOfAReading; Q:TheRateIsACofinalInvariant; Q:TheBoundedApertureRateCertifiesNothing.
- **Node selectivity [L]:** Under RH, zero-pole-positive node sets are exactly supersets of prime powers. Missing one prime power refutes positivity unconditionally. Finite-modification-invariant readings retain node-defect resolution 1/2 and cannot decide positivity under RH. Node incidence does not supply the actual Weil sign. E: Q:RefutationIsFiniteConfirmationIsNot; B:PrimePowerZeroPoleNodeCriticality.
- **Dilation invariant [L], lattice models:** Dilation-equivalent scale/depth pairs have exactly the same depth. With unknown scale, one fixed-aperture nonnegative-level certificate still permits depth 1/2. Two band magnitudes determine depth despite unknown scale; this model reconstruction does not supply ζ source bounds. E: Q:TheDepthIsTheCompleteDilationInvariant; Q:TheMagnitudePairIsFaithfulWhereTheSignIsBlind.
- **Depth resolution [L], fixed lattice scale L:** At a≤L/2, every nonnegative deficit level hides all depths. For L/2<a≤L, level η iff `2L(cosh(δL)−1)≤η`; depth resolution `min(1/2,arcosh(1+η/(2L))/L)≤√(η/L³)`. Scale-matched budget η(L)≤β²L³ certifies depth ≤β. Deficit exponential-rate infimum equals δ; neither reading is a ζ floor producer. E: Q:TheSquareRootPrecisionLaw; Q:TheDepthIsTheCompleteDilationInvariant.
- **Band reconstruction [L], lattice models only:** `t₂=2L(cosh(δL)−1)`, `t₃=4L sinh²(δL)` determine `δ=arcosh(t₃/(2t₂)−1)(t₃−4t₂)/t₂²`; ratio `t₃/(2t₂)−1≤1+η` gives δ≤√(2η)/L. Unknown common background cancels in three bands: `(b₄−b₃)/(b₃−b₂)=2cosh(δℓ)`, determining δ. Isolated-pair moment `Pδ(a)=sinh(2δa)/(2δ)` has doubling ratio `Pδ(2a)/Pδ(a)=2cosh(2δa)`, also determining δ. No arbitrary-spectrum/actual-ζ reconstruction. E: Q:TwoAliasingBandsDetermineTheDepth; Q:ThreeBandsDefeatTheMass; Q:TheMagnitudePairIsFaithfulWhereTheSignIsBlind.
- **Visibility survivors [L], models:** Diluted off-line lattice is free below Nyquist ℓ/2; above it, positivity requires `ℓe^(δ(2a−ℓ))≤2L+2a+ℓ`, hence `δ≤log((2L+2a+ℓ)/ℓ)/(2a−ℓ)` when ℓ<2a≤L. Negative tests occur beyond `ℓ/2+log((3L+ℓ)/ℓ)/(2δ)` within a≤L/2. Finite off-line sets with top-depth gap have explicit refutation aperture. Constant all-aperture mass deficits distinguish on/off-line lattice models. No actual-ζ source sign follows. E: Q:TheHidingLaws; Q:RefutationIsFiniteConfirmationIsNot; Q:TheSquareRootPrecisionLaw.
- **Prime-side reading [L]:** Aperture-R Weil values depend only on λ(n), n≥2, log n<2R. Every finite R admits a non-full-positive weight agreeing with Λ's reading; aperture ≤3/10 is positive for every weight. Full-positive cone forces λ=Λ indexwise; Λ's full sign remains RH. Archimedean Fourier subsidy C(1+|r|)^θ, θ>1/4, admits λ≡1 and destroys weight selection. E: S:CertificateRadiusReadsAFinitePrimeWindow; R:WeilConeUniquenessUnconditional; R:TrivialWeightBoostedWeilCone.
- **Refutation versus confirmation [L]:** A negative compact Weil test refutes positivity at finite aperture; RH iff no finite-radius Weil reading refutes Λ's full positivity. Bounded positive-aperture certificates do not resolve width zero in tempered models. Unbounded positive aperture/radius sets suffice for RH; source existence remains open. E: Q:RefutationIsFiniteConfirmationIsNot; Q:TheApertureCertificateIsBlindInEveryUnit; Q:SparseAperturesSufficeAndTheIncidenceFaceDoesNot.
- **Geometric positivity dictionary [L], not a route:** For continuous compact radial `k`, `Ak(v)=∫k(η²/4+sinh²(v/2))dη`; critical-line Selberg transform is its real cosine transform. Exact horocyclic Fourier decomposition and Kontorovich–Lebedev fibre identity give weight `π⁻²τsinh(πτ)|Whittaker pairing|²`; integrability/Fubini and compact-test approximation are closed. Hyperbolic point-pair positive definiteness iff critical-line Selberg transform nonnegative iff `Ak` is Bochner-positive. Open: source-faithful arithmetic Ξ/Weil identification and sign for that source; the abstract dictionary supplies no new RH route. E: R:HyperbolicAbelTransformHorocyclicSlice; R:BochnerConeFejerTrapezoidWindows; R:HorocyclicSlicePointPairParseval; R:HorocyclicWhittakerEigenfunctionFibreRelation; R:HyperbolicPointPairFormHorocyclicCoordinates; R:HorocyclicFibreKontorovichLebedevPositivity; R:HyperbolicPointPairFormHorocyclicFibreDecomposition; R:HyperbolicPointPairPositiveDefiniteNecessity; R:HyperbolicPointPairMehlerFockDictionary.

- SRT scope [L]: For **every** finite aperture `A>0` and `0<δ≤1/2`, a tempered model of width exactly `δ` has nonnegative complete Weil form on all tests supported in `[-A,A]`, identical there to a weighted on-line shadow. Width zero iff positivity on all compact tests. Actual ζ is positive on sufficiently small apertures and eventually at high height for each fixed profile; neither fact supplies the all-parameter sign. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/TheZeroWidthIsAnUnboundedApertureProperty.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/ZetaSmallApertureHeightWindowPositivity.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/ZetaSingleHeightPositivityAboveArchimedeanHorizon.lean.
- SRT [L]: RH iff, for every compact `C²` profile, aperture and height, the finite prime polynomial is at most the pole-plus-archimedean part; equivalently the existing `2×2` pole-compression inequality. This is full Weil positivity in localized coordinates, not an independent source sign. An odd profile has negative archimedean–pole part at height `0`; comparing prime polynomial at another height to that frozen part fails. Under RH, its prime polynomial at `0` must be negative. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/PrimeTailCouplingCriterion.lean.
- SRT [L]: Windowed Fourier Gram is diagonal for Dirichlet length `X` when `2rX≤1`. Beyond threshold, the off-diagonal is a near-frequency pair sum; centred-height averaging inserts phase `e^{2πiT(ν_j−ν_k)}` without bounding it. For two windows, `m=min(r,ρ)≤log 2`, prime length `N≤X`, pairs satisfy `|ν_j−ν_k|≤m` and off-diagonal norm is at most `‖η‖₂²‖ω‖₂² X(e^{2m}−e^(−2m))·primeVariance(f,N)`. Pair-correlation exchange is a conditional parameter identity, not a proved correlation bound. Actual-ζ diagonal variance, bad-height and conditional depth-gap laws do not exclude off-line zeros. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/WindowedMeanValueDiagonalLaw.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/ZetaLocalizedFormWindowedVarianceLaw.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/DepthGapVisibilityLaw.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/WindowedOffDiagonalBeyondThreshold.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/DoubleWindowMinApertureAndPairCorrelationExchange.lean.
- SRT [L], model: for `L>0`, `0<δ≤1/2`, `4a≤L`, `2δa sinh(2δa)≤1`, contiguous off-line runs of any length/position remain Weil-nonnegative at aperture `a`; arbitrary clusters are likewise invisible when `a·TV(depth)·sinh(2δa)≤1`. For `cosh(δa)>1+8π²`, one compact test makes sufficiently long runs negative in every sufficiently sparse background, after the far-edge Fourier remainder decays. Periodic depth profiles have exact `⌈2aP/L⌉×⌈2aP/L⌉` Toeplitz PSD criterion, mode/Nyquist ceilings and smooth-profile hiding. These are synthetic divisor laws; actual ζ needs source control of coupled prime−archimedean form. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/OffLineRunEdgeVisibilityLaw.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/ClusterTotalVariationBlindness.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/PeriodicDepthProfileToeplitzLaw.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/DepthProfileFourierModeLaw.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/DepthProfileNyquistCeiling.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/SmoothDepthProfileHidingLaw.lean.
- SRT [L], model: if `4r≤L` and `r·TV(depth)·sinh(2δr)≤1`, every height-windowed cluster form at resolution `r` is nonnegative for tests of arbitrary aperture; Toeplitz-positive/smooth profiles have analogous transfer. Cosine-modulated off-line depths also hide at `a≤L/4` when `e(L/P)δe^((L/P)δ)≤1/3`; conversely relative jitter `0<η<1/2` makes a depth-`δ` off-line lattice visible at Nyquist aperture `L/2` if `cosh(δL/2)sin(πη)>1`. These are synthetic spectra, not actual ζ exclusion. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/WindowedBlindnessForClustersAndProfiles.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/BandLimitedDepthProfileHidingLaw.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/CrystallineFragilityOfOffLineLattices.lean.
- SRT [L], actual prime polynomial: finite prime sum is real and has both signs whenever its variance is positive; sub-prime-gap windowed mean and second moment are exact. Under RH, one-sided prime–archimedean coupling yields a height with archimedean part above the variance/envelope threshold. No unconditional coupled sign follows. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/PrimePolynomialConjugateSymmetryAndQuantitativeOscillation.lean.
- SRT [L], actual prime phases: finite prime-only polynomial attains both ends of its ℓ¹ envelope along Kronecker heights; if that envelope exceeds the prime-power tail, full prime polynomial oscillates. Each sufficiently large prime excursion either gives negative localized complete Weil form or occurs at exponentially large height to be paid by the archimedean log growth; under RH only the latter remains. No unconditional sign or zero exclusion follows. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SquareRootThreshold/KroneckerBohrPrimePhaseRealizationAndExponentialHeightEscape.lean.
- SRT [L], model rigidity: a fixed-shape periodic off-line pattern hiding at apertures `a>(P−1)ℓ/2` for unbounded scales `ℓ` must have equal depths and crystalline ordinates; every noncrystal is detectable below Nyquist `Pℓ/2` at all large scales.
